Method: 采用HSE06泛函和有限尺寸校正方法，计算了硅中铜相关缺陷的构型、形成能和跃迁能级。研究的缺陷包括间隙铜(Cu_i)、替位铜(Cu_Si)、铜-硼(Cu-B)、铜-磷(Cu-P)和铜-氢(Cu-H)复合物。

Result: 计算获得了各种铜相关缺陷的详细构型和电子性质。提出了Cu_i4V模型来解释Cu_PL缺陷，该模型能够弥合理论与实验之间的差异。研究揭示了铜相关缺陷的电活性特性及其在硅中的早期沉淀行为。

Conclusion: 该研究通过第一性原理计算为硅中铜相关缺陷提供了系统的理论分析，提出的Cu_i4V模型成功解释了Cu_PL谱线的形成机制，为理解铜在硅中的电活性缺陷和早期沉淀过程提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Copper is a detrimental impurity in silicon with high diffusivity and a high tendency to precipitate. Interaction between Cu and other defects is essential for understanding the nature of Cu precipitation in silicon. Despite extensive experimental investigations of Cu-related defects in silicon, a comprehensive understanding remains elusive due to limitations of techniques in resolving defect configurations, as well as inconsistencies between theoretical and experimental results regarding transition levels. Moreover, the underlying formation mechanism of the well-known $\mathrm{Cu_{PL}}$ line is still unclear. In this work, configurations, formation energies, and transition levels of Cu-related defects in silicon are calculated using the HSE06 functional and finite-size correction. Defects involved in this study include $\mathrm{Cu_i}$, $\mathrm{Cu_{Si}}$, Cu-B, Cu-P, and Cu-H. A $\mathrm{Cu_{i4}V}$ model is proposed to explain the discrepancies between theory and experiment about $\mathrm{Cu_{PL}}$ defect. Our calculations may provide insight into the electrically active defects and the early states of Cu precipitation in silicon.

### [64] [Self-doped Crystal from Preempted Band-inversion Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09820)
*Jiechao Feng,Zhaoyu Han,Michael P. Zaletel,Zhihuan Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出自掺杂维格纳晶体（SDC）源于预阻断的带反转相变，建立了其存在的能带理论判据，并通过自洽哈特里-福克计算在λ-凝胶模型和菱面体五层石墨烯中验证了SDC相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在菱面体石墨烯中观察到"自掺杂"维格纳晶体（SDC），即略微非公度的维格纳晶体与小型费米海共存。需要理论解释这种SDC的起源机制，并建立预测其出现的普适判据。

Method: 1. 提出非微扰论证：SDC源于预阻断的带反转相变；2. 建立简单的能带理论判据；3. 在λ-凝胶模型和菱面体五层石墨烯中进行自洽哈特里-福克计算验证；4. 分析母带贝里曲率分布对SDC形成的影响。

Result: 1. 在λ-凝胶模型中识别出位于"光环"维格纳晶体和反常霍尔晶体之间的SDC相；2. 在菱面体五层石墨烯中预测了位于维格纳晶体和"不合格"光环反常霍尔晶体之间的SDC相；3. 揭示了贝里曲率分布通过量子几何诱导奇异量子相的新机制。

Conclusion: 自掺杂维格纳晶体是预阻断带反转相变的普适产物，其出现可由能带理论判据预测。量子几何（特别是贝里曲率分布）在诱导这类奇异量子相中扮演关键角色，为探索新型量子物态提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent experiments in rhombohedral graphene find evidence for a "self-doped" Wigner crystal (SDC) in which a slightly incommensurate Wigner crystal (WC) coexists with a small Fermi sea. We provide non-perturbative arguments that such SDCs generically arise from preempted band-inversion transitions between commensurate crystals, which motivates simple band-theory criteria for their appearance. Self-consistent Hartree-Fock calculations establish the existence of a SDC consistent with this mechanism in both the $λ$-jellium model and rhombohedral pentalayer graphene (R5G). In the $λ$-jellium model, we identify a SDC phase located between a "halo"-WC and an anomalous Hall crystal (AHC), which would otherwise be connected via a Dirac transition when pinned to commensuration; this contrasts with the WC-AHC transition, which we show cannot be connected by a continuous transition due to a mismatch of symmetry indices. In R5G, we predict a SDC phase located between a WC and a "disqualified" halo anomalous Hall crystal. We discuss in general how the Berry curvature distribution in the parent band affects the appearance of SDC, revealing a novel role of quantum geometry in inducing exotic quantum phases of matter.

</details>


### [65] [A Scalable Configuration-Interaction Impurity Solver via Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10230)
*Jeongmoo Lee,Ara Go*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: AL-ATCI方法通过主动学习识别相关态的决定子流形，显著缓解了有限哈密顿量杂质求解器中希尔伯特空间随轨道数增长的问题，使大浴池和扩大轨道计算更加实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限哈密顿量杂质求解器虽然能提供直接实频谱并自然扩展到扩大杂质哈密顿量，但其应用受到希尔伯特空间随浴池或其他添加的单粒子轨道数量快速增长的限制。

Method: 引入自适应截断组态相互作用（ATCI）的主动学习扩展（AL-ATCI），通过查询大小N_query识别相关态的决定子流形，该参数既控制近似精度又作为内部收敛参数。

Result: 在一维Hubbard模型的动力学平均场理论基准测试中，AL-ATCI重现了精确对角化精度并将元胞计算扩展到N_c=10的团簇；对于三轨道旋转不变Sr2RuO4杂质问题，展示了动力学量的系统收敛和高度压缩的组态空间。

Conclusion: AL-ATCI方法显著缓解了精确对角化和基于组态相互作用的杂质求解器的浴池离散化瓶颈，使大浴池和扩大轨道计算更加实用，计算成本仅随浴池大小弱增长。

Abstract: Finite-Hamiltonian impurity solvers provide direct real-frequency spectra and a natural route to enlarged impurity Hamiltonians, but their applicability is limited by the rapid Hilbert-space growth with the number of bath or other added one-particle orbitals. We introduce an active-learning extension of adaptive-truncation configuration interaction (AL-ATCI) that identifies the determinant manifold relevant to the correlated state. The approximation is systematically controlled by the query size N_query, which also provides an internal convergence parameter when no external benchmark is available. Over the benchmark range studied here, the computational cost grows only weakly with bath size, because enlarging the bath mainly expands the combinatorial determinant space rather than the physically relevant manifold. In dynamical mean-field-theory benchmarks for the one-dimensional Hubbard model, AL-ATCI reproduces exact-diagonalization accuracy and extends cellular calculations to clusters as large as N_c = 10. For a three-orbital rotationally invariant Sr2RuO4 impurity problem, we demonstrate systematic convergence of dynamical quantities and a highly compressed configuration space as N_b is increased from 9 to 18. These results substantially alleviate the bath-discretization bottleneck of exact-diagonalization- and configuration-interaction-based impurity solvers and make large-bath and enlarged-orbital calculations more practical.

</details>


### [66] [Crystalline topological invariants in quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10338)
*Naren Manjunath,Maissam Barkeshli*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综述二维系统中晶格平移、旋转对称性与电荷守恒产生的拓扑不变量，包括整数和分数量子霍尔绝缘体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联系统中晶体对称性产生的拓扑不变量，这些不变量可以区分量子物相，是当前非微扰方法研究的重要方向

Method: 综述性方法，主要关注如何表征、分类和检测二维系统中由晶格平移对称性、旋转对称性以及电荷守恒产生的拓扑不变量

Result: 回顾了近期发展，表明即使是经典模型（如Harper-Hofstadter模型）也能产生丰富的晶体对称性保护拓扑不变量，特别关注整数和分数量子霍尔绝缘体

Conclusion: 晶体对称性在强关联系统中产生重要的拓扑不变量，对这些不变量的研究是理解量子物相分类的关键方向

Abstract: Crystalline symmetries give rise to topological invariants that can distinguish quantum phases of matter. Understanding these in strongly interacting systems is an ongoing research direction requiring non-perturbative methods. Recent developments have demonstrated that even classic models, like the Harper-Hofstadter model of free fermions on a lattice in a magnetic field, yield a host of crystalline symmetry protected topological invariants. Here we review some of these developments, focusing mainly on how to characterize, classify, and detect invariants arising from lattice translation and rotation symmetries along with charge conservation in two-dimensional systems, including integer and fractional Chern insulators.

</details>

Method: 通过拟合磁化数据获得交换哈密顿量，构建包含双二次耦合和单离子各向异性的磁模型。利用该模型计算磁谱并与实验数据对比验证。

Result: 获得了能够解释观测磁场转变的交换哈密顿量。发现双二次耦合和单离子各向异性对于解释磁化率数据中的多个相变都是必要的。计算得到的磁谱与低温比热数据吻合。

Conclusion: 成功建立了γ-Mn₃(PO₄)₂的磁模型，该模型包含双二次耦合和单离子各向异性，能够全面解释实验观测的磁化行为、相变和热力学性质。

Abstract: We present a magnetic model for an antiferromagnetic compound $γ$-Mn$_3$(PO$_4$)$_2$, which was previously shown to exhibit a 1/3 magnetization plateau due to the trimer-based structure of the lattice of magnetic Mn$^{2+}$ ions with $S=5/2$. An exchange Hamiltonian that yields observed field transitions is obtained from fitting magnetization data. It is shown that both biquadratic coupling and single-ion anisotropy are necessary to be present in the magnetic model to explain multiple phase transitions in the magnetic susceptibility data. The calculated magnetic spectrum is in agreement with the low-temperature specific heat data.

</details>


### [68] [Structural Reconstruction Induced d-wave Altermagnetism in $\mathrm{V_{2}X_2}$ ($X = \mathrm{S, Se}$) monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10768)
*Geethanjali S,Sasmita Mohakud*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过在三角晶系VX₂单层中引入硫族簇空位，构建了V₂X₂重构晶格，实现了二维d波反铁磁体，展示了动量依赖的自旋劈裂和d波型反铁磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁性具有无需相对论效应的动量依赖自旋劈裂特性，有望用于下一代自旋电子学应用。本研究旨在探索通过空位驱动重构实现二维d波反铁磁体的有效途径。

Method: 在三角晶系VX₂（X=S, Se）单层中引入硫族簇空位，构建重构的V₂X₂晶格。该结构形成钒原子的反Lieb晶格，包含两个通过C₄晶格旋转对称性和C₂磁对称性相关的磁性亚晶格。分析电子能带结构中的动量依赖自旋劈裂特性。

Result: 重构结构在保持零净磁化的情况下破坏了时间反演对称性和联合反演时间反演对称性。费米面上观察到强烈的各向异性自旋劈裂：沿Γ-X和Γ-Y方向显著，在M点附近消失，显示出对称性强制的节点特征。自旋劈裂遵循四重角调制，与d_{x²-y²}型反铁磁性一致，实空间自旋密度在钒位点上呈现相应的d波模式。

Conclusion: 空位驱动重构为实现二维d波反铁磁体提供了有效途径，为先进自旋电子技术开辟了新方向。该研究展示了通过结构重构调控反铁磁性的可行性。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, featuring momentum-dependent spin splitting without relativistic effects, holds promise for next generation spintronic applications. In this study, we investigate the momentum-dependent spin splitting in the electronic band structure of a reconstructed $V_{2}X_{2}$ ($X=\mathrm{S, Se}$) lattice, achieved by introducing chalcogen cluster vacancies in trigonal $VX_{2}$ ($X=\mathrm{S, Se}$) monolayer. The reconstructed structure forms an inverse Lieb lattice of vanadium atoms, comprising two magnetic sublattices related by $C_{4}$ lattice rotational symmetry and $C_{2}$ magnetic symmetry, resulting in zero net magnetization despite the breaking of time-reversal ($\mathcal{T}$) and combined inversion time-reversal ($\mathcal{PT}$) symmetries. The electronic structure exhibits strongly anisotropic spin splitting on the Fermi surface, pronounced along $Γ\!-\!X$ and $Γ\!-\!Y$ and vanishing near $M$ point, revealing symmetry-enforced nodal features. The spin splitting follows a fourfold angular modulation consistent with $d_{x^{2}-y^{2}}$-type altermagnetism, while the real-space spin density exhibits a corresponding $d$-wave pattern localized on vanadium sites. Our findings demonstrate that vacancy-driven reconstruction provides an effective route realizing two-dimensional d-wave altermagnets, opening a new avenue for advanced spintronic technologies.

</details>


### [69] [Thermalization Fronts in the Hubbard-Holstein Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10775)
*Antonio Picano,Marco Schiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究使用非平衡动力学平均场理论分析弱耦合Hubbard-Holstein模型中电子-声子相互作用突然开启后的弛豫动力学，揭示了电子主导与声子主导区域之间的交叉行为，并展示了热化过程在电子-声子系统中的相干传播特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱耦合Hubbard-Holstein模型在电子-声子相互作用突然开启后的非平衡动力学，扩展先前在Holstein模型中识别的电子主导与声子主导区域交叉行为到有限Hubbard相互作用情况，并探究热态微观形成的动力学过程。

Method: 采用非平衡动力学平均场理论，结合电子-声子耦合的自洽Migdal近似和电子-电子相互作用的二阶微扰理论。使用Step-by-Step DMFT框架分析热化微观过程，在实时间和DMFT迭代数的平面上观察热化前沿的传播。

Result: 弛豫动力学表现出电子主导与声子主导区域之间的交叉行为。热化过程以尖锐传播前沿为标志，在电子可观测量中弱淬灭下即可观察到前沿，而声子区域仅在足够强耦合下才出现可见前沿。弱耦合时局域无色散声子前沿形成延迟，接近和超过交叉点时电子和声子区域前沿在可比时间尺度上发展。当两个前沿都解析时，它们以相同速度传播，表明热化在耦合电子-声子系统中相干传播。

Conclusion: 弱耦合Hubbard-Holstein模型的热化过程通过尖锐传播前沿在电子-声子系统中相干扩展。电子和声子区域前沿形成的不同时间尺度揭示了耦合强度对热化动力学的影响，为理解复杂量子系统中非平衡热化机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium dynamics of the weak-coupling Hubbard-Holstein model after a sudden switch-on of the electron-phonon interaction within nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). Using the self-consistent Migdal approximation for the electron-phonon coupling together with second-order perturbation theory for the electron-electron interaction, we show that the relaxation dynamics exhibits a crossover between electron-dominated and phonon-dominated regimes, extending to finite Hubbard interaction the scenario previously identified in the Holstein model. To investigate the microscopic buildup of the thermal state, we analyze the dynamics within the Step-by-Step DMFT framework. In the plane of real time and DMFT iteration number, thermalization is marked by a sharp propagating front. This front appears in electronic observables already for weak quenches within the simulated time window, whereas the phononic sector exhibits a visible front only at sufficiently strong coupling. Thus, at weak coupling the local dispersionless phonons show a delayed onset of front formation, while near and beyond the crossover the front develops on comparable timescales in both the electronic and phononic sectors. Whenever both fronts are resolved, they propagate with the same velocity, showing that thermalization spreads coherently through the coupled electron-phonon system.

</details>

Abstract: In the fermionic liquids, the Fermi surface is topologically stable,\cite{Volovik2003} which is at the origin of the applicability of the Landau theory of Fermi liquid (LFL). The LFL exists under special condition, when the Green's function has a pole with nonzero residue $Z$. Otherwise one has non-Landau Fermi liquid (NLFL), such as Luttinger liquid, which is described by the same topological invariant. It appears that in general this topological invariant is the property of the fermionic particle, i.e. the particle charge (or the electric charge of electron) is equivalent to the topological charge of the fermion. The conservation of the fermionic charge is equivalent to the conservation of the topological charge. We consider the application of this topological charge to the Landau theory of Fermi liquids. We also consider the application to non-Fermi liquids and crystalline insulators in relation to the Luttinger theorem.

</details>

Abstract: We review recent advances in machine-learning (ML) force-field methods for large-scale Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) simulations of metallic spin systems. We generalize the Behler-Parrinello (BP) ML architecture -- originally developed for quantum molecular dynamics -- to construct scalable and transferable ML models capable of capturing the intricate dependence of electron-mediated exchange fields on the local magnetic environment characteristic of itinerant magnets. A central ingredient of this framework is the implementation of symmetry-aware magnetic descriptors based on group-theoretical bispectrum formalisms. Leveraging these ML force fields, LLG simulations faithfully reproduce hallmark non-collinear magnetic orders -- such as the $120^\circ$ and tetrahedral states -- on the triangular lattice, and successfully capture the complex spin textures emerging in the mixed-phase states of a square-lattice double-exchange model under thermal quench. We further discuss a generalized potential theory that extends the BP formalism to incorporate both conservative and nonconservative electronic torques, thereby enabling ML models to learn nonequilibrium exchange fields from computationally demanding microscopic approaches such as nonequilibrium Green's-function techniques. This extension yields quantitatively accurate predictions of voltage-driven domain-wall motion and establishes a foundation for quantum-accurate, multiscale modeling of nonequilibrium spin dynamics and spintronic functionalities.

Abstract: We present a theoretical investigation of resonant charge transfer in low-energy Li$^+$ ions backscattered from a MoS$_2$ surface, focusing on the influence of excited projectile states. The Hamiltonian parameters are computed using an expanded Huzinaga basis set that explicitly incorporates lithium's $2p$ orbitals. Each orbital channel is treated separately, and electronic correlation effects are introduced approximately through a probabilistic exclusion principle applied to the final charge state. Theoretical calculations demonstrate that including $2p$ channels enhances the agreement with previously reported experimental neutral fractions, where the single-channel descriptions show noticeable discrepancies. Among excited states, the $2p_z$ orbital oriented perpendicular to the surface contributes most significantly due to its spatial extension toward the substrate. Examination of temporal evolution reveals that independent channel occupations exceed unity at small projectile$-$surface separations, highlighting the necessity of dynamical correlation treatments. These findings establish that excited states make non-negligible contributions to charge exchange processes for alkali ions interacting with transition metal dichalcogenide surfaces and should be incorporated for accurate quantitative modeling.

Abstract: We report the growth and characterization of single crystals of the disorted-kagome lattice compound CrPdAs. Spin-glass behaviour with $T_{SG} \sim 60\ {\rm K}$ is observed in all crystals tested. Some growths show in addition a magnetic impurity phase with $T_c$ around 200 K, but annealing produces single-phase crystals without the ferromagnetic impurity phase. Single-phase crystals nevertheless have $29\pm 5\%$ anti-site disorder of the Cr and Pd sites, similar to a previous generation of flux-grown polycrystalline samples. We observe a large linear-coefficient of the heat capacity at low temperature, $γ= 23 \pm 3$ mJ/mole\,K$^2$, which is typical of kagome metals. The calculated band structure shows several Dirac band-crossings very near $E_F$ whose degeneracy is lifted when spin-orbit interaction is included. Our most curious finding is a `non-metallic' in-plane resistivity, extending over the entire measured temperature range from 300 K down to 2 K. This resistivity is quasi-linear below about 130 K, and shows no sign of saturation down to the lowest temperature measured.

Abstract: Layered two-dimensional electron systems exhibit both optical and acousticlike plasmons around the Brillouin-zone center. In the layered cuprate La$_{2-x}$Sr$_x$CuO$_4$, resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) has detected corresponding acousticlike plasmons in a low-energy regime comparable to that of other collective excitations associated with distinct regions of the cuprate phase diagram. This overlap in energy scale raises the question of whether the acousticlike plasmons are significantly influenced by phase-specific electronic phenomena, including the pseudogap, charge and spin order, superconductivity, and strange-metal behavior. Here we show that a single parameter set of the layered $t$-$J$-$V$ model, which incorporates strong correlations and the long-range Coulomb interaction $V$, consistently describes the acousticlike plasmon dispersion across all currently available RIXS data from the underdoped to the heavily overdoped regime. This transferability of a single parameter set exceeds that of earlier theoretical descriptions and supports a picture in which strong correlations persist into the heavily overdoped regime, while the collective plasmon mode exhibits only limited sensitivity to the phase-specific electronic phenomena that distinguish different regions of the phase diagram.

Abstract: We demonstrate that interacting electrons in AB-stacked $\mathrm{MoTe}_2/\mathrm{WSe}_2$ realize a topological Kondo insulator at hole filling $ν=2$ per moiré unit cell. In the presence of only local correlations, a symmetry of the moiré-scale bandstructure enforces a compensated topological semimetal by tying band inversion to band overlap. We show that non-local interactions change the physics qualitatively, since they allow intrinsic, quantum-geometry-induced spin loop currents to feed back on the effective bandstructure, which lift the remaining accidental degeneracies and open a full gap in the spectrum, leading to a fully gapped topological Kondo insulator. We establish this using real-frequency dynamical mean-field theory to capture Kondo physics alongside Hartree-Fock for non-local interactions. The topological Kondo insulator emerges at intermediate displacement fields, where strong correlations manifest through an enhanced spin susceptibility, a suppressed charge susceptibility, and a stronger thermal dependence of the resistivity. Our results are in good agreement with recent experiments on $\mathrm{MoTe}_2/\mathrm{WSe}_2$ bilayers demonstrating topological to trivial phase transitions controlled by the displacement field.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文有两个主要动机：1) 扩展Seidl猜想在多边际最优输运问题中的适用范围，超越之前仅限于平移不变、凸且递减相互作用的限制；2) 严格推导强相互作用系统中绝热连接势的渐近行为，特别是针对量子环上的电子系统。

Conclusion: 论文成功扩展了Seidl猜想的适用范围，为量子环上电子系统的多边际最优输运问题提供了理论支持，并建立了强相互作用极限下绝热连接势的严格渐近理论，为密度泛函理论在强关联系统中的应用提供了数学基础。

Abstract: Our goal in this paper is twofold. First, we characterize the class of pairwise interactions for which the Seidl conjecture on the structure of optimal plans for the symmetric multimarginal optimal transport problem with one-dimensional marginal holds. This extends previous results by Colombo, De Pascale, and Di Marino [Can. Jou. Math., 67 (2015), https://doi.org/10.4153/CJM-2014-011-x], which treated the case of translation-invariant, convex and decreasing interactions. In particular, our results apply to physically relevant interactions for electrons living on a quantum ring. The second main goal of the paper is to rigorously derive the leading order asymptotics of the adiabatic connection potential for strongly interacting systems. More precisely, we show that for electrons in a quantum ring (or one-dimensional interval), not only the Lieb density functional converges to the optimal transport (or strictly correlated) functional in the semiclassical limit, but also the representing potential converges to a regular Kantorovich potential. As an intermediate step, we also extend previous results on the strongly interacting limit of the Lieb functional to periodic systems in arbitrary dimensions.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a duality for Inönü-Wigner contractions attached to real symmetric Lie algebras. Abstract: We consider a two-dimensional Bose gas in the dilute regime where $ρa^2$ is small. For temperatures below the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless critical temperature, we derive an explicit upper bound for the free energy density using Bogoliubov theory. Our result captures the contribution of quasiparticle modes with dispersion relation $\sqrt{p^4 + 8πρ\, δ\, p^2}$ and where $δ= 2 / (|\log(ρa^2)| + \log |\log(ρa^2)|)$.

Result: 证明了高阶CS作用量在闭流形上具有高阶规范不变性，在带边界流形上所有规范依赖性都编码在边界项中。纯规范的高阶WZW项恒为零，高阶规范WZW项是恰当的。

Conclusion: 成功建立了严格的更高阶陈-西蒙斯规范理论框架，推导了高阶WZW和规范WZW项，并完全刻画了这些理论在闭流形和带边界流形上的规范不变性行为，为更高阶规范理论的发展提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We derive higher Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) and gauged WZW (gWZW) terms within strict higher Chern--Simons (CS) gauge theory. Starting from the Cartan homotopy formula, we obtain the $(2n+2)$-dimensional higher CS forms and transgression forms for strict Lie 2-groups presented by Lie crossed modules. Given two 2-connections related by a higher gauge transformation, higher transgression forms yield canonical higher WZW and gWZW terms. We prove that, for the symmetric invariant polynomial associated with differential crossed modules, the pure-gauge higher WZW term vanishes identically, whereas the higher gWZW term is exact. Consequently, the higher CS action is higher-gauge invariant on closed manifolds, and on manifolds with boundary all gauge dependence is encoded in boundary terms.

Abstract: We discuss a no-go theorem for Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) of quasiparticles (phonons) from the viewpoint of operator algebras, using the van Hove model. However, the self-consistency condition is a constraint concerning the definition of the field, and is insufficient to establish the no-go theorem for BEC. In this paper, we prove the no-go theorem for BEC via two routes. First, imposing time cluster properties on the $β$-KMS states precludes BEC. Second, under nonlinear dispersion with $s > 2$, the treatment of infrared divergences automatically reduces the algebra of physical observables, and BEC is mathematically excluded on the reduced algebra. In particular, the latter property admits an interpretation in terms of the ideal theory of the resolvent algebra.

</details>

Abstract: Standard zeta function regularisation enforces a scale-independent prescription for spectral aggregation, effectively fixing the relative weight of spectral contributions. We relax this constraint by replacing the derivative at $s=0$ with a finite-difference construction based on $ζ_{A}(0)$ and $ζ_{A}(q-1)$. In finite systems, it gives rise in the macroscopic limit to Tsallis-type quantities and a $q$-controlled information-geometric structure. In infinite dimensions, it yields an effective action whose variation $δΓ_{q}=\mathrm{Tr}(A^{-q}δA)$ realises scale-dependent spectral weighting. Within this framework, zeta function regularisation, effective action, nonextensive scaling, and information geometry emerge as manifestations of a common principle of finite-difference spectral aggregation.

</details>

Conclusion: 提出的Tricomi框架为非分数阶建模宽记忆异常弛豫提供了系统方法，兼具理论严谨性、被动可实现性和数值可处理性，在介电、电化学和生物组织等领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Anomalous relaxation with memory spectra arises in disordered solids, soft matter, biological tissues and electrochemical interfaces. Fractional-order models capture broad power-law behaviour efficiently, but they can obscure spectral structure and are not always convenient for passive realisation or finite-dimensional simulation. We introduce a non-fractional passive framework based on the Tricomi confluent hypergeometric function, combined with a bounded Moebius normalisation that enforces prescribed low-frequency and high-frequency plateaux while preserving a broad dispersive transition. The resulting family contains the Debye and Cole-Cole responses as exact subcases, while extending them to asymmetric two-plateau dispersive laws with independently tunable low- and high-frequency exponents. For an admissible parameter range, we prove that the bounded block admits a Stieltjes representation with nonnegative spectral density, implying complete monotonicity, passivity, causality and compatibility with standard circuit and state-space descriptions. Building on this structure, we derive a passive Gauss-Stieltjes discretisation leading to Foster-type rational approximations and first-order state-space realisations with positive poles and residues. Numerical experiments show convergence of these finite-dimensional approximations across moderate-memory and long-tail regimes, enabling passive reduced-order representations of broad-memory responses. The framework is then validated on broadband dielectric data and battery electrochemical impedance spectra. In tissues, multi-block Tricomi mixtures improve complex-domain fitting accuracy relative to classical Cole-Cole baselines while preserving interpretable modal structure.

</details>

Conclusion: 论文表明，在QED线性发散的情况下，可以构造次级广义散射算符来严格处理散射问题，而不需要依赖ε展开，从而部分解决了奥本海默提出的关于QED散射理论数学严格性的问题。

Abstract: First, we consider generalized wave and scattering operators and derive modifications of commutation relations (between scattering operators and unperturbed operators) when the corresponding deviation factors behave as $\exp\{i t {\mathcal C}_{\pm}\}$ for $t\to \pm \infty$. Then, we construct so called secondary generalized scattering operators for the related case of linear divergence in QED, which gives a positive answer (in that case) to the well-known problem of J. R. Oppenheimer regarding scattering operators in QED: "Can the procedure be freed of the expansion in $\varepsilon$ and carried out rigorously?"

</details>

Method: 基于Leopold和Pickl（2017）获得的非相对论量子电动力学（QED）平均场描述，采用弱耦合和动力学标度极限，推导了玻色子系统发射和吸收相干光子的有效非线性级联方程。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a system of bosons trapped in a confining potential, interacting with a quantized field of coherent photons in the mean field description of non-relativistic Quantum Electrodynamics (QED) obtained by [N. Leopold and P. Pickl , 2017]. We derive the effective nonlinear cascade equations governing the emission and absorption of coherent photons by the boson subsystem in a combined weak-coupling and kinetic-scaling limit. We demonstrate that solutions to this nonlinear cascade describe a monotone decreasing energy flow in the boson subsystem. Thereby, we prove that a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) forms dynamically, under conservation of the total boson $L^2$ mass. We note that this process is crucially different from thermal relaxation to the ground state, and fundamentally depends on the nonlinear nature of the cascade dynamics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子几何张量是厄米的，包含实对称量子度量和虚反对称贝里曲率。本文旨在探索塞曼量子几何张量的非厄米特性及其物理意义。

Method: 将塞曼量子几何张量分解为正常和反常的度规-曲率部分，分析其在二维狄拉克系统中的表现，并通过线性响应理论建立与输运系数的对应关系。

Result: 发现塞曼量子几何张量具有非厄米结构，包含传统厄米部分和新的反常部分。在狄拉克系统中，反常塞曼曲率产生径向通量奇点，与狄拉克节点的切向绕场对偶。线性响应中，回旋电导率的四个对称分辨分量与塞曼量子几何张量的四个分量一一对应。

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一框架，将非厄米塞曼量子几何、局部狄拉克节点拓扑和可测量的输运特征联系起来，为实验探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: The conventional quantum geometric tensor (QGT) is Hermitian, with a real symmetric quantum metric and an imaginary antisymmetric Berry curvature. We show that the Zeeman QGT is generically non-Hermitian and admits a natural decomposition into normal and anomalous metric-curvature sectors. The normal sector reduces to the conventional Hermitian structure, whereas the anomalous sector contains an imaginary symmetric metric-like tensor and a real antisymmetric curvature-like tensor with no counterpart in the standard QGT. In a two-dimensional Dirac system, the anomalous Zeeman curvature develops a radial flux singularity that is Hodge-dual to the tangential winding field of the Dirac node. This recasts the same local $π_1$ topology into a curvature-flux language, analogous to the flux representation of global $π_2$ topology by the conventional Berry curvature. At the level of linear response, the four symmetry-resolved components of the gyrotropic conductivity are in one-to-one correspondence with the four components of the Zeeman QGT, while their distinct low-frequency scalings provide an additional diagnostic for isolating the underlying geometric sector. The reciprocal kinetic magnetoelectric response offers a complementary experimental route to probe the same structure. These results establish a unified framework connecting non-Hermitian Zeeman quantum geometry, local Dirac-node topology, and measurable transport signatures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息几何构造在统计物理学和Friston自由能原理的物理解释中都有应用，该研究旨在探索信息几何空间中的基本动力学，特别是伯努利空间中的经典和量子行为。

Method: 推导伯努利空间中拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子的谱，求解亥姆霍兹方程的格林函数，在信息空间中量化动量，研究自由粒子和各种量子（谐振）振子的能量和波函数。

Result: 获得了伯努利空间中拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子的谱和亥姆霍兹方程的格林函数，证明了Goehle和Griffin使用的Kullback-Leibler势能的二次近似导致信息空间中的量子振子等价于欧几里得空间中的量子摆。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将经典和量子动力学框架扩展到信息几何空间，建立了信息空间量子振子与欧几里得空间量子摆之间的等价关系，为信息几何在统计物理和认知科学中的应用提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We study elementary classical and quantum dynamics in an information geometric space corresponding to a Bernoulli random variable, extending work by Goehle and Griffin [Chaos, Solitons & Fractals, 188, 115535, (2024)], who study the information theoretic analog of the spring-mass system. Information geometric constructions are useful in both statistical physics and in physical interpretations of Friston's free energy principle, a form of the Bayesian brain hypothesis. In this letter, we derive the spectrum for the Laplace-Beltrami operator in Bernoulli space and find Green's functions for the Helmholtz equation, which provides solutions to the wave, heat, and Poisson equations. We then show how to quantize momentum in Bernoulli space and obtain energies and wavefunctions for both a free particle and a variety of quantum (harmonic) oscillators in this space. In particular, we show that quadratic approximation of the Kullback-Leibler potential used by Goehle and Griffin results in a quantum oscillator in information space that is equivalent to a quantum pendulum in Euclidean space.

</details>


### [90] [Generative Circuit Design for Quantum-Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09756)
*Ryota Kemmoku,Qi Gao,Shu Kanno,Kimberlee Keithley,Ikko Hamamura,Naoki Yamamoto,Kouhei Nakaji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于生成量子本征求解器(GQE)的框架，通过Transformer策略优化量子态制备电路结构，在量子选择组态相互作用(QSCI)中显著减少门数并提高精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSCI是噪声量子设备上近似电子基态的可行方法，但硬件约束下制备高质量子空间需要优化态制备电路设计

Method: 使用基于Transformer的策略训练GQE框架，优化QSCI子空间能量的ansatz结构，在N2分子活性空间(最多32量子比特)上验证

Result: 优化电路以显著更低的门数达到化学精度：相比一阶Trotter近似平均减少98%两量子比特门，相比qDRIFT近似减少83%；波函数紧凑性与热浴组态相互作用(HCI)相当，在强关联拉伸键区域，达到化学精度所需子空间比HCI小50%

Conclusion: GQE框架能有效优化QSCI的态制备电路，大幅降低硬件要求，在强关联体系中表现优于经典HCI方法，为大规模量子化学计算提供了实用方案

Abstract: Quantum-selected configuration interaction (QSCI) has emerged as a feasible approach for approximating electronic ground states on noisy quantum devices toward large-system demonstrations. In QSCI, Slater determinants are sampled from a quantum-prepared state, and the Hamiltonian is then diagonalized in the sampled subspace. To create a high-quality subspace under hardware constraints, the design of the state-preparation circuit is crucial. Here, we present a Generative Quantum Eigensolver (GQE)-based framework that optimizes ansatz structures using a Transformer policy trained on the QSCI subspace energy. We validate the framework on N2 in active spaces of up to 32 qubits. We found that the optimized circuits reach chemical precision with substantially lower gate counts than time-evolved circuits. Quantitatively, this corresponds to an average reduction of 98% in the required two-qubit gate count relative to the single-step first-order Trotterized approximation and 83% relative to the qDRIFT approximation. Furthermore, the resulting wavefunctions are competitive with heat-bath configuration interaction (HCI) in terms of compactness. In stretched-bond, strongly correlated regimes, they achieve chemical precision with subspaces that are 50% smaller than those required by HCI.

</details>


### [91] [Sector length distributions of recursively definable graph states through analytic combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09766)
*Eloïc Vallée,Kenneth Goodenough,Paul E. Gunnells,Tim Coopmans,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用生成函数方法分析量子态的Shor-Laflamme分布，针对递归可定义的图态给出了闭式表达式，并推导了纠缠度量和噪声鲁棒性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态的Shor-Laflamme分布（SLD）描述了k体关联性，可用于研究纠缠和纠错，但传统方法计算复杂，解析表达式难以获得。需要开发更有效的数学工具来处理SLD分析。

Method: 采用生成函数方法编码量子态家族的SLD，而非单个态的SLD。针对"递归可定义"的图态（包括路径图、环图、星图、网格图等）推导闭式表达式。应用解析组合数学技术。

Result: 获得了递归可定义图态的SLD闭式表达式，并推导出：1）可集中纠缠的解析表达式；2）去极化噪声下保真度的界限；3）多体纠缠判据。为一大类常见图态提供了完整的解析描述。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了生成函数和解析组合数学在量子信息理论中的有效性，为SLD分析提供了新工具，可应用于纠缠度量和噪声鲁棒性分析，为更广泛的量子态家族研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The sector length distribution or Shor-Laflamme distribution (SLD) of quantum states is governed by the $k$-body correlations amongst the different systems, and has been used to study entanglement and error correction. A succinct description of a quantum state's SLD can be obtained by representing it through the coefficients of an appropriate weight enumerator polynomial, yielding bounds on fidelity under depolarizing noise and on multipartite entanglement. However, such expressions quickly grow out of hand and are generally difficult to achieve analytically, reflecting the computational hardness of the SLD. We sidestep this problem and, instead of a single state's SLDs, encode a family of quantum state's SLD as a generating function. We then find closed-form expressions for a large class of graph states which we call `recursively definable' and which include many common graphs such as path graphs, cycle graphs, star graphs, grid graphs, and more. As direct corollary, we obtain analytical expressions for such graph states' concentratable entanglement, bounds on their depolarizing fidelity, and a multipartite entanglement criterion. Our work opens up the use of generating functions and more generally analytic combinatorics to solve problems in quantum information theory.

</details>


### [92] [Leggett-Garg Inequality Violations Bound Quantum Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09772)
*Nick Abboud,Yuntao Guan,Barry Bradlyn,Jorge Noronha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Leggett-Garg不等式违反为有界可观测量在稳态纯态和热态中提供了量子Fisher信息的严格下界，将定性的实在性基础测试转化为量子灵敏度的定量见证，并在集体设置中为多体系统的多体纠缠深度提供下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Leggett-Garg不等式违反从量子力学基础测试转化为实用的量子资源定量见证，特别是建立时间相关性与量子Fisher信息、多体纠缠深度之间的联系，为实验上可访问的多体量子相干见证提供理论基础。

Method: 证明有界可观测量在稳态纯态和热态中违反Leggett-Garg不等式可推导出量子Fisher信息的严格下界；分析Leggett-Garg违反受谱矩、磁化率和f-求和规则约束的机制；展示单个集体可观测量时间相关性作为多体量子相干见证的实验可行性。

Result: 建立了Leggett-Garg不等式违反与量子Fisher信息之间的定量关系；证明了时间相关性可作为多体纠缠深度的下界；揭示了Leggett-Garg违反受多体响应组织相同约束的规律；提供了无需全态重构的实验可访问多体量子相干见证方案。

Conclusion: Leggett-Garg不等式违反不仅是量子实在性的定性测试，更是量子Fisher信息、多体纠缠深度等量子资源的定量见证，单个集体可观测量时间相关性为实验探测多体量子相干提供了实用工具，连接了基础量子力学与量子计量学、多体物理。

Abstract: We prove that a violation of a Leggett-Garg inequality for bounded observables in stationary pure states and thermal states yields a rigorous lower bound on the quantum Fisher information. This turns a qualitative foundations test of realism in quantum systems into a quantitative witness of useful quantum sensitivity and, in the collective setting, into a lower bound on multipartite entanglement depth in many-body systems. We further demonstrate that Leggett-Garg violations are constrained by the same spectral moments, susceptibilities, and $f$-sum-rule bounds that organize many-body response. Our results show that temporal correlations of a single collective observable can serve as an experimentally accessible witness of many-body quantum coherence, without requiring full state reconstruction.

</details>


### [93] [Rigorous quantum state tomography for distributed quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09775)
*Hans Mättig-Vásquez,Aldo Delgado,Luciano Pereira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于分布式量子计算机的量子态层析协议，无需假设远程纠缠作为原始资源，基于投影最小二乘方法，仅使用局部操作和经典通信


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算通过联网多个量子处理器来扩展量子设备规模，但现有层析协议通常假设远程纠缠作为原始资源，这在实际实现中具有挑战性

Method: 将基于投影2-设计的投影最小二乘层析扩展到多量子处理器系统，仅使用各处理器内的局部操作和节点间的经典通信，假设单个量子处理器内的纠缠可信，可使用相互无偏基执行

Result: 推导了PLS估计量的严格非渐近迹范数误差界，明确显示了与节点数的指数依赖关系；建立了从PLS估计量估计纠缠负性的认证误差界；对分布在多个设备上的最多7个量子比特系统进行了数值模拟验证

Conclusion: 该协议为分布式量子计算机提供了一种实用的量子态层析方法，无需远程纠缠资源，具有理论保证的误差界，并通过数值模拟验证了理论结果

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing offers a promising approach to scaling quantum devices by networking multiple quantum processors. We present a quantum state tomography protocol tailored for distributed quantum computers that avoids assuming remote entanglement as a primitive resource. The protocol extends projected least-squares (PLS) tomography based on projective 2-designs to systems composed of multiple quantum processors, using only local operations within each processor and classical communication between nodes. Assuming entanglement within each individual quantum processor is trusted, the protocol can be executed using mutually unbiased bases. We derive rigorous, non-asymptotic trace-norm error bounds for the PLS estimator, with explicit exponential dependence on the number of nodes. In addition, we establish certified error bounds for estimating entanglement negativity from the PLS estimator. Numerical simulations for systems of up to seven qubits distributed across several devices validate the theoretical error bounds.

</details>


### [94] [Resist-free shadow deposition using silicon trenches for Josephson junctions in superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09796)
*Tathagata Banerjee,Stephen Daniel Funni,Saswata Roy,Judy J. Cha,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无光刻胶的约瑟夫森结制造方法，使用刻蚀硅沟槽技术，实现了184微秒的中位能量弛豫时间，减少了化学污染并提高了工艺兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于光刻胶的约瑟夫森结制造方法存在化学污染问题，限制了衬底表面处理、结材料和可扩展性。需要一种无光刻胶的制造方案来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用基于刻蚀硅沟槽的无光刻胶制造方法，该方法与CMOS工艺兼容，可轻松集成到现有的量子比特基础层制造和化学处理创新中。制造了Al-AlOx-Al约瑟夫森结和量子比特。

Result: 测量到中位能量弛豫时间高达184微秒，衬底-金属界面污染最小，35小时时间尺度上的能量弛豫波动窄且呈正态分布。

Conclusion: 该方法为衬底制备和新材料平台提供了更宽的工艺窗口，解决了传统光刻胶方法的污染问题，提高了超导量子比特制造的性能和兼容性。

Abstract: Superconducting qubit fabrication innovations continue to be explored to achieve higher performance. Despite improvements to base layer fabrication and processing, resist-based Josephson junction (JJ) schemes have largely remained unchanged. The polymer mask during deposition causes chemical contamination and limits in situ and ex situ surface preparation, junction materials, and scalability. Here, we demonstrate a resist-free approach to junction fabrication based on etched silicon trenches that is CMOS compatible and easily integrated into existing innovations in qubit base layer fabrication and chemical processing. We fabricate Al-AlOx-Al JJs and qubits using this method, measuring median energy relaxation times up to 184 microseconds. We find minimal contamination at the substrate-metal interface and fluctuations of energy relaxation on a 35 hour timescale that are narrow and normally distributed. The method widens the process window for substrate preparation and new materials platforms.

</details>


### [95] [Optimising Quantum Error Correction Using Morphing Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09797)
*Mackenzie H. Shaw,Barbara M. Terhal*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子纠错码的定义和搜索通常不考虑综合征提取电路在基本门和测量层面的具体实现方式。这限制了代码在实际硬件上的优化潜力，特别是在连接性、门选择和资源利用方面。

Method: 采用变形电路方法优化综合征提取电路和代码，具体包括：1) 在阿贝尔双块群代数代码间进行代码优化；2) 处理二维代码的边界问题；3) 设计具有单次提取特性的代码；4) 提高对抗测量和重置错误的稳定性实验性能。特别地，交替综合征提取电路（通过交替时间反转轮次执行）可视为两轮变形电路，其容错特性比非交替电路更容易计算分析。

Result: 该方法发现了具有实际意义的新代码和综合征提取电路，包括阿贝尔双块群代数变形电路，这些电路具有比现有电路更好的代码参数和连接性要求。

Conclusion: 变形电路为量子纠错码的优化提供了系统方法，能够直接在硬件实现层面（连接性、门选择、资源使用）优化代码性能，为实际量子计算系统的设计提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) codes are traditionally defined and searched for without specifying the manner in which its syndrome extraction circuits are executed using elementary gates and measurements. We show how morphing circuits introduced in Refs. [1-3] provide a way of optimising syndrome extraction circuits and codes directly in terms of connectivity, choice of two-qubit gate (ISWAP versus CNOT) and number of physical qubits. We discuss morphing circuits in code optimisation among Abelian two-block group algebra (2BGA) codes, handling boundaries for 2D codes, codes with single-shot properties, and improving performance in stability experiments against measurement and reset errors. We show that alternating syndrome extraction circuits - executed with alternating time-reversed rounds - can be viewed as a two-round morphing circuit whose fault-tolerant properties are computationally much easier to examine than non-alternating syndrome extraction circuits. Our methods find new codes and syndrome extraction circuits of practical interest, including Abelian 2BGA morphing circuits with better code parameters and connectivity than existing circuits.
  [1] Matt McEwen, Dave Bacon, and Craig Gidney. Relaxing hardware requirements for surface code circuits using time-dynamics. Quantum, 7:1172, 2023. [2] Craig Gidney and Cody Jones. New circuits and an open source decoder for the color code, 2023. [3] Mackenzie H. Shaw and Barbara M. Terhal. Lowering connectivity requirements for bivariate bicycle codes using morphing circuits.

</details>


### [96] [Discovery of the Solution to the "Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen Paradox"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09826)
*Roman Schnabel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了1935年爱因斯坦-波多尔斯基-罗森（EPR）的思想实验，指出其正确性已获实验验证，但其中包含的悖论根源可被定位并最终解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPR思想实验是量子物理学中最著名的思想实验，它首次描述了量子关联，为当今"深度"量子技术提供了早期洞见。然而，该工作中包含一个悖论，薛定谔在1935年就已认识到这一点，后来的贝尔实验进一步证实了这个悖论。作者旨在定位这个悖论在推理链中的起源，从而最终解决它。

Method: 作者通过重新审视EPR的原始推理链，分析其中导致悖论的逻辑环节。虽然没有明确说明具体分析方法，但可以推断作者采用了逻辑分析和哲学推理的方法，结合量子力学基本原理，来识别悖论的根源。

Result: 作者声称能够精确定位悖论在EPR推理链中的起源。这意味着找到了导致矛盾的具体逻辑环节或假设，从而为最终解决这个长期存在的量子力学悖论提供了关键线索。

Conclusion: 通过定位EPR悖论在推理链中的具体起源，作者最终解决了这个悖论。这表明EPR思想实验虽然正确且具有开创性，但其中的悖论并非量子力学本身的内在矛盾，而是源于特定的推理假设或逻辑错误。

Abstract: In 1935, Albert Einstein, Boris Podolsky and Nathan Rosen (EPR) published a thought experiment that is entirely correct, has been demonstrated in real experiments, and is now the most famous in quantum physics. Their pioneering work described, for the first time, quantum correlations and can be regarded as a very early glimpse into today's 'deep' quantum technologies, by which I mean those that enhance functionality by making use of quantum correlations. However, their work also contains a paradox that Erwin Schroedinger had already recognised as such in 1935 and which has since been cemented by the so-called Bell experiments. Here, I am now able to pinpoint the origin of the paradox within the chain of reasoning, which ultimately resolves the paradox.

</details>


### [97] [Planted-solution SAT and Ising benchmarks from integer factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09837)
*Itay Hen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于整数分解的SAT求解器和伊辛优化基准测试实例族，将N=p×q的算术约束编码为CNF公式，提供可验证的基准测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为SAT求解器和伊辛优化提供可扩展、结构化且可验证的基准测试实例，这些实例具有内置的真实解(p,q)，便于明确验证求解器输出

Method: 将两个素数p和q的乘积N=p×q的算术约束编码为合取范式(CNF)公式，满足该公式的赋值对应N的有效分解。对于d位素数，进位收缩总数约为d^4量级

Result: SAT求解器的经验基准测试显示，在测试范围内，中位运行时间随因子位长的增加呈指数增长。该构造提供了由单个参数控制的可扩展基准测试族

Conclusion: 基于整数分解的构造为SAT求解器和伊辛优化提供了可扩展、结构化、可验证的基准测试实例族，并配有开源生成软件

Abstract: We present a family of planted-solution benchmark instances for satisfiability (SAT) solvers and Ising optimization derived from integer factorization. Given two primes $p$ and $q$, the construction encodes the arithmetic constraints of $N = p \times q$ as a conjunctive normal form (CNF) formula whose satisfying assignments correspond to valid factorizations of~$N$. The known pair $(p,q)$ serves as a built-in ground truth, enabling unambiguous verification of solver output. We show that for two $d$-bit primes the total number of carry contractions is on the order of $d^4$. Empirical benchmarks with SAT solvers show that median runtime grows exponentially in the bit-length of the factors over the range tested. The construction provides a scalable, structured, and verifiable benchmark family controlled by a single parameter, accompanied by open-source generation software.

</details>


### [98] [A Polylogarithmic-Depth Quantum Multiplier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09847)
*Fred Sun,Anton Borissov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子整数乘法算法，电路深度和T深度均为O(log² n)，使用O(n²)门和辅助量子比特，在Clifford+T模型中具有最低的T深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高效的量子整数乘法算法对于实现大规模容错量子计算至关重要。现有乘法算法在电路深度和T深度方面存在优化空间，特别是在Clifford+T模型中，T深度直接影响容错量子计算的资源消耗和可行性。

Method: 通过指示器控制的复制生成部分积，然后使用二进制加法器树进行累加。这种方法实现了并行累加，每层仅增加对数深度的开销。算法在Clifford+T门集合下实现，特别优化了T深度。

Result: 实现了电路深度和T深度均为O(log² n)的量子整数乘法算法，使用O(n²)门和辅助量子比特。在Clifford+T模型中，该设计具有已知最低的T深度，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该量子乘法算法通过优化电路深度和T深度，特别是实现了O(log² n)的T深度，为大规模容错量子算法的实际可行性提供了重要进展，是量子计算基础算术运算的重要改进。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for multiplying two $n$-bit integers with overall circuit depth and $T$-depth both bounded by $O(\log^{2} n)$, while using $O(n^{2})$ gates and ancillary qubits. Our construction generates partial products via indicator-controlled copying and adds them using a binary adder tree, enabling parallel accumulation with logarithmic depth overhead per level. To the best of our knowledge, our design has the lowest $T$-depth among all multiplication algorithms using the Clifford + $T$ model. By optimizing both circuit depth and $T$-depth, our construction advances the practical feasibility of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [99] [Automorphism-Induced Entanglement Bounds in Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11059)
*Saikat Sur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了具有非平凡自同构群的图结构上多体哈密顿量基态的最大平衡二分纠缠熵的上界，该界由保持二分结构的自同构子群的不可约表示多重度的加权和对数给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于简并度的纠缠熵上界在某些情况下不够紧致。本文旨在利用哈密顿量的对称性（自同构群）来推导更紧致的纠缠熵上界，特别是在具有高度对称性的图结构上。

Method: 作者推导了一个与具体模型无关的上界，适用于定义在具有非平凡自同构群的图上的多体哈密顿量。该上界基于保持二分结构的自同构子群的不可约表示多重度的加权和对数。

Result: 对于完全图K_n，新上界将纠缠熵的标度从线性改进为对数，与熵的精确值一致。新上界与已知的简并度上界互补，两者没有绝对的优劣关系。

Conclusion: 利用哈密顿量的对称性（自同构群）可以推导出比简并度上界更紧致的纠缠熵上界，特别是在高度对称的系统中。这一结果为理解对称性对量子纠缠的限制提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We derive an upper bound on the maximum balanced bipartite entanglement entropy of ground states of many-body Hamiltonians defined on a graph, agnostic to any particular model, that possesses a nontrivial automorphism group. We show that the entropy is bounded by the logarithm of a weighted sum of multiplicities of irreducible representations of the bipartition-preserving automorphism subgroup. This bound complements the known degeneracy-based bound, with neither universally dominating the other. For the complete graph $K_n$, the new bound yields an exponential improvement from linear to logarithmic scaling in the system size, consistent with the exact value of the entropy.

</details>


### [100] [Protein-Ligand Free Energy Perturbation on Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09857)
*Zhen Li,Milana Bazayeva,Thaddeus Pellegrini,Mario Motta,Subhamoy Bhowmik,Susanta Das,Danil Kaliakin,Fangchun Liang,Akhil Shajan,Kenneth M. Merz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合经典与量子硬件的QM/MM-FEP混合方法，用于克服传统力场参数问题，在蛋白质-配体复合物研究中取得与实验合理一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于力场的自由能微扰方法在蛋白质-配体复合物研究中存在力场参数相关的问题，需要开发更精确的计算方法。

Method: 开发了QM/MM混合方法，在MM部分使用AMBER（ff19SB和GAFF2力场），QM部分使用QUICK进行Hartree-Fock计算，并开发Python接口实现经典硬件上的HCI基准计算和量子硬件上的LUCJ-SQD/extSQD计算。

Result: 以热解酶抑制剂为例，经典HCI方法与量子LUCJ-SQD/extSQD方法均与实验结果合理一致，后者结果更接近实验值，且两种方法的执行时间相当。

Conclusion: 该方法在NISQ时代具有高应用潜力，量子计算方法在精度和执行时间方面均表现出竞争力。

Abstract: The use of free energy perturbation (FEP) methods to study protein-ligand complexes is one of the most important tools in structure-based drug design. Because FEP methods typically rely on force fields, they may suffer from force field parameter-related issues. Herein, we present a quantum mechanics/molecular mechanics (QM/MM) hybrid method to overcome deficiencies in force-field models by using QM bookending approaches on both classical and quantum hardware. In the MM part of this QM/MM FEP method, AMBER is used to simulate the protein receptor and the unperturbed moiety of the ligand, with the ff19SB and GAFF2 force fields. In the QM part, QUICK was used to conduct Hartree-Fock (HF) calculations, followed by heat-bath configuration interaction (HCI) as a benchmark on classical devices. To enable the HCI function in QUICK, we developed a Python-based interface to execute HCI from IBM's qiskit-addon-dice-solver. Moreover, the same interface also enabled this work to execute QM/MM FEP calculations on quantum hardware using the Local Unitary Cluster Jastrow (LUCJ) ansatz, followed by sample-based diagonalization (SQD) and extended-SQD (extSQD) post-processing. Using a series of thermolysis inhibitors as an example, we find reasonable agreement with experiment between the classical HCI method and the LUCJ-SQD/extSQD method, with the latter yielding a result closer to the experimental value. The execution time between the HCI-based FEP method and the LUCJ-SQD/extSQD-based FEP method is also comparable, indicating a high potential for utility in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era.

</details>


### [101] [Q-PIPE A Practical Quantum Phase Encoding Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09869)
*Brian García Sarmina,Emmanuel Martínez-Guerrero,Janeth De Anda Gil,Sun Guo-Hua,Dong Shi-Hai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q-PIPE提出了一种新的量子图像相位编码方法，通过量子相位反冲机制和格雷码序列优化空间遍历，实现高效图像数据量子态编码，相比传统方法显著降低门复杂度并支持原生有限差分计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子图像处理面临经典高维图像数据高效转换为量子态的挑战。现有方法存在权衡：振幅编码（FRQI）门复杂度高且算术能力有限，基态编码（NEQR）初始化开销随图像分辨率和强度位深线性增长，频域方法需要复杂变换进行基本像素运算并需要大量后处理重建像素信息。缺乏实用的相位编码方法是主要问题。

Method: 提出Q-PIPE（量子灰度相位注入像素编码）方法：利用量子相位反冲机制，通过优化的格雷码序列进行空间遍历，将连续强度值高效映射到计算基态。采用基本门计数O(qN)，相比标准基态编码实现O(logN)改进。直接在相位域操作，无需深度算术电路即可原生计算有限差分。通过将输入映射到[-π, π]范围并引入与空间寄存器维度成反比的概率阈值方程，缓解经典读出漏洞（相位混叠和频谱泄漏）。

Result: 通过量子边缘检测（QED）的概念验证展示了强准确性：对于量化输入获得精确重建，对于连续数据在多个基准数据集上获得低平均绝对误差（MAE）。该方法建立了高度可并行化、NISQ兼容的子程序。

Conclusion: Q-PIPE建立了一种高效的量子图像相位编码方法，显著降低了量子图像处理的输入/输出数据加载开销，推进了量子计算机视觉发展，同时减少了更广泛量子机器学习工作流中的I/O负担。

Abstract: A major hurdle in Quantum Image Processing (QIMP) is efficiently transferring classical, high-dimensional image data into quantum states. Current methods face trade-offs: amplitude encoding (FRQI) is computationally expensive in gate complexity and limited arithmetic capabilities, while basis encoding (NEQR) incurs heavy initialization overhead scaling with image resolution and intensity bit-depth. Frequency-domain approaches further demand complex transformations for basic pixel-wise arithmetic and extensive post-processing to reconstruct pixel information. To address the lack of practical phase encodings, we introduce Q-PIPE (Quantum-Gray Phase Injection for Pixel Encoding). Exploiting the quantum phase kickback mechanism and optimized spatial traversal via a Gray-code sequence, Q-PIPE efficiently maps continuous intensity values into the computational basis with an elementary gate count of $O(qN)$ a $O(\text{log}N)$ improvement over standard basis encoding. Operating directly in the phase domain enables native computation of finite differences without deep arithmetic circuits. Classical readout vulnerabilities, including phase aliasing and spectral leakage, are mitigated by mapping inputs to $[-π, π]$ and introducing a probability threshold equation that scales inversely with the dimension of the spatial register. A proof-of-concept performing Quantum Edge Detection (QED) via directional derivatives demonstrates strong accuracy, yielding exact reconstructions for quantized inputs and low Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for continuous data across multiple benchmark datasets. Ultimately, Q-PIPE establishes a highly parallelizable, NISQ-compatible subroutine that advances quantum computer vision while reducing input/output (I/O) data-loading overhead in broader Quantum Machine Learning (QML) workflows.

</details>


### [102] [Detuning-Controlled Phase Transition from Passive to Active Regimes in Non-Markovian Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09888)
*Meysam Helmi Barati Farimani,Ali Mortezapour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究两量子比特量子电池，发现非马尔可夫环境记忆与相干充电竞争可增强储能和充电功率，失谐诱导可提取功的一阶相变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子电池在开放量子系统中的性能优化，研究环境记忆效应与相干充电的竞争如何影响能量存储和可提取功，为噪声鲁棒的量子能量存储提供策略

Method: 研究两量子比特量子电池模型，调节充电器-电池耦合强度、失谐参数和环境相互作用，分析非马尔可夫环境记忆效应与相干能量交换的干涉机制

Result: 发现环境记忆可增强储能和充电功率，强耗散抑制ergotropy；失谐具有双重作用：减少耗散并诱导记忆核相移；可提取功在临界失谐处出现不连续跃变，呈现一阶相变；耦合-失谐相图显示热力学非活跃与做功区域的尖锐边界

Conclusion: 相位控制的相干性和非马尔可夫性为开放量子电池中的功提取优化提供了强大机制，为噪声鲁棒的量子能量存储提供了实用策略

Abstract: We investigate a two-qubit quantum battery where coherent charger-battery coupling competes with non-Markovian environmental interactions. By tuning the coupling strengths and detuning, we identify regimes in which environmental memory enhances energy storage and charging power, while strong dissipation suppresses ergotropy by driving the battery into passive states. We show that detuning plays a dual role: reducing dissipation and inducing a phase shift in the memory kernel that controls the interference between coherent energy exchange and environment-induced backflow. As a result, although the stored energy varies smoothly, the extractable work exhibits a discontinuous onset at a critical detuning, signaling a first-order phase transition in ergotropy. The corresponding phase diagram in the coupling-detuning plane reveals a sharp boundary between thermodynamically inactive and work-producing regimes. Our results demonstrate that phase-controlled coherence and non-Markovianity provide a powerful mechanism for optimizing work extraction in open quantum batteries, offering practical strategies for noise-resilient quantum energy storage.

</details>


### [103] [Enhanced dissipative criticality at an exceptional point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09892)
*Jongjun M. Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展开放Dicke模型中，当异常点与耗散相变重合时，临界涨落被强烈放大，临界指数被修正，为临界量子传感提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索异常点（非厄米简并点）与耗散相变重合时对临界行为的影响，研究如何利用异常点工程化调控开放量子系统的临界标度。

Method: 采用扩展开放Dicke模型（两个腔耦合到集体自旋），通过数值模拟研究异常点与耗散相变重合时的临界行为，并基于异常点诱导的Jordan块动力学建立解析理论。

Result: 数值结果显示在正常相和超辐射相中都出现了增强的临界标度行为，与基于异常点诱导Jordan块动力学的解析理论一致，表明临界涨落被强烈放大且受修正的临界指数支配。

Conclusion: 异常点可作为工程化调控开放量子系统临界标度的机制，在临界量子传感等领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) represent non-Hermitian degeneracies where eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce, giving rise to enhanced sensitivity and critically damped dynamics. We demonstrate that when an EP coincides with a dissipative phase transition in an extended open Dicke model of two cavities coupled to a collective spin, the critical fluctuations are strongly amplified and governed by modified critical exponents. Numerical results reveal enhanced critical scaling in both the normal and superradiant phases, in agreement with an analytical theory based on EP-induced Jordan-block dynamics. Our results establish EPs as a mechanism to engineer critical scaling in open quantum systems, with potential applications to critical quantum sensing.

</details>


### [104] [Inequivalence of Landau-Lifshitz and Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics for a single quantum spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09900)
*Yuefei Liu,Olle Eriksson,Erik Sjöqvist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子Landau-Lifshitz方程(q-LL)与量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程(q-LLG)在单自旋s≥1时不等价，不同于经典对应物总是等价的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子Landau-Lifshitz方程和量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程之间的等价关系，这两种非线性纯度保持主方程将经典原子自旋动力学扩展到量子领域。经典LL和LLG方程对于任意数量自旋都是等价的（仅时间参数重新标度），但量子版本只在特定情况下等价。

Method: 通过分析单自旋系统（特别是自旋-1粒子在各项异性晶体场中），比较q-LL和q-LLG方程的时间演化。引入时间重新标度失配参数来量化两种动力学的不等价性。

Result: 对于自旋-1粒子，q-LL和q-LLG方程产生不等价的时间演化。虽然两种动力学在定性上相似，但存在可量化的差异，通过时间重新标度失配参数可以测量这种不等价性。

Conclusion: 量子Landau-Lifshitz方程和量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程在单自旋s≥1时不等价，这揭示了两种方程中耗散机制的根本差异，尽管产生的轨迹在定性上相似。

Abstract: We examine the relation between the quantum Landau-Lifshitz equation ($q$-LL) [Phys. Rev. Lett. 110, 147201 (2013)] and quantum Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation ($q$-LLG) [Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 266704 (2024)]; two non-linear purity preserving master equations that extend classical atomistic spin dynamics into the quantum regime. While the classical LL and LLG counterparts for any number of spins are known to be equivalent, i.e., give identical spin trajectories up to a rescaling of the time parameter, the quantum formulations are equivalent only in certain cases, such as for pure states or for arbitrary single spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ states. Here, we demonstrate that this equivalence breaks down even at the level of a single spin, provided $s \geq 1$. Focusing on a spin-1 particle in an anisotropic crystal field, we show that the $q$-LL and $q$-LLG equations generate inequivalent time evolution. We introduce temporal rescaling misfits that quantify the inequivalence of the two types of dynamics. Although our results highlight fundamental differences in dissipation mechanisms encoded in these equations, the resulting trajectories remain qualitatively similar for this system.

</details>


### [105] [Phase-enhanced excitations in pumped collective nuclear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09915)
*Mihai A. Macovei,Fabian Richter,Adriana Pálffy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究核两能级系统在泄漏宽带腔中的量子动力学，通过双相干X射线场驱动，发现交叉关联增强核激发概率，核统计可从亚泊松到超泊松分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究外部驱动核两能级系统在泄漏腔中的量子动力学，探索相干泵浦下交叉关联对核激发概率、统计特性和集体效应的影响

Method: 理论分析核两能级系统在泄漏宽带腔中的量子动力学，采用双相干X射线电磁场同时驱动核系综和腔模，研究相同频率下交叉关联效应

Result: 交叉关联增强核激发概率（依赖外场相位差），核激发态呈现亚泊松到超泊松统计，揭示了光子吸收/发射过程中的诱导关联，交叉关联影响超辐射衰变和集体兰姆位移

Conclusion: 双相干驱动下交叉关联显著影响核系综量子动力学，可调控核统计特性，为控制核激发过程和集体量子效应提供理论依据

Abstract: The quantum dynamics of an externally driven ensemble of nuclear two-level systems embedded in a leaky broadband cavity is investigated theoretically. In the considered scenario both the nuclear ensemble and the cavity mode are coherently pumped by two externally applied x-ray electromagnetic fields. When the frequencies of the applied coherent fields are identical, cross-correlations among the existing decay channels increase the nuclear excitation probabilities depending on the phase difference of the applied fields. Our results show that the excited state of the nuclear ensemble may exhibit sub- to super-Poissonian nuclear statistics, demonstrating induced correlations during photon absorption or emission processes. The role of cross-correlations for the superradiant decay and the collective Lamb shift of the ensemble is also investigated.

</details>


### [106] [A Framework for Predicting Entanglement Spectra of Gapless Symmetry-Protected Topological States in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10128)
*Wen-Tao Xu,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种系统分析一维无隙对称保护拓扑态纠缠谱的框架，通过量子信道变换从平凡态预测非平凡态的边界共形场论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无隙对称保护拓扑态的纠缠谱由边界共形场论描述，但如何系统预测这些边界理论是一个关键开放问题。需要建立框架来理解和分析gSPT态的纠缠谱特性。

Method: 通过将SPT纠缠子应用于平凡临界态获得gSPT态，发现非平凡gSPT态的约化密度矩阵可通过量子信道从平凡态获得。该量子信道仅在纠缠切割附近作用，修改相应的共形边界条件，从而预测描述纠缠谱的边界共形场论。

Result: 该框架成功应用于不同对称性保护和不同中心荷的gSPT态，分析了纠缠切割边界条件的稳定性，为系统分析gSPT态纠缠谱提供了有效方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了系统分析和理解gSPT态纠缠谱的框架，通过量子信道变换连接平凡和非平凡gSPT态，能够预测描述纠缠谱的边界共形场论，解决了该领域的关键开放问题。

Abstract: The concept of gapped symmetry-protected topological (SPT) states has been generalized to gapless SPT (gSPT) states. Similar to gapped SPT states, gSPT states in one dimension exhibit universal degeneracies in their entanglement spectra. The entanglement spectra of gSPT states are further described by boundary conformal field theories, whose systematic prediction is a key open question. To address this problem, we focus on the class of gSPT states that are obtained by applying unitary SPT entanglers to trivial, critical states in one dimension. We find that the reduced density matrix of a non-trivial gSPT state can be obtained, either exactly or approximately, by applying a quantum channel to the reduced density matrix of the trivial gSPT state. This quantum channel acts only near the entanglement cut and modifies its corresponding conformal boundary condition, allowing us in turn to predict the boundary conformal field theory describing the entanglement spectra. We apply this framework to gSPT states protected by various symmetries and having different central charges, and further analyze the stability of boundary conditions of the entanglement cut. Our work thereby provides a framework for systematically analyzing and understanding the entanglement spectra of gSPT states.

</details>


### [107] [Logical Compilation for Multi-Qubit Iceberg Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09956)
*Cordell Mazzetti,Sayam Sethi,Rich Rines,Pranav Gokhale,Jonathan Mark Baker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个针对高码率量子纠错码的逻辑-物理映射与编译框架，通过优化映射策略和编译技术显著提升量子电路性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率量子纠错码（k>1）虽然能提高量子比特利用率，但降低了容错能力，因为任意物理比特的错误都可能影响所有逻辑比特。现有研究未解决如何将程序量子比特最优映射到这些码的逻辑比特上以最大化电路保真度的问题。

Method: 提出双管齐下的框架：(1) 对于给定映射，执行逻辑到物理编译，利用Hadamard交换律和门合并技术高效编码输入门；(2) 通过合并优化、噪声偏置的打包启发式算法快速搜索可能的映射空间，无需穷举枚举即可识别高性能量子比特分配方案。

Result: 在71个基准电路上，电路深度减少34%，单量子比特门和双量子比特门数量分别最多减少31%和17%，总变分距离提升1.75倍，逻辑选择率相对于朴素编译平均提升86%。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个探索高码率量子纠错码映射与编译的研究，提出的框架能有效解决高码率码的逻辑-物理映射问题，显著提升量子电路性能，为有限量子比特资源下的量子计算提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in quantum computing have enabled practical use of quantum error detecting and correcting codes. However, current architectures and future proposals of quantum computer design suffer from limited qubit counts, necessitating the use of high-rate codes. Such codes, with their code parameters denoted as $[[n, k, d]]$, have more than $1$ logical qubit per code (i.e., $k > 1$). This leads to reduced error tolerance of the code, since $\lceil (d-1)/2\rceil$ errors on any of the $n$ physical qubits can affect the logical state of all $k$ logical qubits. Therefore, it becomes critical to optimally map the input qubits of a quantum circuit to these codes, in such a way that the circuit fidelity is maximized. \par However, the problem of mapping program qubits to logical qubits for high-rate codes has not been studied in prior work. A brute force search to find the optimal mapping is super exponential (scaling as $O(n!)$, where $n$ is the number of input qubits), making exhaustive search infeasible past a small number of qubits. We propose a framework that addresses this problem on two fronts: (1) for any given mapping, it performs logical-to-physical compilation that translates input gates into efficiently encoded implementations utilizing Hadamard commutation and gate merging; and (2) it quickly searches the space of possible mappings through a merge-optimizing, noise-biased packing heuristic that identifies high-performing qubit assignments without exhaustive enumeration. To the best of our knowledge, our compiler is the first work to explore mapping and compilation for high-rate codes. Across 71 benchmark circuits, we reduce circuit depth by $34\%$, gate counts by up to $31\%$ and $17\%$ for one-qubit and two-qubit gates, and improve total variation distance by $1.75\times$, with logical selection rate improvements averaging $86\%$ relative to naive compilation.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum metrological advantage of high-order squeezed states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09958)
*Rubén Gordillo-Hachuel,Erik Torrontegui,Cristina de Dios,Ricardo Puebla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高阶压缩态（m阶相位压缩态和多压缩态）在量子计量学中的潜在优势，相比传统高斯压缩真空态，这些非高斯态在相同粒子数条件下能提供显著的计量精度提升，但需要更高阶的测量可观测量，且对退相干通道的鲁棒性不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联可用于提高参数估计精度超越经典极限。在标准干涉或旋转协议下，已知最优单模高斯态是标准压缩真空态，可实现海森堡极限精度。本研究旨在探索高阶压缩态（m阶相位压缩态和多压缩态）在量子计量中的潜在优势，超越高斯态的限制。

Method: 研究了两类高阶压缩态家族：m阶相位压缩态和多压缩态。在标准干涉或旋转协议下，分析这些非高斯态在相同粒子数条件下的计量性能。研究了高阶压缩阶数对优势的影响，以及实现完全计量性能所需的高阶测量可观测量。最后分析了这些态在标准退相干通道（纯退相位和零温度阻尼）下的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 高阶压缩态相比最优压缩真空态在相同粒子数条件下能提供显著的计量优势；2. 优势在低和高粒子数区域均存在，但具体行为取决于所选的高阶压缩态家族；3. 更高阶的压缩能增强优势，但需要更高阶的测量可观测量才能实现完全计量性能；4. 相对于标准压缩态参考态，这些态对阻尼噪声表现出合理的鲁棒性（在一定噪声强度内），但对纯退相位噪声的计量优势变得脆弱。

Conclusion: 高阶压缩态在量子计量学中具有超越高斯态的潜在增强能力，但面临主要挑战和限制：需要更高阶的测量可观测量，且对不同类型的退相干通道表现出不同的鲁棒性。研究为超越高斯态的量子计量学发展提供了详细分析。

Abstract: Quantum correlations can be harnessed to improve the precision in parameter estimation beyond classical capabilities. Under a standard interferometric or rotation protocol, it is well established that the optimal single-mode Gaussian state is a standard squeezed vacuum, which enables Heisenberg limited precision. In this work, we investigate the potential metrological advantage of two distinct families involving high-order squeezing, namely, mth-phase and multisqueezed states. Our results show that these non-Gaussian states can grant a significant metrological advantage with respect to the optimal squeezed vacuum under equivalent conditions, i.e. at equal occupations. Their advantage holds both at low and large occupations, but its behavior critically depends on the chosen family of high-order squeezing. While higher squeezing orders enhance the advantage, this comes at the cost of higher-order observables in the measurement for full metrological performance. Finally, we study their robustness to standard decoherence channels, i.e. pure dephasing and zero-temperature damping. Employing standard squeezing as reference state, our results indicate a reasonable robustness against damping up to a certain noise strength, while their metrological advantage becomes fragile under pure dephasing. Our work shows the potential enhancement in quantum metrology beyond Gaussian states, carefully detailing the main challenges and limitations.

</details>


### [109] [Finite-temperature quantum Krylov method from real-time overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10543)
*Hiroto Yamamoto,Katsuhiro Morita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于实时重叠序列gn=⟨φ|e^{-inτH}|φ⟩的有限温度量子多体系统评估框架，无需在量子设备上指定目标温度即可获得宽温度范围内的热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确评估量子多体系统的有限温度性质是一个核心挑战。现有量子方法通常需要在每个目标温度下制备热态，使得低温计算在电路深度和精度方面特别困难。

Method: 引入基于实时重叠序列gn=⟨φ|e^{-inτH}|φ⟩的框架，通过该序列可在宽温度范围内获得热力学量，无需在量子设备上指定目标温度。对于一维自旋-1/2海森堡模型，采用周期性边界条件，并利用与S_tot^z守恒兼容的伪随机向量来评估磁化率，无需显式对称性分解。

Result: 在无噪声情况下，准确获得了比热、磁化率和熵。通过适当的稳定化处理，在有限测量统计误差（σ~10^-3）下，该方法仍能保持主要热力学特征。

Conclusion: 基于实时重叠的有限温度评估框架为近未来量子硬件上的有限温度计算提供了一个有前景的方法。

Abstract: Accurately evaluating finite-temperature properties of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge. Many existing quantum approaches typically require thermal-state preparation at each target temperature, making low-temperature calculations especially demanding in terms of circuit depth and accuracy. Here we introduce a distinct framework based only on the real-time overlap sequence $g_n=\langle φ|e^{-inτH}|φ\rangle$, which enables thermodynamic quantities to be obtained over a broad temperature range, without specifying a target temperature on the quantum device. For the one-dimensional spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Heisenberg model with periodic boundary conditions, we obtain accurate specific heat, magnetic susceptibility, and entropy in the noiseless case. Magnetic susceptibility is also evaluated accurately without explicit symmetry-sector decomposition by employing pseudorandom vectors compatible with $S_{\mathrm{tot}}^{z}$ conservation. With suitable stabilization, the method further retains the main thermodynamic features under finite-shot statistical errors up to $σ\sim10^{-3}$. Our results establish real-time-overlap-based finite-temperature evaluation as a promising framework for finite-temperature computation on near-future quantum hardware.

</details>


### [110] [A parallel and distributed fixed-point quantum search algorithm for solving SAT problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09980)
*He Wang,Jinyang Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并行定点搜索算法解决SAT问题，利用量子纠缠并行处理CNF公式中的子句，显著减少电路深度，适合NISQ时代


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法解决SAT问题需要O(√2^n)查询，且存在Souffle问题：当解的数量未知时，算法过早或过晚终止会显著降低获得解的概率

Method: 提出并行定点搜索算法，利用量子纠缠使CNF公式中的每个子句可以独立处理，从而显著减少电路深度，并讨论了分布式实现方法

Result: 该算法特别适合噪声中等规模量子计算时代，通过减少电路深度和分布式处理来应对当前量子硬件的限制

Conclusion: PFP算法为SAT问题提供了更适合NISQ时代的量子解决方案，通过并行处理和电路深度优化克服了Grover算法的局限性

Abstract: Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problem is of fundamental importance in computer science and many application domains. For Grover's algorithm, solving the SAT problem requires $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{2^n})$ queries--where n denotes the number of logic variables in the problem. However, Grover's algorithm suffers from the Souffle problem: specifically, when the number of solutions is unknown, terminating the algorithm too early or too late leads to a significant reduction in the probability of obtaining a solution. In this paper, we propose a parallel fixed-point (PFP) search algorithm to solve the SAT problem. By exploiting entanglement, each clause in the conjunctive normal form (CNF) formula can be processed independently, leading to a significant reduction in circuit depth. We also discuss how to perform the algorithm in distributed manner. These make the PFPS algorithm particularly suitable for the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Lattice Boltzmann with Denoising Collision Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09997)
*Trong Duong,Matthias Möller,Norbert Hosters*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于去噪型碰撞算子的量子晶格玻尔兹曼方法，通过将碰撞动力学重构为围绕参考状态的线性化平衡分布流形上的正交投影，避免了传统量子LBM中基于层析成像的更新过程，实现了无需后选择的全相干多时间步模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶格玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）在经典硬件上具有高度并行性，但其量子算法实现受到碰撞过程（本质上是非线性和不可逆的）的阻碍。现有的量子LBM方法通过在每个时间步重复进行量子层析成像和状态准备来实现碰撞过程，导致显著的开销。

Method: 提出了一种基于去噪型碰撞算子的量子LBM，将碰撞动力学重构为围绕参考状态的线性化平衡分布流形上的正交投影。该方法过滤非平衡分量，同时保持晶格对称性并近似非线性项以恢复流体动力学行为。提供了完整的量子电路实现流程，包括分布编码、碰撞、流传输、边界条件以及物理量（如流体动力）的测量。还提出了一种无需后选择的确定性投影算子实现方法。

Result: 数值实验针对对流-扩散和流动问题进行，结果表明该方法能够高精度地再现宏观行为，性能取决于参考状态的选择。该方法避免了基于层析成像的更新，为全相干多时间步LBM模拟铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种创新的量子晶格玻尔兹曼方法，通过几何投影方法替代传统的非线性碰撞处理，显著降低了量子实现的复杂度。该方法为高效量子流体动力学模拟提供了新途径，特别是在避免层析成像开销和实现全相干多时间步模拟方面取得了重要进展。

Abstract: The Lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) is a well-established mesoscopic approach for simulating fluid dynamics by evolving particle distribution functions on discrete lattices. While the LBM is highly parallelizable on classical hardware, its translation to quantum algorithms is impeded by the collision process, which is intrinsically nonlinear and irreversible. Several existing quantum formulations implement this process through repeated quantum tomography and state preparation at every timestep, leading to significant overheads. We introduce a quantum LBM based on a denoising-type collision operator that avoids tomography-based updates. The collision dynamics are reformulated as an orthogonal projection onto the linearized manifold of equilibrium distributions around a reference state. This geometric approach filters non-equilibrium components while preserving lattice symmetries and approximating nonlinear terms needed to recover hydrodynamic behavior. A complete pipeline is presented with efficient gate-level realizations, incorporating encoding of distributions, collision, streaming, boundary conditions, and measurement of physical quantities such as hydrodynamic forces. In addition, we outline an approach for implementing projector-based operators deterministically without postselection, paving the way to fully coherent multi-timestep LBM simulations. Numerical experiments for advection-diffusion and flow problems demonstrate that the method reproduces macroscopic behaviors with high accuracy, with performance depending on the choice of reference state.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Error Mitigation Strategies for Variational PDE-Constrained Circuits on Noisy Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10099)
*Prasad Nimantha Madusanka Ukwatta Hewage,Midhun Chakkravarthy,Ruvan Kumara Abeysekara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了三种噪声通道（退极化、振幅阻尼、比特翻转）对求解偏微分方程的变分量子电路的破坏性影响，并评估了三种误差缓解策略的有效性，揭示了物理约束电路固有的噪声弹性特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期量子硬件上，硬件噪声会严重降低变分量子电路求解偏微分方程的解保真度并破坏收敛性，因此需要系统研究噪声影响并评估误差缓解策略的实际效果。

Method: 采用三种噪声通道模型（退极化、振幅阻尼、比特翻转）对求解热方程、Burgers方程和Saint-Venant浅水方程的变分量子电路进行系统研究，并基准测试三种误差缓解策略：通过Richardson多项式拟合的零噪声外推、概率误差消除和基于逆混淆矩阵的测量误差缓解。

Result: 在6量子比特、4层电路上的数值实验表明：ZNE在低噪声（p=0.001）下能将绝对误差降低82-96%；物理约束电路展现出固有噪声弹性，在p=0.01时比无约束电路保持25-47%更高的保真度；PEC在低门数时提供近乎精确的校正，但在p≥0.02时超过约60个门后采样开销呈指数增长变得不实用。

Conclusion: 物理约束电路通过结构化梯度信息吸收噪声，系统误差在所有噪声水平下占主导地位（43-58%），而PDE残差分量随噪声增加从约10%增长到约31%。这些结果为在NISQ硬件上部署变分PDE求解器提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits (VQCs) solving partial differential equations (PDEs) on near-term quantum hardware face a critical challenge: hardware noise degrades solution fidelity and disrupts convergence. We present a systematic study of three noise channels; depolarizing, amplitude damping, and bit-flip on VQCs constrained by PDE residual loss functions for the heat equation, Burgers' equation, and the Saint-Venant shallow water equations. We benchmark three error mitigation strategies: zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE) via Richardson polynomial fitting, probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), and measurement error mitigation through inverse confusion matrices. Our numerical experiments on 6-qubit, 4-layer circuits demonstrate that ZNE reduces absolute error by 82-96% at low noise (p = 0.001), with effectiveness degrading gracefully at higher noise strengths. We prove analytically and confirm numerically that physics-constrained circuits exhibit inherent noise resilience: at p = 0.01, constrained circuits maintain 25-47% higher fidelity than unconstrained counterparts, with the advantage scaling with PDE complexity. PEC provides near-exact correction at low gate counts but incurs exponential sampling overhead, rendering it impractical beyond ~60 gates at p >= 0.02. Error budget decomposition reveals that systematic errors dominate at all noise levels (43-58%), while the PDE residual component grows from ~10% to ~31% as noise increases, indicating that physics constraints absorb noise through structured gradient information. These results establish practical guidelines for deploying variational PDE solvers on NISQ hardware.

</details>


### [113] [Correlated decoherence in a common environment activated by relative motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10109)
*Yang Wang,Zhilei Sun,Feiyi Liu,Min Guo,Yuhan Jiang,Mingyang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究两个空间分离的边界子系统在相对运动下通过结构化环境耦合的动力学，发现相对运动通过多普勒频移打开相关退相干通道，存在运动学阈值v>2u_φ，阈值上下分别对应相干介质作用和不可逆相关退相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相对运动如何影响空间分离量子系统通过共同环境耦合的动力学，特别是运动如何改变环境介导的相干耦合和相关退相干过程。

Method: 采用高斯开放系统框架，通过积分掉环境自由度得到由边界位置处修饰环境关联函数控制的影响泛函，分析相对运动引起的多普勒频移效应。

Result: 发现运动学阈值v>2u_φ：阈值以下环境主要作为相干介质；阈值以上打开共振壳层，环境支持有限交叉噪声通道，产生不可逆相关退相干。相干耦合由修饰关联函数的推迟分量控制，退相干率由其Hadamard分量控制。

Conclusion: 建立了运动诱导激发产生与开放量子系统中相关退相干之间的直接联系，指出在超导-声子平台中可通过过量相关退相获得实验可观测特征。

Abstract: We study two spatially separated boundary subsystems coupled to a common structured environment under relative motion in a Gaussian open-system framework. By integrating out the environment, we obtain an influence functional governed by a dressed environmental correlator evaluated at the boundary positions, which encodes both coherent mediation and correlated fluctuations. Relative motion opens a correlated decoherence channel through Doppler-shifted spectral overlap of the boundary excitations, leading to a kinematic threshold at $v>2u_φ$. Below threshold, the dominant resonant contribution to the off-diagonal noise kernel is absent and the environment acts predominantly as a coherent mediator at leading resonant order. Above threshold, a resonant shell opens and the same environment supports a finite cross-noise channel, producing irreversible correlated decoherence. In the reduced dynamics, coherent coupling is governed by the retarded component of the dressed correlator, while the decoherence rate is controlled by its Hadamard component. These results establish a direct connection between motion-induced excitation production and correlated decoherence in open quantum systems, and point to experimentally accessible signatures in superconducting--phononic platforms through excess correlated dephasing.

</details>


### [114] [An Information-Theoretic Bound on Thermodynamic Efficiency and the Generalized Carnot's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10762)
*Anna Gabetti,Fabrizio Dolcini,Davide Girolami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导出比卡诺极限更严格的热机效率上限，该界限依赖于热机内部状态与哈密顿量之间的统计关联，适用于经典和量子热机，并在有限时间循环中可达，为实际能量收集机器提供了设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡诺定理为热机效率设定了理论极限，但该极限通常只在准静态过程中可达。实际热机在有限时间内运行，效率往往低于卡诺极限。因此需要建立更严格、更实用的效率界限，为实际能量收集设备的设计提供指导。

Method: 作者推导了一个基于热机内部状态与哈密顿量之间统计关联的效率界限。该界限适用于多热浴情况，并证明即使在有限时间循环中也可达到。通过量子点耦合费米子热浴的引擎模型验证该界限的可达性。

Result: 1. 建立了比卡诺极限更严格的效率上限，该界限依赖于统计关联；2. 证明该界限在有限时间循环中可达；3. 展示了量子点-费米子热浴引擎能够达到该界限，即使在非准静态区域；4. 该界限适用于经典和量子热机。

Conclusion: 该研究为热机效率建立了更精确的理论界限，突破了传统卡诺极限的局限性，为设计实际高效能量收集机器提供了理论基础和设计原则，特别是在有限时间运行条件下。

Abstract: We derive a bound on the efficiency of thermal engines that can be sharper than Carnot's limit. It is a function of statistical correlations between the engine internal state and Hamiltonian, can be saturated even in finite-time cycles, and applies to both classical and quantum engines. Specifically, the bound establishes the exact maximal efficiency of engines operating with multiple baths, tightening the upper limit set by Carnot's theorem. Then, we show that an engine made of a quantum dot coupled with fermionic baths can achieve the bound, even when operating beyond the quasistatic regime. The result provides a design principle for realistic energy harvesting machines.

</details>


### [115] [Robust quantum metrology using disordered probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11635)
*Vishnupriya K.,Harikrishnan K. J.,Amit Kumar Pal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子探针在玻璃无序存在下的鲁棒性，定义了无序标记来量化无序对量子费希尔信息的影响，并推导了探针的内在鲁棒尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子器件中普遍存在无序，这会影响量子参数估计和传感中使用的量子探针的性能。研究量子探针对玻璃无序的鲁棒性对于实际量子传感应用至关重要。

Method: 通过将量子费希尔信息展开为无序分布标准化中心矩的不同阶次，定义无序标记来量化无序效应。对量子探针进行分类，分析无序敏感探针在参数上存在相同弱无序时的行为。推导探针的内在鲁棒尺度，并提出无需无序平均即可从无序自由探针哈密顿量估计最大可承受无序强度的方法。

Result: 无序标记的绝对值在无序强度上表现出二次依赖性。推导出与无序竞争的内在鲁棒尺度。在单量子比特探针（无序磁场）和多量子比特探针（无序一维Kitaev模型）中验证了理论结果。
