Conclusion: 该研究提供了量化量子探针对无序鲁棒性的系统框架，定义了无序标记和内在鲁棒尺度，为评估量子传感系统在实际无序环境中的性能提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Disorder is ubiquitous in quantum devices including quantum probes designed and fabricated for quantum parameter estimation and sensing. We investigate the robustness of a quantum probe against the presence of glassy disorder. We define a disorder marker quantifying the effect of the disorder by expanding the quantum Fisher information in terms of different orders of the standardized central moments of the disorder-distributions. We classify the quantum probes in terms of the possible values of the disorder marker, and analytically show, for a disorder-sensitive probe with identical and weak disorder on all or a subset of the parameters of the probe-Hamiltonian, that the absolute value of the disorder marker exhibits a quadratic dependence on the disorder strength. We derive a robustness scale intrinsic to the probe that competes with the disorder, and provide a prescription for estimating the maximum disorder strength that the probe can withstand from the disorder-free probe-Hamiltonian for a given initial state of the probe, which can be computed without the disorder averaging. We demonstrate our results in the case of a single-qubit probe under disordered magnetic field, and a multi-qubit probe described by a disordered one-dimensional Kitaev model with nearest-neighbor interactions.

</details>


### [116] [Unitary Designs from Two Chaotic Hamiltonians and a Random Pauli Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10122)
*Ning Sun,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 使用两个混沌哈密顿量和中间随机泡利操作，通过长时间演化实现幺正设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在量子系统中实现幺正设计的新方法，减少所需混沌哈密顿量的数量，利用量子混沌特性生成量子随机性

Method: 提出协议：两个不同的混沌哈密顿量长时间演化，中间插入随机泡利操作；基于混沌哈密顿量的普适泡利谱理论；数值验证使用高斯幺正系综哈密顿量和随机自旋模型

Result: 理论证明两个混沌哈密顿量加中间随机泡利操作足以生成幺正设计；数值验证支持理论预测；分析了有限时间和有限尺寸修正

Conclusion: 该工作为量子随机性的动力学生成提供了新见解，为在混沌系统中实现幺正设计提供了新途径，突破了至少需要三个混沌哈密顿量的限制

Abstract: The realization of unitary designs is of fundamental interest in quantum science and typically requires the ability to implement structured quantum circuits. Recent developments have explored the possibility of generating unitary designs using only a small number of quantum quenches, in which the evolution during each interval is governed by a static Hamiltonian. In particular, it has been established that at least three chaotic Hamiltonians are required when only Hamiltonian evolutions are employed. In this work, we propose the emergence of unitary designs in the temporal ensemble of qubit systems evolved under two distinct chaotic Hamiltonians for sufficiently long times, supplemented by an intermediate random Pauli operation inserted between them. This result follows from the universal Pauli spectrum of chaotic Hamiltonians, a central concept in the study of non-stabilizerness. Our theoretical predictions are verified numerically using explicit examples, including Gaussian unitary ensemble Hamiltonians and random spin models. We further investigate finite-time and finite-size corrections to the protocol. Our results provide new insights into the dynamical generation of quantum randomness and offer a new route toward realizing unitary designs in chaotic systems.

</details>


### [117] [Protecting Quantum Simulations of Lattice Gauge Theories through Engineered Emergent Hierarchical Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11085)
*Zhanpeng Fu,Wei Zheng,Roderich Moessner,Marin Bukov,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子模拟约束希尔伯特空间多体晶格模型的策略，通过Floquet工程框架实现近似动力学选择规则，保护U(1)规范理论免受局部对称性破坏，延长量子模拟寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算平台无法完美限制希尔伯特空间到目标规范扇区，规范理论中的局部约束破坏不可避免，这给模拟基础物理带来了巨大挑战。

Method: 开发Floquet工程框架，通过时间层次结构重组目标扇区的偏离，产生一系列可控的涌现局部对称性，形成近似动力学选择规则，强烈限制扇区间耦合。

Result: 数值验证了一维U(1)量子链模型理论，显示某些扇区保持极长寿命，其他扇区在更短时间尺度上失稳。揭示"缺陷"运动受动力学约束，仅通过扇区内动力学辅助才能移动。

Conclusion: 该方法可延长复杂多体问题量子模拟的寿命，当涌现或期望的局部对称性仅近似实现时，提供被动纠错机制。

Abstract: We present a strategy for the quantum simulation of many-body lattice models with constrained Hilbert spaces. We focus on lattice gauge theories (LGTs), which underlie a wide range of phenomena in particle physics, condensed matter, and quantum information. In present-day quantum computing platforms, perfect restrictions of the Hilbert space to the desired gauge sectors are beyond reach: for LGTs, violations of the local constraint are unavoidable, posing a formidable challenge for the emulation of the underlying physics. Here, we develop a Floquet-engineering framework that restructures departures from a target sector such that a series of emergent local symmetries occurs hierarchically in time and in a controllable way. This leads to a set of approximate dynamical selection rules that strongly restrict inter-sector couplings, resulting in a pronounced, symmetry-controlled hierarchy of lifetimes for the state population to spread among sectors. Concretely, this protects $U(1)$ LGTs against violations of the {defining} local symmetry. While some sectors remain very long-lived, others are destabilized on shorter timescales. We numerically verify our theory for the one-dimensional $U(1)$ quantum link model. In addition, we reveal that `defects', whose movement accounts for violations of the gauge constraint, are kinetically constrained, becoming mobile only through the assistance of intra-sector dynamics, which we describe using an effective quantum marble model. Our results can thus be used to extend the lifetime, in the spirit of passive error correction, of quantum simulations of complex many-body problems when emergent or desired local symmetries are only implemented approximately.

</details>


### [118] [Characterizing entanglement dynamics in QED scattering processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10136)
*Massimo Blasone,Silvio De Siena,Gaetano Lambiase,Bruno Micciola,Kyrylo Simonov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子电动力学散射过程中螺旋度自由度间的纠缠动力学，通过量子映射建模，发现仅含费米子的散射能保持初始最大纠缠，且映射的固定点通常为最大纠缠态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子电动力学散射过程中螺旋度自由度间的纠缠动力学特性，理解量子场论中纠缠的演化规律。

Method: 使用量子映射建模QED散射过程，将固定动量散射视为广义测量（POVM），分析量子映射的谱结构及其对纠缠动力学的影响。

Result: 仅含费米子的散射过程能保持初始最大纠缠；量子映射在任意初始态迭代后的固定点通常为纯的最大纠缠态；映射结构也能描述含光子的散射纠缠动力学。

Conclusion: QED散射中的纠缠动力学由量子映射的谱结构决定，这些映射的特性源于QED相互作用的离散对称性，为理解量子场论中的纠缠演化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study entanglement dynamics among helicity degrees of freedom in quantum electrodynamics (QED) scattering processes. For generic initial states, we consider scattering at fixed momentum, corresponding to a generalized measurement described by a positive operator-valued measure, resulting in a post-measurement state. Such processes are modeled in terms of quantum maps, whose spectral structure fully determines the associated entanglement dynamics. For scattering involving fermions only, maximal entanglement present in the initial state is always preserved. Moreover, iterating the corresponding quantum maps on arbitrary initial states, we obtain the fixed points of the maps, which, in the largest number of cases, are asymptotic (pure) maximally entangled states. The structure of the maps also accounts for the entanglement dynamics in processes involving both fermions and photons. The defining properties of these maps originate from discrete symmetries of the QED interaction.

</details>


### [119] [Encrypted clones can leak: Classification of informative subsets in Quantum Encrypted Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10155)
*Gabriele Gianini,Omar Hasan,Corrrado Mio,Stelvio Cimato,Ernesto Damiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 加密克隆协议允许未知量子比特的冗余存储，但仅能通过消耗密钥的解密恢复一个克隆副本，符合不可克隆定理。本文评估了该协议的信息泄露模式，揭示了其结构性的保密限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密克隆协议中的加密旨在实现与不可克隆定理兼容的冗余存储，而非专门设计用于保证机密性。因此需要明确评估其保密特性，分析存储寄存器中不同子集的信息泄露情况。

Method: 将加密克隆存储寄存器中的子集分类为授权集、完全无信息集和部分信息集。分析非授权子集对输入状态的残留依赖性，精确表征这种依赖性的发生条件。

Result: 发现中间非授权子集可能仅保留对输入状态的受限残留依赖，这种泄露模式具有奇偶依赖性，揭示了加密克隆协议的结构性保密限制。

Conclusion: 加密克隆协议虽然符合不可克隆定理，但其保密特性存在结构性限制，非授权子集可能泄露部分信息，且泄露模式与奇偶性相关。这为量子信息存储的安全性评估提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Encrypted cloning enables the redundant storage of an unknown qubit while remaining compatible with the no-cloning theorem, since only one clone can later be recovered through key-consuming decryption. Because encryption in this protocol is introduced to enable cloning-compatible redundancy rather than to guarantee confidentiality by design, its secrecy properties must be assessed explicitly. Here we classify the subsets of the encrypted-clone storage register into authorized, completely non-informative, and partially informative sets. We show that intermediate non-authorized subsets may retain only a restricted residual dependence on the input state, and we characterize exactly when this dependence occurs. The resulting leakage pattern is parity-dependent, revealing a structural confidentiality limitation of encrypted cloning.

</details>


### [120] [Noise-Induced Resurrection of Dynamical Skin Effects in Quasiperiodic Non-Hermitian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11455)
*Wuping Yang,H. Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 噪声恢复准周期非厄米系统中的动力学趋肤效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期势场中非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）的抑制机制，探索噪声如何影响非厄米系统中的输运行为。传统认知中强准周期势会通过局域化抑制动力学趋肤效应（DSE），但本文发现噪声能够意外地恢复这种效应。

Method: 采用微扰分析，将非厄米薛定谔动力学映射到非互易主方程，研究Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声对系统的影响。分析噪声诱导点隙的形成机制及其对局域化和输运行为的影响。

Result: 噪声有效地将系统映射到具有复谱的非互易主方程，产生噪声诱导的点隙。这一机制使系统退局域化，恢复定向输运，并在静态NHSE不存在的区域复活DSE。弛豫动力学表现出对噪声强度的非单调依赖性，反映了噪声辅助退局域化与噪声诱导退相干之间的竞争。

Conclusion: 噪声能够通过诱导点隙机制恢复准周期非厄米系统中的动力学趋肤效应，为控制开放量子系统中的输运行为提供了新途径。这一发现揭示了噪声在非厄米系统中的积极作用，挑战了传统认知中噪声仅起破坏作用的观点。

Abstract: The non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE) refers to the accumulation of an extensive number of eigenstates at system boundaries under open boundary conditions (OBCs). As a dynamical consequence, wave packets in such systems drift and ultimately accumulate at a boundary, giving rise to the dynamical skin effect (DSE). While strong quasiperiodic potentials are known to suppress the DSE by inducing localization, we show that the introduction of Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) noise unexpectedly restores it. Using perturbative analysis, we demonstrate that noise effectively maps the non-Hermitian Schrödinger dynamics onto a non-reciprocal master equation, whose complex spectrum develops a noise-induced point gap. This mechanism enables delocalization, reinstates directional transport, and revives the DSE even in regimes where the static NHSE is absent. Moreover, the relaxation dynamics exhibit a non-monotonic dependence on noise strength, reflecting a competition between noise-assisted delocalization and noise-induced decoherence. Our results uncover a noise-enabled mechanism for resurrecting the DSE and suggest a new route for controlling transport in quasiperiodic, open quantum systems.

</details>


### [121] [Interference-Protected Subradiance and Bound States in Nested Atomic Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10197)
*Bella Santosa,Daniel Leykam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于闵可夫斯基和构造的确定性方法，用于工程化原子阵列，产生具有内置相关性的准无序结构，实现模式选择性辐射耦合，增强亚辐射态的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波导量子电动力学中的集体亚辐射态对无序高度敏感，这限制了其可扩展性和鲁棒性。需要开发能够抵抗无序影响的工程化原子阵列结构。

Method: 采用基于闵可夫斯基和构造的确定性方法，生成具有内置相关性的准无序原子阵列结构。这种方法能够实现模式选择性辐射耦合：暗模式之间的相互作用被参数化抑制，而亮模式可以杂化。

Result: 研究了这些亚辐射态和类束缚态模式对中等位置无序的稳定性。结果表明，通过工程化的准无序结构，可以实现鲁棒且可解析控制的亚辐射现象。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条通过工程化准无序实现鲁棒、可解析控制亚辐射的途径，对原子-波导和电路量子电动力学实验具有直接相关性。

Abstract: Collective subradiant states in waveguide QED are highly sensitive to disorder, limiting their scalability and robustness. We propose a deterministic approach to engineering atom arrays based on a Minkowski sum construction, generating quasi-disordered structures with built-in correlations. This leads to mode-selective radiative coupling: interactions between dark modes are parametrically suppressed, while bright modes can hybridize. We study the stability of these subradiant and bound-state-like modes against moderate positional disorder. Our work provides a route to robust, analytically controllable subradiance through engineered quasi-disorder, with direct relevance to atom-waveguide and circuit QED experiments.

</details>


### [122] [Model-Free Quantum Stabilization via Finite-Difference Lyapunov Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10255)
*Robert Vrabel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模型的量子态稳定控制框架，仅使用测量导出的Lyapunov可观测量进行有限差分评估，无需系统动力学先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制方法需要精确的系统动力学模型（哈密顿量、耗散结构等），这在实验环境中往往难以获得或存在不确定性。需要开发一种仅依赖测量数据、无需先验模型知识的鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 结合三个关键要素：1) 基于符号的Lyapunov下降法；2) 自适应增益放大；3) 有限差分版本的LaSalle不变性原理。控制器仅使用离散测量数据，无需导数计算。

Result: 在无漂移情况下，该方法保证沿采样时刻的渐近稳定性；在存在未知漂移和噪声时，保证实用的输入到状态稳定性(ISS)。控制律简单、无导数、实验可行。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限维量子系统提供了一种完全模型无关的稳定控制方法，为随机、子空间和多量子比特的模型无关量子控制奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a model-free framework for stabilizing quantum states using only empirical finite-difference evaluations of a measurement-derived Lyapunov observable. The controller requires no knowledge of the Hamiltonian, dissipative structure, or generator of the dynamics, and relies solely on discrete measurement data. The approach combines three key elements: sign-based Lyapunov descent, adaptive gain amplification, and a finite-difference analogue of LaSalle's invariance principle. We provide rigorous conditions under which these mechanisms guarantee asymptotic stabilization along the sampling instants in the drift-free case and practical input-to-state stability (ISS) in the presence of unknown drift and noise. The resulting feedback law is simple, derivative-free, and experimentally feasible. A qubit example illustrates the complete closed-loop scheme and the predicted ISS-type behavior. Although demonstrated on a single qubit, the theory applies to arbitrary finite-dimensional quantum systems and offers a foundation for further developments in stochastic, subspace, and multi-qudit model-free quantum control.

</details>


### [123] [Comparing quantum and classical finite state generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10315)
*Prasenjit Deb,Almut Beige,Lewis A. Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 经典随机有限状态生成器在时序测量中可超越Tsirelson界限，而量子机器在时间延迟下能保持更长的相关性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell-CHSH不等式在空间量子关联基准测试中很成功，但本文表明它们通常不足以用于时序量子关联的基准测试。需要开发区分经典和量子时序过程的方法。

Method: 参数化基于单比特的经典随机有限状态生成器和基于单量子比特的量子随机有限状态生成器，比较它们输出序列的时序相关性，使用Bell-CHSH类不等式进行分析，考虑两个观察者Alice和Bob的顺序测量，以及测量间的时间延迟。

Result: 对于顺序测量，经典机器可以超越Tsirelson界限2√2；在时间延迟情况下，量子机器通过维持更长的相关性而优于经典对应物，特别是在一般扰乱操作下。

Conclusion: Bell-CHSH类不等式不足以基准测试时序量子关联；经典机器可超越Tsirelson界限；量子机器在时间延迟下保持相关性更优；该结果可用于区分量子与经典过程，并识别量子技术应用的新资源。

Abstract: Bell-CHSH-like inequalities have been very successful in benchmarking {\it spatial} quantum correlations. However, as this paper illustrates, they are in general not sufficient for benchmarking {\it temporal} quantum correlations. To show this, we parametrise classical and quantum stochastic finite state generators based on a single bit and a single qubit, respectively, and compare the temporal correlations of their output sequences using a Bell-CHSH-like inequality. We find that for sequential measurements by two observers, Alice and Bob, classical machines can exceed the Tsirelson bound of $2\sqrt{2}$, due to their fundamental structure. However, when we consider a time delay between consecutive measurements, we find examples where the quantum machines outperform their classical counterparts by maintaining correlations longer under generally scrambling operations. Our result can be used to distinguish quantum from classical processes and to identify novel resources for quantum technology applications.

</details>


### [124] [Complementary Quantum Time Distributions from a Single Operational Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10339)
*Mathieu Beau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于自由演化和投影测量的单操作协议通过不同后处理程序产生不等价的量子时间分布，构建了基于流动的活动时间分布和基于存在的量子频闪分布，为隧道效应中的Hartman效应提供了操作解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子系统中时间分布的不同操作定义，特别是针对隧道效应中的Hartman效应，提供基于流动和存在的互补时间概念，以操作性地解释量子时间分布机制。

Method: 采用基于自由演化和投影测量的单操作协议，通过不同后处理程序构建两种量子时间分布：基于流动的活动时间分布和基于存在的量子频闪分布，应用于隧道效应分析。

Result: 区域量子频闪均值饱和，而流动时间均值在Hartman区域先减小后随势垒宽度增加而增长；框架内提供了Hartman效应的操作解释，捕捉早期穿透、主导反射和频谱滤波传输机制。

Conclusion: 通过单一操作协议的不同后处理可以产生不等价的量子时间分布，基于流动和存在的互补时间概念为隧道效应中的Hartman效应提供了操作解释，揭示了量子时间分布的机制。

Abstract: A single operational protocol based on free evolution and projective measurements yields inequivalent quantum time distributions through distinct post-processing procedures. We construct an activity-based time-of-flow (TF) distribution and a presence-based quantum stroboscopic (QS) distribution, providing complementary operational notions of time. Applied to tunneling, the regional QS mean saturates, whereas the TF mean first decreases in the Hartman regime and then grows for larger barrier widths. Within this framework, we provide an operational interpretation of the Hartman effect in terms of quantum time distributions associated with flow through the exit region and occupation within the barrier, capturing the mechanism of early penetration, dominant reflection, and spectrally filtered transmission.

</details>


### [125] [A Detector-Based Inference Framework for Quantum Theory and Spacetime Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10349)
*Marcello Rotondo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子理论与时空几何在统一的推断框架中涌现，通过探测器模型将量子振幅与测量事件关联，从探测器状态空间的信息几何重构洛伦兹时空度规，并推导出爱因斯坦场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子理论与时空几何的统一描述框架，从信息推断的角度解释量子理论和广义相对论的基本结构，为量子引力提供操作性的基础。

Method: 采用基于探测器的框架，通过探测器状态和核函数为测量事件分配振幅；使用具有相位结构的高斯探测器模型，从探测器状态空间的信息几何（量子几何张量）重构时空度规；构建结合探测器变形代价和几何项的有效一致性泛函。

Result: 从耦合的位置和时间探测器扇区重构出洛伦兹时空度规，振幅和相位变形共同贡献几何；标量曲率获得操作性解释为可区分结果的局部赤字；一致性泛函的平稳配置产生爱因斯坦方程，探测器变形产生应力-能量张量。

Conclusion: 量子理论和时空几何可在统一的推断结构中涌现，探测器变形提供物质的操作性概念，真空配置不必平坦，局部变形在标量扇区恢复标准场论行为，为量子引力提供了新的基础框架。

Abstract: We develop a detector-based framework in which quantum theory and spacetime geometry arise within a common inferential structure. Detector states and a detector kernel assign amplitudes to measurement events, allowing quantum theory to be interpreted as weighting hypothetical configurations consistent with observed detector clicks.
  Using a Gaussian detector model with phase structure, we show that distinguishability induces an information geometry on detector-state space, described by the quantum geometric tensor. A Lorentzian spacetime metric is reconstructed from coupled position and time detector sectors, with both amplitude and phase deformations contributing to geometry. Scalar curvature acquires an operational interpretation as a local deficit of distinguishable outcomes.
  We construct an effective consistency functional combining detector-deformation cost with a geometric term selected by locality and diffeomorphism invariance. Its stationary configurations yield the Einstein equation, with a stress-energy tensor arising from detector deformations. Vacuum configurations need not be flat, while local deformations provide an operational notion of matter and recover standard field-theoretic behavior in the scalar sector.

</details>


### [126] [First-principles study of dispersive readout in circuit QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11722)
*Angela Riva,Prakritish Gogoi,Nicolas Gheeraert,Serge Florens,Alex W. Chin,Alain Sarlette,Alexandru Petrescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理模拟研究了超导量子比特色散读取中驱动幅度对保真度和T1时间的影响，揭示了传统Lindblad主方程在描述该现象时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特色散读取实验中，增加测量驱动幅度本应提高读取速度和保真度，但实验观察到超过某个驱动幅度后保真度会饱和或下降，且常伴随量子比特能量弛豫时间T1的减少。传统的Lindblad主方程无法捕捉这一效应，而现有的有效主方程方法依赖于对系统和环境谱的强假设，仅与部分观测结果一致。

Method: 采用第一性原理模拟方法，考虑电路QED哈密顿量与测量传输线的微观模型耦合，允许环境具有任意谱（包括滤波器）。通过模拟完整的幺正动力学，研究系统发射谱随驱动功率的变化，并分析量子比特T1时间对读取驱动幅度的依赖关系。

Result: 研究发现量子比特T1时间对读取驱动幅度的依赖关系对环境谱的细节非常敏感。特别地，当在量子比特频率处放置Purcell陷波滤波器时，T1时间会随驱动幅度增加而下降。与第一性原理模型相比，Lindblad主方程显示出普遍的定性缺陷。

Conclusion: 该工作通过第一性原理模拟揭示了超导量子比特色散读取中驱动功率依赖效应的微观机制，强调了环境谱细节对量子比特弛豫行为的重要影响，并指出了传统Lindblad主方程在描述此类现象时的局限性。

Abstract: The speed and fidelity of dispersive readout of superconducting qubits should improve by increasing the amplitude of the measurement drive. Experiments show, however, that beyond some drive amplitude there is always a saturation or drop in fidelity, often associated with a decrease in qubit energy relaxation time $T_1$. A simple Lindblad master equation does not capture the latter effect. More involved approaches based on effective master equations rely on strong assumptions about the spectra of the system and the bath and only partially agree with observations. Here, we perform a first-principles simulation of the full unitary dynamics of dispersive readout by considering the circuit QED Hamiltonian coupled to a microscopic model for the measurement transmission line, allowing for its arbitrary spectrum, including filters. Our access to the dynamics of the bath degrees of freedom allows us to investigate the emission spectrum of the system as a function of drive power. We show how the dependence of qubit $T_1$ on readout drive amplitude is sensitive to the details of the bath spectrum. In particular, we find that $T_1$ drops with increasing drive amplitude when a Purcell notch filter is placed at the qubit frequency, and that the Lindblad master equation shows general qualitative defects compared to the first-principles model.

</details>


### [127] [An Undergraduate Course in Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10396)
*Peter Young*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 这是一门面向物理科学本科生、无需量子力学背景的量子计算课程文本，重点讲解Shor算法和量子纠错入门


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为物理科学专业的本科生提供系统的量子计算入门教育，填补传统课程中量子计算教学的空白，使学生能够掌握量子计算的核心概念和应用

Method: 采用渐进式教学法，从线性代数复习开始，逐步构建量子计算知识体系，最终聚焦于Shor算法和量子纠错这两个核心主题

Result: 开发了一套完整的量子计算课程材料，已在加州大学圣克鲁兹分校成功实施，适合一学期或一学季的本科教学

Conclusion: 该课程文本为物理科学本科生提供了有效的量子计算入门途径，通过精心设计的教学路径使学生能够理解Shor算法和量子纠错等高级主题

Abstract: This is the text for a one quarter or one semester undergraduate course on quantum computing that has been given at the University of California Santa Cruz. It is intended for students in the physical sciences who have already studied linear algebra (though a review of this topic is given in the course). No prior knowledge of quantum mechanics is required. The most important topics covered are Shor's algorithm and an introduction to quantum error correction. Most of the text is a build-up to these topics.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum Vacuum Radiation Near a Critical Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10406)
*Gabriele Orlando,Daniele Lamberto,Franco Nori,Salvatore Savasta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过非绝热调制哈密顿参数，将量子临界点附近的虚激发转化为实光子，实现量子真空辐射，临界点增强光子通量和辐射的非经典特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相变重塑光-物质系统的基态，但由此产生的量子关联（如压缩和纠缠）由于涉及虚激发而难以实验观测。研究如何通过非绝热调制将这些虚激发转化为实光子，从而探测量子临界基态。

Method: 通过非绝热调制哈密顿参数，将量子临界点附近的虚激发转化为实光子。开发了一个系统包含高阶过程的框架，即使在小调制幅度下也能处理相关效应。

Result: 临界点附近显著增强发射的光子通量和辐射的非经典特性，即使存在热涨落主导的情况。临界性可作为真空涨落的高效放大器，为探测和利用量子临界基态提供新途径。

Conclusion: 临界性能够放大真空涨落，将虚激发转化为可观测的量子真空辐射，为实验探测量子临界基态的量子关联提供了新方法，并开辟了利用量子临界性的新途径。

Abstract: Equilibrium quantum phase transitions profoundly reshape the ground state of light-matter systems, yet, the resulting quantum correlations, such as squeezing and entanglement, remain experimentally inaccessible since they involve virtual ground state excitations. We investigate how nonadiabatic modulation of a Hamiltonian parameter can convert these virtual excitations into real photons, enabling quantum vacuum radiation. We show that proximity to the critical point strongly enhances the emitted photon flux and the non-classical nature of the emitted radiation, even when thermal fluctuations are expected to dominate. In addition, higher-order processes become relevant even for small modulation amplitudes, and we develop a framework that systematically incorporates them. Our results reveal that criticality can act as an efficient amplifier of vacuum fluctuations, offering new routes to probe and exploit quantum critical ground states.

</details>


### [129] [Worst-case Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd algorithm with average-case correct quantum Fourier transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10428)
*Changpeng Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了Linden和de Wolf的量子傅里叶变换平均情况验证协议，证明了在三种不同场景下，Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd算法可以在仅假设QFT平均正确性的条件下实现可证明的良好最坏情况性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linden和de Wolf提出了验证量子傅里叶变换平均正确行为的轻量级协议，并证明了良好的平均情况QFT性能足以保证多个量子任务中的良好最坏情况性能。本文旨在探索该最坏情况到平均情况归约的另一个应用。

Method: 使用强化版的Linden-de Wolf协议，将最坏情况到平均情况的归约应用于Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd算法。通过分析三种不同场景，证明在仅假设QFT平均正确性的条件下，该算法仍能实现可证明的良好最坏情况性能。

Result: 在三种不同场景下，Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd算法可以在仅假设量子傅里叶变换平均正确性的条件下，实现可证明的良好最坏情况性能。这扩展了最坏情况到平均情况归约的应用范围。

Conclusion: 通过强化Linden-de Wolf协议，本文成功将最坏情况到平均情况的归约应用于Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd算法，证明了在仅要求QFT平均正确性的条件下，该算法仍能保证良好的最坏情况性能，为量子算法验证提供了新的理论支持。

Abstract: In [\href{https://quantum-journal.org/papers/q-2022-12-07-872/}{Quantum 6, 872, 2022}], Linden and de Wolf proposed a lightweight protocol for verifying the average-case correct behavior of the quantum Fourier transform (QFT). They proved that good average-case QFT performance suffices for good worst-case performance in several quantum tasks. Here we provide another application of this worst-case-to-average-case reduction, using a strengthened Linden-de Wolf protocol. We show that, across three distinct scenarios, the Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd algorithm can be executed with provably good worst-case performance, assuming only that the QFT is correct on average.

</details>


### [130] [The spontaneous disentanglement hypothesis and causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10562)
*Eyal Buks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于最大熵原理的自发解纠缠假设，通过拉格朗日乘子法确保与因果性一致，适用于有限维希尔伯特空间的量子系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学基础中的一些突出问题激发了自发解纠缠假设，但某些情况下该假设会违反因果性原理，需要调和这一矛盾

Method: 基于最大熵原理重新表述自发解纠缠假设，使用拉格朗日乘子法确保与因果性原理一致

Result: 提出了一个与因果性兼容的自发解纠缠假设表述，适用于任何有限维希尔伯特空间的量子系统

Conclusion: 通过最大熵原理和拉格朗日乘子法，成功调和了自发解纠缠假设与因果性原理的矛盾，为量子力学基础问题提供了新的理论框架

Abstract: The hypothesis that disentanglement spontaneously occurs in quantum systems is motivated by some outstanding issues in the foundations of quantum mechanics. However, for some cases, spontaneous disentanglement enables the violation of the causality principle. To mitigate the conflict with causality, a formulation for the hypothesis, which is based on the maximum entropy principle, is proposed. The method of Lagrange multipliers is implemented to ensure consistency with causality. The proposed formulation is applicable for any quantum system having a Hilbert space of finite dimensionality.

</details>


### [131] [Post-Cut Metadata Inference Attacks on Quantum Circuit Cutting Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10592)
*Samuel Punch,Krishnendu Guha,Utz Roedig*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子电路切割在执行过程中产生的元数据构成实际侧信道，攻击者仅凭片段宽度、深度和双量子比特门数等元数据就能高精度推断算法家族、切割机制和哈密顿量结构等信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路切割技术虽然扩展了量子设备的计算能力，但创建了新的机密性漏洞：半诚实云提供商可以观察到片段级执行记录。本文旨在形式化这一安全表面，并证明切割后执行记录构成实际的元数据侧信道。

Method: 提出结构化推理攻击，仅使用提供商可见的编译电路元数据（片段宽度、深度和双量子比特门计数）。在八个算法家族的1,200个电路片段上评估攻击效果，这些片段针对三种硬件拓扑进行转换，并在156量子比特生产量子计算机上验证执行时间不变性。

Result: 在严格实例不相交泛化下，攻击恢复算法家族的准确率达0.960（AUC 0.999），切割机制准确率0.847（AUC 0.924），哈密顿量k-局域性准确率0.960（AUC 0.998）。连接性和几何推断分别达到AUC 0.986和0.942，拓扑推断高于随机水平（AUC 0.666）。

Conclusion: 电路切割并非机密性中立的，元数据泄漏应被视为量子云系统中的首要安全关切。匹配足迹控制和消融研究证实泄漏是结构主导的，而非规模伪影所致。

Abstract: Quantum circuit cutting enables near-term quantum devices to execute workloads exceeding their qubit capacity by decomposing circuits into independently runnable fragments. While this extends computational reach, it creates a previously unexplored confidentiality surface: the fragment-level execution transcript observable by a semi-honest cloud provider. We formalise this surface and demonstrate that post-cut transcripts constitute a practical metadata side channel. Operating solely on provider-visible compiled circuit metadata (fragment width, depth, and two-qubit gate count), we evaluate a structured inference attack across six classification objectives spanning algorithm identity, cut mechanism, and coarse Hamiltonian structure. Our corpus comprises 1,200 circuit fragments across eight algorithm families transpiled against three hardware topologies, validated on a 156-qubit production quantum computer confirming that QPU execution time remains invariant across a 25x variation in compiled depth. Under strict instance-disjoint generalisation, our attack recovers algorithm family with 0.960 accuracy (AUC 0.999), cut mechanism with 0.847 accuracy (AUC 0.924), and Hamiltonian k-locality with 0.960 accuracy (AUC 0.998). Connectivity and geometry inference achieve AUC of 0.986 and 0.942 with strong stability under size-holdout. Topology inference remains above chance (AUC 0.666). A matched-footprint control and ablation study confirm leakage is structure-dominated and not explained by scale artefacts. These results demonstrate that circuit cutting is not confidentiality-neutral and that metadata leakage should be treated as a first-class security concern in quantum cloud systems.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive H-EFT-VA: A Provably Safe Trajectory Through the Trainability-Expressibility Landscape of Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10607)
*Eyad I. B. Hamid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: A-H-EFT通过自适应扩展Hilbert空间可达范围，在保持梯度方差Ω(1/poly(N))的同时解决了静态H-EFT-VA的参考态间隙问题，显著提升了变分量子算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态H-EFT-VA虽然通过分层EFT UV截断解决了Barren Plateau问题，但其定位机制将ansatz限制在多项式子空间，导致远离|0>^N的态存在参考态间隙，限制了算法的表达能力。

Method: 提出自适应H-EFT-VA（A-H-EFT），通过沿安全轨迹扩展可达Hilbert空间来导航可训练性与表达性之间的权衡。定理1证明当σ(t) ≤ 0.5/√(LN)时梯度方差保持在Ω(1/poly(N))，并通过安全扩展推论和单调增长引理确保无间断跳跃的扩展。

Result: 在16个实验中（最多N=14），A-H-EFT达到保真度F=0.54，是静态H-EFT-VA（F=0.27）的两倍，显著优于HEA（F~0.01），梯度方差始终≥0.5。对于Heisenberg XXZ模型（Δ_ref=1），A-H-EFT能识别负基态而静态方法失败，结果具有统计显著性（p < 10^-37）。

Conclusion: A-H-EFT在保持可训练性的同时显著扩展了表达范围，解决了静态方法的参考态间隙问题，是首个在VQA景观中具有严格界限的安全轨迹方法，具有超参数鲁棒性，无需搜索即可部署。

Abstract: H-EFT-VA established a physics-informed solution to the Barren Plateau (BP) problem via a hierarchical EFT UV-cutoff, guaranteeing gradient variance in Omega(1/poly(N)). However, localization restricts the ansatz to a polynomial subspace, creating a reference-state gap for states distant from |0>^N. We introduce Adaptive H-EFT-VA (A-H-EFT) to navigate the trainability-expressibility tradeoff by expanding the reachable Hilbert space along a safe trajectory. Gradient variance is maintained in Omega(1/poly(N)) if sigma(t) <= 0.5/sqrt(LN) (Theorem 1). A Safe Expansion Corollary and Monotone Growth Lemma confirm expansion without discontinuous jumps. Benchmarking across 16 experiments (up to N=14) shows A-H-EFT achieves fidelity F=0.54, doubling static H-EFT-VA (F=0.27) and outperforming HEA (F~0.01), with gradient variance >= 0.5 throughout. For Heisenberg XXZ (Delta_ref=1), A-H-EFT identifies the negative ground state while static methods fail. Results are statistically significant (p < 10^-37). Robustness over three decades of hyperparameters enables deployment without search. This is the first rigorously bounded trajectory through the VQA landscape.

</details>


### [133] [Role of Asymmetry in the Performance Optimization of a Relativistic Quantum Otto Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10651)
*Monika,Shishram Rebari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析了由含时谐振子驱动的相对论量子奥托循环，通过引入绝热过程的不对称性，研究了突然压缩和突然膨胀两种情景对相对论量子奥托发动机性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相对论量子奥托循环的性能，特别关注绝热过程中的不对称性如何影响发动机效率，探索在相对论量子系统中实现高效能量转换的可能性。

Method: 采用含时谐振子驱动的相对论量子奥托循环模型，通过引入绝热过程的不对称性，分析突然压缩和突然膨胀两种情景。使用Omega函数作为统一的性能度量指标，进行解析表征，并通过参数效率-功图、相图分析等方法研究系统性能。

Result: 突然压缩情况下效率趋近于1，而突然膨胀情况下效率限制在1/2。增加振子速度可同时提高功输出和效率。相图分析显示，随着振子速度增加，发动机运行区域扩大，而制冷区域相应缩小。

Conclusion: 相对论量子奥托循环的性能强烈依赖于绝热过程的不对称性，突然压缩能实现接近完美的效率，而突然膨胀则效率受限。通过优化振子速度可以同时提升功输出和效率，为设计高效的相对论量子热机提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We present an analytical study of the relativistic quantum Otto cycle driven by a time-dependent harmonic oscillator. By imposing an asymmetry on the two adiabatic processes of this cycle, we obtain distinct scenarios of sudden compression and sudden expansion, and analyze how asymmetry affects the performance of the relativistic quantum Otto engine. By leveraging the Omega function as a unified performance metric, we analytically characterize the efficiency in both scenarios. Our findings demonstrate that the efficiency approaches unity in the sudden compression case, while it is restricted to one-half for the sudden expansion case. Furthermore, we investigate the impact of increasing oscillator velocity on the extracted work and identify parameter regimes where either sudden compression or sudden expansion dominates. Additionally, we examine the optimal operating point using parametric efficiency-work plots, whose loop-shaped structure shows that increasing oscillator velocity enhances both work output and efficiency. Finally, through a detailed phase diagram analysis of the Otto cycle, we observe that the operational region corresponding to the engine mode expands with increasing oscillator velocity, while the refrigeration regime shrinks correspondingly.

</details>


### [134] [Symplectic perspective to quantum computing for Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10794)
*Efstratios Koukoutsis,Kyriakos Hizanidis,Lucas I Inigo Gamiz,Oscar Amaro,Christos Tsironis,Abhay K. Ram,George Vahala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个用于经典哈密顿系统的量子计算辛框架，利用幺正量子演化与辛相空间动力学之间的几何兼容性，实现指数级压缩的量子表示和潜在的量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子计算模拟经典哈密顿系统时，需要建立量子演化与经典动力学之间的几何对应关系，以实现高效的量子表示和计算加速。

Method: 1. 建立量子演化与Kähler流形上经典哈密顿流之间的精确对应，实现几何量子化方案；2. 对Liouville可积系统，通过作用-角变量和Koopman-von Neumann编码实现有限维幺正演化；3. 对不可积系统，采用Lie正则微扰理论构造近辛变换，将动力学映射为近似可积形式。

Result: 1. 识别出允许指数压缩量子表示的经典哈密顿系统族；2. 纠缠编码在系综规模上提供指数压缩，并通过振幅估计技术实现可观测量估计的量子加速；3. 推导出量子辛方案的量子计算复杂度，显示内存需求的指数压缩和相对于系统尺寸的潜在多项式加速。

Conclusion: 该辛框架为经典哈密顿系统的量子模拟提供了几何基础，实现了量子表示的指数压缩和计算加速，并推导了量子相空间可观测量的输运演化方程。

Abstract: This work develops a symplectic framework for quantum computing to be applied to classical Hamiltonian systems, exploiting the intrinsic geometric compatibility between unitary quantum evolution and symplectic phase-space dynamics in a two-fold way. The first part is devoted in establishing an exact correspondence between quantum evolution and classical Hamiltonian flow on a Kahler manifold. This correspondence enables a geometric quantization scheme that identifies a family of classical Hamiltonian systems admitting exponentially compressed quantum representations-appropriate for quantum simulation. In the second part we demonstrate that Liouville-integrable Hamiltonian dynamics induce finite-dimensional unitary evolution through action-angle variables and Koopman-von Neumann encoding. This allows efficient quantum representation and parallel evolution of large phase-space ensembles, where entangled encodings provide exponential compression in ensemble size and enable quantum speed-ups in observable estimation via amplitude estimation techniques. For non-integrable systems, Lie canonical perturbation theory is incorporated to construct near-symplectic transformations that map dynamics to approximately integrable forms, preserving unitary evolution up to a controlled error. We derive the resulting quantum computational complexity of the proposed quantum-symplectic scheme, revealing both an exponential compression in memory requirements and a potential polynomial speed-up with respect to the system size. Finally, the transport evolution equation governing the quantum phase-space observables is obtained.

</details>


### [135] [Effective schemes for fusion of hyperentangled W states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10880)
*Wen-Xiu Zhang,Wen-Qiang Liu,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种超纠缠W态的融合机制，可将多个较小规模的超纠缠W态融合成更大规模的超纠缠W态，仅需基本光学元件，无需条件量子门或辅助光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超纠缠态在量子信息处理中具有重要价值，能显著提高信道容量和增强噪声抵抗能力。然而，如何高效地构建大规模超纠缠W态是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出了两种超融合机制：1) 将n光子超W态和m光子超W态融合为(n+m-2)光子超W态；2) 将n光子、m光子和t光子超W态融合为(n+m+t-3)光子超W态。仅使用偏振分束器、平衡分束器、半波片、单光子探测器和交叉克尔非线性等基本光学元件，无需条件量子门、路径耦合器或辅助光子。

Result: 成功构建了在偏振和空间自由度上超纠缠的W态。方案仅产生一个垃圾输出态，表明具有高效率。融合后的W态规模显著增大，且保持了超纠缠特性。

Conclusion: 提出的超融合机制为构建大规模超纠缠W态提供了高效、实用的方案，仅需基本光学元件，无需复杂量子门或辅助资源，在量子信息处理中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Hyperentangled states are fascinating resources in quantum information processing as they can significantly increase the channel capacity and enhance noise resistance. We explore a hyperfusion mechanism to fuse one n photon hyper-W state and one m-photon hyper-W state into a large-scale (n+m-2)-photon hyper-W state. Another mechanism to fuse one n-photon hyper-W state, one m-photon hyper-$W$ state, and one $t$-photon hyper-W state into an (n+m+t-3)-photon hyper-W state is also proposed. These two hyperfusion mechanisms are constructed employing only polarizing beam splitters, balanced beam splitters, half-wave plates, single-photon detectors, and cross-Kerr nonlinearities. Conditional quantum gates, path couplers, and ancillary photons are not required in our constructions. Moreover, our fused $W$ states are hyperentangled in the polarization and spatial degrees of freedom of single-photon systems. The presence of only one garbage output state demonstrates that high efficiency can be achieved in our schemes.

</details>


### [136] [Answering Counting Queries with Differential Privacy on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10881)
*Arghya Mukherjee,Hassan Jameel Asghar,Gavin K. Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子编码数据集上计数查询的差分隐私保护，分析了两种振幅测量技术的隐私特性，并提出了量子差分隐私机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着差分隐私成为隐私保护数据分析的事实标准，量子环境下的差分隐私研究日益重要。论文旨在探索如何在量子编码数据集上安全地回答计数查询，这是最基本但丰富的统计类型。

Method: 1. 将量子数据集上的计数查询转化为两个正交态振幅的测量问题；2. 分析两种振幅测量技术的差分隐私特性：基于计算基的重复测量和经典振幅估计算法；3. 推导计数查询的全局敏感度紧界；4. 提出计数查询的差分隐私振幅估计算法；5. 讨论如何外包给量子服务器进行盲计算。

Result: 1. 证明了基于计算基重复测量技术在计数查询上的隐私结果优于一般查询的已知结果，且该机制因固有随机性而增强了隐私；2. 推导了添加或删除单个数据项时振幅最大可能变化的紧界（全局敏感度）；3. 提出了计数查询的差分隐私振幅估计算法；4. 展示了如何将计算外包给量子服务器实现盲差分隐私计算。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子编码数据集上的计数查询提供了有效的差分隐私保护机制，证明了量子环境下的隐私增强效应，并建立了从量子振幅测量到差分隐私的理论连接，为量子隐私保护数据分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Differential privacy is a mathematical notion of data privacy that has fast become the de facto standard in privacy-preserving data analysis. Recently a lot of work has focused on differential privacy in the quantum setting. Continuing on this line of study, we investigate how to answer counting queries on a quantum encoded dataset with differential privacy. An example of a counting query is ``How many people in the dataset are over the age of 25 and with a university education?'' Counting queries form the most basic but nonetheless rich set of statistics extractable from a dataset. We show that answering these queries on a quantum encoded dataset reduces to measuring the amplitude of one of two orthogonal states. We then analyze the differential privacy properties of two algorithms from literature to measure amplitude: one which performs repeated measurements in the computational basis, and the other which utilizes the classic amplitude estimation algorithm. For the first technique, we prove privacy results for the case of counting queries that improve on previously known results on general queries, and show that the mechanism in fact \emph{amplifies} privacy due to inherent randomness. For the second method, we derive a tight bound on maximum possible change in the amplitude if we add or remove a single item in the dataset, a quantity called global sensitivity which is central in making an algorithm differentially private. We then show a differentially private version of the amplitude estimation algorithm for counting queries. We also discuss how these methods can be outsourced to a quantum server to blindly compute counting queries with differential privacy.

</details>


### [137] [The non-local Hong-Ou-Mandel effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10886)
*Yuki Kodama,Jonte R. Hance,Holger F. Hofmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文展示了如何在四路径干涉仪中通过后选择空间分离的光子输出来实现局域传播光子与模式交换光子之间的干涉，从而观察到Hong-Ou-Mandel效应，即使光子从未在同一分束器相遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多光子干涉与纠缠之间的基本关系，研究即使光子路径在空间上分离且从未在同一分束器相遇的情况下，是否仍能观察到Hong-Ou-Mandel效应，从而为光学量子技术开辟新可能性。

Method: 使用四路径干涉仪，通过后选择空间分离的光子输出端口，实现局域传播光子与通过模式交换的光子之间的干涉。利用局部相移来映射不同输出位置检测到的光子之间的非经典关联。

Result: 成功在空间分离的输出端口观察到Hong-Ou-Mandel效应，即使光子从未在同一分束器相遇。通过局部相移映射出了光子之间的非经典关联，阐明了线性光学在产生空间分离光子纠缠中的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多光子干涉与纠缠之间的基本关系，表明即使光子路径在空间上分离，通过适当的干涉仪设计和后选择，仍能观察到非经典关联和Hong-Ou-Mandel效应，为光学量子技术提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Two-photon interference effects arise because photons are indistinguishable particles. In the wellknown Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) effect, the transmission of two photons at a beam splitter interferes destructively with the reflection of both photons, requiring both photons to "bunch up" by leaving the beam splitter on the same side. Here, we show that the interference between locally propagating photons and photons exchanged by a mode swap can be implemented by post-selecting spatially separated photon outputs of a four-path interferometer. Even though the photons detected at spatially separated locations must have travelled along paths that never met up at the same beam splitter, the Hong-Ou-Mandel effect can be observed in correlations between the output ports that originate from the association of detection events with non-local output modes defined by the two single photon inputs. Local phase shifts can be used to map out non-classical correlations between the photons detected at different output locations, clarifying the role of linear optics in generating entanglement between spatially separated photons. Our work thus establishes a fundamental relation between multiphoton interference and entanglement, opening the door to new possibilities in optical quantum technologies.

</details>


### [138] [Compiler Framework for Directional Transport in Zoned Neutral Atom Systems with AOD Assistance: A Hybrid Remote CZ Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11000)
*Lingyi Kong,Chen Huang,Zhemin Zhang,Yidong Zhou,Xiangyu Ren,Shaochen Li,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于定向传输的远程CZ门方案，解决中性原子阵列中声光偏转器移动受限的纠缠瓶颈问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于声光偏转器的原子移动方案存在行列交叉限制、设备速度限制和硬件范围限制，这些瓶颈严重制约了长距离连接能力

Method: 采用定向传输作为远程纠缠的默认方式，保留声光偏转器用于通道设置和微调。在反阻塞条件下，通过失谐调制的π脉冲序列驱动里德堡激发沿动态可重置的辅助通道进行定向传输，实现静止非相邻量子比特间的CZ门

Result: 与纯声光偏转器方案相比，纠缠阶段持续时间减少约50%至90%，能够实现超出物镜限制的长距离连接

Conclusion: 基于定向传输的远程CZ门方案克服了移动受限的纠缠限制，为中性原子阵列提供了高效的长距离连接能力

Abstract: We present a directional-transport (DT)-based remote CZ gate and compiler for zoned neutral-atom arrays that overcomes movement-bound entanglement limitations. Current AOD-based shuttling faces row/column non-crossing constraints, device-speed limits, and hardware-restricted range - bottlenecks for long-distance connectivity. Our approach reserves AODs for channel setup and micro-tuning while making DT the default for remote entanglement. Under antiblockade, a detuning-modulated pi-pulse sequence drives directional transport of a Rydberg excitation along a dynamic and resettable ancilla corridor, realizing a CZ gate between stationary, non-adjacent qubits. This cuts entangling-stage duration by approximately 50 to 90 percent versus AOD-only baselines and enables long-distance connectivity beyond objective-limited shuttling.

</details>


### [139] [QuMod: Parallel Quantum Job Scheduling on Modular QPUs using Circuit Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11013)
*Vinooth Kulkarni,Aaron Orenstein,Xinpeng Li,Shuai Xu,Daniel Blankenberg,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 开发用于模块化量子系统的多程序调度器，解决量子处理器互联架构下的资源分配和并行执行问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算向模块化架构发展，多个量子处理器通过经典互连形成大规模系统，云提供商需要确保多用户共享资源时的公平高效分配，特别是并行执行多个作业时面临新的调度挑战

Method: 开发了一个多程序调度器，联合考虑量子比特映射、并行电路执行、子电路间的测量同步，以及使用动态电路在量子处理器间进行量子隐形传态操作

Result: 提出了一个能够处理模块化量子系统复杂调度问题的框架，考虑了量子计算特有的约束如量子纠缠、测量同步和跨处理器通信

Conclusion: 随着量子计算向大规模模块化架构发展，需要专门的多程序调度器来解决资源分配和并行执行问题，本文提出的调度框架为量子云服务提供商管理共享模块化量子系统提供了解决方案

Abstract: The quantum computing community is increasingly positioning quantum processors as accelerators within classical HPC workflows, analogous to GPUs and TPUs. However, many real-world applications require scaling to hundreds or thousands of physical qubits to realize logical qubits via error correction. To reach these scales, hardware vendors employing diverse technologies -- such as trapped ions, photonics, neutral atoms, and superconducting circuits -- are moving beyond single, monolithic QPUs toward modular architectures connected via interconnects. For example, IonQ has proposed photonic links for scaling, while IBM has demonstrated a modular QPU architecture by classically linking two 127-qubit devices. Using dynamic circuits, Bell-pair-based teleportation, and circuit cutting, they have shown how to execute a large quantum circuit that cannot fit on a single QPU. As interest in quantum computing grows, cloud providers must ensure fair and efficient resource allocation for multiple users sharing such modular systems. Classical interconnection of QPUs introduces new scheduling challenges, particularly when multiple jobs execute in parallel. In this work, we develop a multi-programmable scheduler for modular quantum systems that jointly considers qubit mapping, parallel circuit execution, measurement synchronization across subcircuits, and teleportation operations between QPUs using dynamic circuits.

</details>


### [140] [SPATE: Spiking-Phase Adaptive Temporal Encoding for Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11022)
*Nouhaila Innan,Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SPATE：一种基于脉冲的时空编码方法，将静态量子机器学习扩展到时间信息处理，通过脉冲统计到量子旋转的映射提升特征表示质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子机器学习流水线主要依赖静态编码（如角度和幅度映射），限制了处理时间信息的能力，需要一种能够有效整合时间结构的编码机制

Method: 提出SPATE（Spiking-Phase Adaptive Temporal Encoding）方法：1）将实值表格特征转换为漏积分-发放脉冲序列；2）将脉冲统计映射到量子旋转；3）通过受控相位操作添加少量时间量子比特；4）引入编码中心评估协议独立评估表示质量

Result: SPATE在多个数据集上产生更强的特征表示：Blobs数据集CKTA达0.966（角度编码仅0.632），Fisher分数7.37（角度编码仅0.70）；Moons数据集CKTA达0.506（角度/幅度编码仅0.015）。在固定量子比特预算下，Wine数据集准确率0.826、AUC 0.978，Moons数据集准确率0.840、AUC 0.923

Conclusion: SPATE为构建更具信息量的量子特征表示提供了实用的脉冲-相位接口，在资源受限条件下显著提升了量子机器学习的表示能力和性能

Abstract: Most quantum machine learning (QML) pipelines still rely on static encodings such as angle and amplitude maps, and this limits their ability to handle temporal information. To address this limitation, this paper uses spike-based data representation as an effective encoding mechanism that incorporates temporal structure into quantum feature preparation. Specifically, we propose Spiking-Phase Adaptive Temporal Encoding (SPATE), a novel spike-driven temporal encoding method that converts real-valued tabular features into leaky integrate-and-fire spike trains and maps spike statistics to quantum rotations, augmented with a small set of temporal qubits through controlled phase operations. An encoding-centric evaluation protocol is also introduced to assess representation quality independently of the classifier, covering centered kernel-target alignment (CKTA), Fisher-style separability, inter/intra-class distance ratios, silhouette score, normalized entropy, and pairwise total-variation (TVpair) collapse indicators. Under stratified cross-validation, SPATE yields stronger representations across multiple datasets. For example, SPATE reaches a CKTA of 0.966 and a Fisher score of 7.37 on Blobs, compared with a CKTA of 0.632 and a Fisher score of 0.70 using angle encoding, and achieves a CKTA of 0.506 on Moons, compared with 0.015 using angle or amplitude encoding. These gains translate into stronger hybrid quantum neural network performance within a fixed qubit budget across several tasks, including an accuracy of 0.826 and an AUC of 0.978 for Wine, as well as an accuracy of 0.840 and an AUC of 0.923 for Moons. These results demonstrate that SPATE provides a practical spike-to-phase interface for building more informative quantum feature representations under constrained resources.

</details>


### [141] [Autonomous Quantum Error Correction of Spin-Oscillator Hybrid Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11145)
*Sungjoo Cho,Ju-yeon Gyhm,Hyukjoon Kwon,Hyunseok Jeong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需测量的自旋-振子混合量子比特稳定方案，通过自主量子纠错实现硬件高效的噪声偏置逻辑量子比特


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子纠错需要重复的综合征测量和前馈操作，这增加了硬件复杂性和资源消耗。本文旨在开发一种无需测量的自主纠错方案，同时保持硬件效率

Method: 采用连续变量-离散变量混合方法，通过工程化Lindbladian将编码空间转化为吸引稳态子空间。具体实现是将存储模式通过受控分束器和自旋依赖位移相互作用耦合到快速冷却的浴中

Result: 该方案在保持传统耗散工程硬件效率的同时，简化了系统-浴耦合要求。构建与简单逻辑门兼容，并利用了实验平台（如囚禁离子系统）已演示的基本操作

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的硬件高效、噪声偏置逻辑量子比特实现路径，无需重复综合征测量和前馈操作，为量子计算的实际应用提供了有前景的方案

Abstract: We propose a novel measurement-free scheme for stabilizing a spin-oscillator hybrid qubit via autonomous quantum error correction. The engineered Lindbladian renders the code space into an attractive steady-state subspace, realized by coupling the storage mode to a rapidly cooled bath through a controlled beam-splitter and spin-dependent displacement interactions. The continuous variable-discrete variable hybrid approach to autonomous quantum error correction preserves the hardware efficiency of conventional dissipation engineering while simplifying the required system-bath coupling. The construction is compatible with simple logical gates and leverages primitives already demonstrated in experimental platforms, such as trapped-ion systems, suggesting a practical route to hardware-efficient, noise-biased logical qubits without repeated syndrome measurements and feedforward.

</details>


### [142] [Cross-Sensor RGB Spectrograms: A Visual Method for Anomaly Detection in Classical and Quantum Magnetometer Triads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11190)
*Manas Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将三个同步磁力计的短时傅里叶变换功率谱映射到RGB通道的交叉传感器RGB谱图可视化方法，用于诊断测量健康和局部磁活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多磁力计阵列分析将每个传感器简化为独立功率谱，丢弃了传感器间结构信息，而这些信息本身对测量健康和局部磁活动具有诊断价值。

Method: 开发理论框架，将三个同步磁力计的STFT功率谱分别映射到RGB通道，形成交叉传感器RGB谱图。传感器间相干性显示为中性灰或白色，而单个或两个传感器特有的谱能量则显示为饱和颜色。

Result: 形式化了图像构建方法，推导了其时频分辨率特性，给出了每通道归一化选择的明确说明，并提出了颜色异常分类法，能够区分相干宽带活动、单传感器故障、不对称成对源和缓慢时间漂移。

Conclusion: 该方法作为独立的方法构建块，可插入任何同步采样磁力计三合一的监测管道前端，适用于经典磁通门传感器和量子磁力计，特别有助于区分量子极限噪声和技术伪影。

Abstract: Stationary multi-magnetometer arrays are routinely deployed in geomagnetic observatories, laboratory shielded rooms, and ground-based monitoring stations. The standard analysis pipeline reduces each sensor to an independent power spectrum, discarding any inter-sensor structure that is itself diagnostic of measurement health and of localised magnetic activity. This paper develops a purely theoretical framework for a deliberately simple visualisation that maps the short-time Fourier (STFT) power spectra of three concurrent magnetometers into the red, green, and blue channels of a single image: the \emph{cross-sensor RGB spectrogram}. Inter-sensor coherence appears as neutral grey or white, while spectral energy that is unique to one or two sensors stands out as saturated colour. We formalise the construction of the image, derive its time-frequency resolution properties, give an explicit account of the per-channel normalisation choice, and present a colour-anomaly taxonomy that distinguishes coherent broadband activity, single-sensor faults, asymmetric pairwise sources, and slow temporal drift. A companion long-window variant is described for resolving features in the ultra-low frequency (ULF) band. The construction is presented without reference to any particular dataset or implementation; it is intended as a self-contained methodological building block that can be inserted into any monitoring pipeline whose front end is a synchronously sampled magnetometer triad. Because the construction operates on scalar magnitude time series alone, it applies equally to classical fluxgate sensors and to quantum magnetometers -- optically pumped magnetometers (OPMs), nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centre arrays, and superconducting quantum interference devices (SQUIDs) -- where distinguishing quantum-limited noise from technical artefacts is a central diagnostic challenge.

</details>


### [143] [Low-dose Image Recognition with Quantum Computational Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11303)
*Hiroshi Okamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子计算成像在低剂量电子显微镜成像中具有优势，通过量子算法从n个候选图像中识别正确图像


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电子显微镜中低剂量成像问题，特别是对电子束敏感样本的成像挑战

Method: 使用两个qudit放置在电子束附近，实现电子显微镜与量子计算机之间的量子信息完全传输，并开发量子算法从多个候选图像中识别正确图像

Result: 提出的量子计算成像方案在低剂量电子显微镜成像中具有优势，能够处理比成像电子希尔伯特空间有效维度更多的候选图像

Conclusion: 量子计算成像为电子显微镜中的低剂量成像问题提供了有前景的解决方案，特别是在处理电子束敏感样本时具有显著优势

Abstract: We show that quantum computational imaging is advantageous in the setting of low-dose electron microscopy of beam-sensitive specimens. Two qudits placed near the electron beam enable full transfer of quantum information between the electron microscope and a quantum computer in the proposed scheme, providing the specimen is a phase object. We present a quantum algorithm that identifies the correct image among n candidate images, where n is larger than the effective dimension of the Hilbert space of the imaging electron.

</details>


### [144] [Engineered non-Gaussian Coherence as a Thermodynamic Resource for Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11313)
*Kingshuk Adhikary*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用量子非高斯态优化量子电池性能，通过工程化QNG态中的相干性，在各种高斯充电器配置下实现量子优势，并探索热展宽和环境耦合对稳定性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势在能量收集设备中具有重要意义，可能重塑热力学概念。量子非高斯态作为资源态能够实现通用量子操作，提供超越高斯态的热力学性能提升潜力，因此需要探索其在量子电池中的应用。

Method: 采用先前提出的量子非高斯态生成方案作为框架，将其直接集成到电池设置中。利用工程化QNG态中的相干性，在各种高斯充电器配置下优化量子电池性能，同时探索热展宽程度和环境耦合对充电器的影响，实现精确热管理下的稳定性能。

Result: 研究证明了量子非高斯态在量子能量存储单元中利用热力学资源的可行性，展示了QNG态能够增强量子电池性能，并在精确热管理下保持稳定运行。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子能量存储单元中热力学资源的开发利用提供了概念验证，表明量子非高斯态是实现量子优势的有前景候选方案，能够优化量子电池在各种高斯充电器配置下的性能。

Abstract: Accessing quantum advantage (QA) is a legitimate task in energy harvesting devices, and it is potentially reshaping thermodynamic concepts. In this respect, the resourceful quantum non-Gaussian (QNG) states are promising candidates that precisely enable universal quantum operations to enhance thermodynamic performance with capabilities beyond what Gaussian states can achieve. We recently proposed [K. Adhikary, D. W. Moore, and R. Filip, {\em Quantum Sci. Technol.} \textbf{10}, 035048 (2025)] the QNG state generation scheme, which serves as the framework for this study and is directly integrated into the battery setting to figure out QA. By leveraging coherence in the engineered QNG states, we aim to optimize the performance of quantum batteries for various Gaussian charger profiles under unitary dynamics. We further exploit the degree of thermal broadening and environmental coupling to the charger, which is capable of fostering stable performance under precise thermal management. This study provides a proof-of-concept for exploiting thermodynamic resources in quantum energy storage units.

</details>


### [145] [Fidelity-informed neural pulse compilation of a continuous family of quantum gates with uncertainty-margin analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11314)
*Arash Fath Lipaei,Ebrahim Khaleghian,Selin Aslan,Gani Göral,Zidong Lin,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 开发了基于保真度信息的神经脉冲编译框架，用于在三量子比特NMR处理器上实现单量子比特门的连续族，通过端到端学习从轴角参数到控制序列的直接映射，并引入风险感知优化提高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子门编译方法通常将目标幺正操作分解为校准好的基础门序列，这种方法存在校准开销大、参数漂移敏感等问题。特别是在NMR等量子平台上，重复的每门优化成本高昂，需要更高效、鲁棒的编译策略。

Method: 1. 开发神经脉冲编译框架，直接从SU(2)操作的轴角参数学习到分段恒定射频控制序列的映射；2. 通过驱动哈密顿量的时序传播子进行端到端训练，使用全局相位不敏感的幺正保真度作为学习信号；3. 引入结构化扰动的不确定性集合，基于右尾条件风险价值(RU-CVaR)进行风险感知重新设计，提高脉冲对参数扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 数值模拟显示单个模型能够泛化到连续的栅参数范围；2. 在实验台上三量子比特NMR设备上验证了代表性编译脉冲；3. 风险感知优化产生了在选定不确定性模型内具有更宽容错裕度的脉冲解；4. 实现了实验可访问环境中的连续脉冲级门合成。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在实验可访问环境中实现连续脉冲级门合成，并提出了一种硬件感知的编译策略。虽然不确定性模型针对NMR定制，但神经编译和风险感知优化框架具有通用性，可扩展到其他校准开销大、参数漂移或控制约束使重复每门优化成本高昂的量子架构中。

Abstract: We develop a fidelity-informed neural pulse-compilation framework for a continuous family of single-qubit gates on a three-qubit liquid-state nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) processor. Instead of decomposing each target unitary into a sequence of calibrated basis gates, the method learns a direct map from the axis-angle parameters of an arbitrary U_2 in SU(2) operation to a piecewise-constant radio-frequency control sequence that implements the desired transformation. Training is performed end-to-end through the time-ordered propagator of the driven Hamiltonian using global-phase-insensitive unitary fidelity as the learning signal. We show numerically that a single model generalizes across a continuous range of gate parameters and experimentally validate representative compiled pulses on a benchtop three-qubit NMR device. In addition, we analyze sensitivity to structured perturbations in Hamiltonian and control parameters by introducing a prescribed uncertainty set and performing a comparative risk-aware redesign based on right-tail Conditional Value-at-Risk (RU-CVaR). This stage produces pulse solutions with broader tolerance margins within the chosen uncertainty model. The results demonstrate continuous pulse-level gate synthesis in an experimentally accessible setting and illustrate a hardware-aware compilation strategy that can be extended to other quantum platforms. While the uncertainty model considered here is tailored to NMR, the neural compilation and risk-aware optimization framework are general and may be useful in architectures where calibration overhead, parameter drift, or control constraints make repeated per-gate optimization costly.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal Two-Qubit Gates for Group-IV Color-Centers in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11340)
*Jurek Frey,Katharina Senkalla,Philipp J. Vetter,Fedor Jelezko,Frank K. Wilhelm,Matthias M. Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过量子最优控制实现GeV色心与13C核自旋间的高保真度两比特门，为分布式量子计算提供可扩展策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石中IV族掺杂色心具有长寿命核自旋，是分布式量子计算节点和量子中继器的重要候选。实现电子自旋与核自旋间快速鲁棒的量子门操作是长距离纠缠分布协议的关键

Method: 针对现有GeV色心与强耦合13C核自旋实验平台，采用量子最优控制方法进行数值研究，在现实噪声条件下探索不同参数区域和门操作

Result: 获得了鲁棒性强的两比特门，保真度超过99.9%

Conclusion: 该框架为IV族量子节点提供了可扩展策略，可适配相关架构

Abstract: Color centers associated with group-IV dopants in diamond with long-lived nuclear spins have emerged as major candidates for distributed quantum computing nodes and quantum repeaters. Several proof-of-principle experiments have already been demonstrated. A key operation for long-distance entanglement-distribution protocols are fast and robust gates between the electron spin and a nuclear spin. Here, we investigate numerically for an existing experimental platform of a Germanium-vacancy (GeV) center with a strongly-coupled ${}^{13}$C spin, how such gates can be implemented via quantum optimal control. In the presence of realistic noise we investigate different parameter regimes and gate operations and obtain robust two-qubit gates with fidelities exceeding $99.9 \%$. The framework provides a scalable strategy for group-IV quantum nodes and can be adapted to related architectures.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum Sensing with Joint Emitter-Fluorescence Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11377)
*Yuliya Bilinskaya,Sreenath K. Manikandan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个可解析处理的驱动量子谐振发射器模型，用于研究共振荧光中的早期量子关联，并设计联合测量策略探测驱动场的量子噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究驱动量子谐振发射器（如振荡带电偶极子）在共振荧光过程中，驱动场、谐振发射器和荧光场之间在早期时间建立的量子力学关联，探索这些量子特征在实验中的可观测性。

Method: 构建可解析处理的驱动量子谐振发射器模型，设计对量子发射器及其荧光场进行联合测量的检测策略，通过同时量子测量来探测驱动场相对于最大经典相干态的量子噪声。

Result: 模型能够表征驱动场、谐振发射器和荧光之间在早期时间建立的量子关联，提出的联合测量策略可以揭示这些量子特征，特别是通过短时实验探测驱动场的量子噪声。

Conclusion: 该模型和方法为量子传感应用提供了新途径，在量子光学、量子声学和量子引力等感兴趣的场景中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We present an analytically tractable model of a driven quantum harmonic emitter, such as an oscillating charged dipole, emitting radiation via resonance fluorescence. With this model we are able to characterize the quantum mechanical correlations that are built up at early times between the drive, the resonant emitter, and its fluorescence. We describe detection strategies that can reveal these quantum signatures in experiments by performing joint measurements on the quantum emitter and its fluorescence field. In particular, we show that simultaneous quantum measurements of a driven quantum emitter and its fluorescence can be used to probe the quantum noise of the driving field, relative to the maximally classical coherent state of the driving field, in short-time experiments. We conclude by discussing the applications to quantum sensing in quantum optical, quantum acoustic, and quantum gravitational scenarios of interest.

</details>


### [148] [From GDSII to Wafer: EDA Design Flow and Data Conversion for Wafer-Scale Manufacturing of Superconducting Quantum Chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11379)
*Ling Qiao,Fumin Luo,Qinglang Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了面向晶圆级制造的量子芯片电子设计自动化技术栈，重点关注从GDSII设计文件到完整制造数据转换的关键流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超导量子计算向千比特乃至百万比特规模发展，晶圆级制造成为实现大规模、经济高效量子处理器的关键路径。这种制造范式对量子芯片电子设计自动化提出了新要求：设计工具不仅要生成满足量子电路物理约束的版图，还要确保设计数据能够无缝转换为晶圆厂可执行的完整制造文件集，从而实现从设计意图到物理芯片的可靠转换。

Method: 论文从GDSII作为单一权威数据源出发，系统分析了量子EDA技术栈的关键阶段：基于工艺设计套件的设计规则检查、版图与原理图一致性验证、可制造性设计优化、晶圆布局规划以及掩模数据准备。提出了量子专用DRC规则及其物理基础，建立了多层工艺堆栈模型，并对主流Q-EDA工具的制造数据流覆盖范围进行了基准测试。

Result: 提出了包含9个量子专用设计规则检查规则的完整集合及其物理基础，建立了多层工艺堆栈模型，评估了主流Q-EDA工具的制造数据流覆盖范围，并设计了Q-EDA系统的具体架构。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地处理了晶圆级制造的量子EDA技术栈，为从设计到制造的完整数据转换流程提供了框架。最后讨论了该领域的核心挑战和未来发展方向，为大规模量子处理器制造提供了关键的技术基础。

Abstract: Superconducting quantum computing is advancing toward the thousand- and even million-qubit regime, making wafer-scale fabrication an essential pathway for achieving large-scale, cost-effective quantum processors. This manufacturing paradigm imposes new requirements on quantum-chip electronic design automation (Q-EDA): design tools must not only generate layouts (GDSII files) that satisfy quantum-circuit physical constraints but also ensure that the design data can be seamlessly converted into a complete set of manufacturing files executable by a wafer foundry, thereby enabling reliable translation from design intent to physical chip. This paper focuses on this critical data-conversion pipeline and presents a systematic treatment of the Q-EDA technology stack for wafer-scale fabrication. Starting from GDSII as the single authoritative data source, we analyze the key stages including process-design-kit (PDK)-based design rule checking (DRC), layout-versus-schematic (LVS) verification, design for manufacturability (DFM) optimization, wafer layout planning, and mask data preparation (MDP). We describe the concrete architecture of a Q-EDA system, present nine quantum-specific DRC rules together with their physical underpinnings and a multi-layer process stack model, and benchmark the manufacturing data-flow coverage of mainstream Q-EDA tools. Finally, we discuss the core challenges and future directions in this field.

</details>


### [149] [When T-Depth Misleads: Predicting Fault-Tolerant Quantum Execution Slowdown under Magic-State Delivery Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11409)
*Boshuai Ye,Arif Ali Khan,Peng Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种考虑T态交付约束的量子电路执行模型，引入松弛比和最大累积需求过剩作为评估指标，证明这些指标比传统T深度更能预测执行性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子算法的高效执行受限于非Clifford操作所需的magic态产生速率。传统电路优化主要关注T深度，但静态T深度在有界T态交付约束下无法可靠预测可执行性能，需要显式建模交付约束。

Method: 引入一个捕获供需不平衡的模型，使用两个关键量：松弛比（调度灵活性的结构指标）和Δ_max（累积需求过剩的度量）。通过构建的有向无环图族、算术电路和精确量子傅里叶变换轨迹进行实证评估。

Result: Δ_max是执行减速的强调度级指标，并为固定调度提供了可执行完工时间的可证明下界。在4,904个实例中，下界零违反，88.9%的情况在一个周期内。松弛比是比T深度更强的停顿和反转风险结构预测因子，Δ_max是最强的减速预测因子。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在容错量子编译中显式建模交付约束的重要性，提出的模型和指标比传统T深度方法更能准确预测量子电路在实际交付约束下的执行性能。

Abstract: The efficient execution of fault-tolerant quantum algorithms is fundamentally limited by the production rate of magic states required for non-Clifford operations. While circuit optimization typically targets T-depth, static T-depth does not reliably predict executable performance under bounded T-state delivery. We introduce a model that captures demand-supply imbalance using two key quantities: slack ratio, a structural indicator of scheduling flexibility, and Delta_max, a measure of cumulative demand surplus. We show that Delta_max is a strong schedule-level indicator of execution slowdown and yields a provable lower bound on executable makespan for a fixed schedule. Empirical evaluation on constructed directed acyclic graph (DAG) families, with arithmetic circuits and exact quantum Fourier transform (QFT) traces providing additional grounding, shows that slack ratio is a stronger structural predictor than T-depth for stall and inversion risk, while Delta_max is the strongest predictor of slowdown. Across 4,904 instances, the lower bound shows zero violations, with 88.9% of cases within one cycle. These results highlight the importance of explicitly modeling delivery constraints in fault-tolerant quantum compilation.

</details>


### [150] [Unfair Sampling of Quantum Annealing in Weighted Graph Bipartitioning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11449)
*Shunta Ide,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子退火在加权图二分问题中，惩罚系数增加能改善不公平采样，但会降低基态概率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火在组合优化问题中表现出不公平采样现象，即简并基态无法被等概率采样，这对解多样性评估和组合计数等应用很重要。目前对约束优化问题中不公平采样的机制理解不足，特别是在加权图二分问题这类代表性约束优化问题中。

Method: 研究加权图二分问题中惩罚系数对采样公平性的影响。通过数值模拟和D-Wave Advantage2系统的实验，分析惩罚系数变化对采样公平性的影响。对随机生成的实例进行扩展分析，系统规模达到12个自旋。

Result: 增加惩罚系数能减少代表性单个实例中的不公平采样，这种定性行为在实际硬件上也得到验证。扩展分析显示，虽然这种趋势并非普遍存在，但超过70%的实例表现出随着惩罚系数增加，采样公平性单调提高，即使在研究的最大系统规模下也是如此。

Conclusion: 增加惩罚系数可以改善采样公平性，但在实际退火条件下会以降低基态概率为代价。这些结果呼吁对约束优化问题中不公平采样机制进行更深入的理论理解。

Abstract: Quantum annealing (QA) is a promising approach for solving combinatorial optimization problems; however, it is known to exhibit unfair sampling, in which degenerate ground states are not sampled with equal probability even for sufficiently long annealing times. Fair sampling is important in applications such as solution diversity assessment and combinatorial counting, yet the mechanism of unfair sampling remains poorly understood, particularly in constrained combinatorial optimization problems. In this work, we investigate unfair sampling of QA in weighted graph bipartitioning problems (GBP), a representative constrained optimization problem. We study how the penalty coefficient in the penalty method affects sampling fairness. Through numerical simulations and experiments on the D-Wave Advantage2 system, we show that increasing the penalty coefficient reduces unfair sampling in a representative single instance, and that this qualitative behavior is also observed on actual hardware. A scaling analysis over randomly generated instances with up to 12 spins reveals that, while this trend does not hold universally, more than 70% of instances exhibit monotonically increasing sampling fairness as the penalty coefficient increases, even at the largest system size studied. These results show that increasing the penalty coefficient improves sampling fairness, though at the cost of ground-state probability under practical annealing conditions, and call for a deeper theoretical understanding of unfair sampling in constrained optimization problems.

</details>


### [151] [Accuracy-Cost Trade-offs for Reference VQE Calculations of H$_2$ on IBM Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11478)
*Julen Larrucea,Marita Oliv,Jeanette Lorenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提供了一个在2026年IBM量子处理器上计算氢分子基态能量的硬件验证参考数据集，通过标准化工作流程评估了不同因素对能量精度的影响，为量子化学应用提供了透明基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子化学应用提供透明基准，评估当前IBM量子硬件的实际能力和限制，帮助新用户了解不同选择的影响以及预期的运行时间和成本。

Method: 使用标准化工作流程，在多个2026年IBM量子处理器上进行变分基态能量计算，系统评估了采样次数、后端选择、优化策略和运行时变异性对能量精度的影响。

Result: 电路简化通过锥形映射提供最一致的精度提升；弹性级别1显著提高精度但成本大幅增加；基于会话的执行相比单作业执行没有系统性精度优势，尽管计费时间明显更高。

Conclusion: 该数据集和分析为评估IBM量子硬件在量子化学应用中的当前能力和限制提供了透明基准，有助于新用户了解不同配置选择的影响，并提供了实际运行成本和效果的全面概览。

Abstract: We present a hardware-validated reference dataset for variational ground-state energy calculations of the hydrogen molecule H\(_2\) on several IBM Quantum processors available in 2026. Using a standardized workflow, we benchmark the impact of shot count, backend choice, optimization strategy, and runtime variability on the achievable energy accuracy relative to exact diagonalization. The resulting dataset and analysis provide a transparent baseline for assessing the current capabilities and limitations of IBM Quantum hardware for quantum-chemistry applications, and are meant to ease the entry for new users by providing a comprehensive overview of choices and their effects as well as runtime efforts and costs that can be expected.
  Across the configurations studied here, circuit simplification through tapered mappings provides the most consistent accuracy gains, resilience level 1 improves accuracy at a substantial cost premium, and session-based execution yields no systematic accuracy advantage over single-job execution despite markedly higher billed time.

</details>


### [152] [Arbitrary-Velocity Volkov Wavepackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11503)
*D. Ramsey,J. McKeown,J. P. Palastro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过调控Volkov态间的动量关联，构建时空结构化的波包，使其概率密度峰值以任意定制速度传播，该速度与场振幅和速度期望值均无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电轻子在电磁平面波场中的演化，探索如何通过调控量子态来定制波包的传播特性，实现超越传统期望值的可控运动。

Method: 采用Volkov态叠加方法，通过施加特定的动量关联来构造时空结构化的波包，使概率密度峰值以任意定制速度传播。

Result: 成功构建了具有定制传播速度的波包，该速度可独立于场振幅和速度期望值进行选择，且动量关联会改变期望值轨迹，为任意速度提供了可观测的物理特征。

Conclusion: 通过调控Volkov态间的动量关联，可以实现带电轻子波包传播速度的任意定制，这为量子态操控和波包动力学提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: The evolution of a charged lepton in the field of an electromagnetic plane wave can be described as a superposition of Volkov states. Here we demonstrate that imposing specific momentum correlations among Volkov states produces a spatiotemporally structured wavepacket whose probability-density peak travels at an arbitrary, tailored velocity. This velocity can be chosen independently of both the field amplitude and the velocity expectation value. The imposed momentum correlations modify the expectation-value trajectory, providing a measurable signature of the arbitrary velocity within a physical observable.

</details>


### [153] [Tackling instabilities of quantum Krylov subspace methods: an analysis of the numerical and statistical errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11532)
*Maria Gabriela Jordão Oliveira,Karl Michael Ziems,Nina Glaser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Krylov子空间量子算法在无噪声模拟中会出现数值不稳定，但在实际噪声环境下统计波动是主要问题，而非病态性；作者提出了两种新指标来评估解的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov子空间方法是早期容错量子算法中用于估计量子系统基态能量的重要方法，但其数值稳定性问题（特别是病态性）可能影响能量估计的准确性。作者旨在分析这些方法在有无采样噪声情况下的数值稳定性和统计问题。

Method: 通过数值模拟分析Krylov子空间方法在有无采样噪声情况下的表现。在理想无噪声模拟中研究广义特征值问题的稳定性，在实际噪声设置中分析统计波动的影响。提出了两种新指标（虚部滤波器和酉滤波器）来评估所得解的可信度，这些指标无需真实特征谱的先验知识。

Result: 1. 在理想数值模拟中，随着Krylov子空间尺寸增加，广义特征值问题确实变得不稳定；2. 在实际噪声设置中，这些方法主要不是受病态性影响，而是统计波动占主导地位，可能阻碍可靠解的提取；3. 提出的虚部和酉滤波器能够成功评估所得解的可信度。

Conclusion: Krylov子空间量子算法的数值稳定性问题在实际噪声环境中主要表现为统计波动而非病态性。需要适当的正则化或滤波技术来确保可靠解提取。新提出的滤波器指标为评估解的可信度提供了有效工具，无需真实特征谱信息。

Abstract: Krylov subspace methods are among the most extensively studied early fault-tolerant quantum algorithms for estimating ground-state energies of quantum systems. However, the rapid onset of ill-conditioning might make accurate energies difficult or even impossible to retrieve. In this communication, we analyse the numerical stability and statistical problems of these methods using numerical simulations both in the presence and absence of sampling noise. While in ideal numerical simulations the generalized eigenvalue problem indeed becomes unstable with increased Krylov subspace size, we find that, in realistic noisy settings, these methods do not primarily suffer from ill-conditioning. Instead, statistical fluctuations dominate and can prevent reliable solution extraction unless appropriate regularization or filtering techniques are employed. We consequently introduce two new metrics, the imaginary and unitary filters, that successfully assess the reliability of the obtained solutions without any knowledge of the true eigenspectrum.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum circuit optimization for arbitrary high-dimensional bipartite quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11534)
*Gui-Long Jiang,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用受控增量(CINC)门和局部门构建任意维度量子门(quNit-quMit门)的合成方案，证明了CINC门与局部门构成高维量子计算的通用门集，实现了O(n²)的CINC门上界，特别对于受控quNit-quMit门仅需2个CINC门


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维量子门在量子计算中具有重要前景，但需要有效的实现方案。本文旨在为任意维度的量子系统(quNit-quMit系统)提供高效的量子电路合成方法，减少所需的基本门数量。

Method: 提出了一种合成方案，使用受控增量(CINC)门和局部门来构建任意quNit-quMit门的量子电路。该方案将高维量子门分解为CINC门和局部门的组合，证明了CINC门与局部门构成高维量子计算的通用门集。

Result: 实现了O(n²)的CINC门上界，这是目前已知的最佳结果。特别地，对于受控quNit-quMit门，仅需2个CINC门，而先前方案需要2n个。这显著减少了实现高维量子门所需的资源。

Conclusion: CINC门与局部门构成高维量子计算的通用门集，提出的合成方案为高维量子门实现提供了高效方法，显著降低了资源需求，特别是对于受控门实现了从2n到2的改进。

Abstract: Implementation of high-dimensional (HD) quantum gates shows very promising perspectives for HD quantum computation. A bipartite quantum system with arbitrary dimensions $n$ and $m$ is termed a quNit-quMit. Here we propose a synthesis scheme to construct the quantum circuit for general quNit-quMit gates with controlled increment (CINC) gates and local gates. This shows that CINC gates combined with local gates form a universal gate set for HD quantum computation. An upper bound of $O(n^2)$ CINC gates is achieved for arbitrary quNit-quMit gate implementation in the proposed scheme, which is the best known result. Especially for the controlled quNit-quMit gates, our scheme requires only 2 CINC gates, whereas the previous scheme required $2n$.

</details>


### [155] [A Systematic Study of Noise Effects in Hybrid Quantum-Classical Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11541)
*Bhavna Bose,Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了变分量子分类器在经典数据噪声与量子硬件噪声共同作用下的鲁棒性，发现经典输入噪声会显著加剧量子退相干效应，导致训练不稳定和分类精度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多孤立地关注量子电路噪声，而忽略了经典数据采集噪声与NISQ硬件噪声的联合影响。在噪声不可避免的近期量子机器学习环境中，需要全面理解多级噪声对QML模型性能的影响。

Method: 使用Titanic数据集作为基准，在量子编码前对经典特征应用多种数据集级噪声模型（斑点噪声、脉冲噪声、量化噪声、特征丢弃），同时在电路级使用Qiskit Aer模拟器引入硬件启发的量子噪声通道（退极化噪声、振幅阻尼、相位阻尼、泡利误差、读出误差）。

Result: 实验结果表明，经典输入数据中的噪声会显著加剧量子退相干效应，导致训练过程更不稳定，分类准确率明显下降。经典噪声与量子噪声的联合作用对模型性能产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 在NISQ时代设计和评估量子机器学习管道时必须考虑噪声因素，需要同时考虑经典噪声和量子噪声对QML性能的影响，这强调了噪声感知的QML管道设计的重要性。

Abstract: Near-term quantum machine learning (QML) models operate in environments wherein noise is unavoidable, arising from both imperfect classical data acquisition and the limitations of noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware. Although most existing studies have focused primarily on quantum circuit noise in isolation, the combined influence of corrupted classical inputs and quantum hardware noise has received comparatively little attention.
  In this work, we present a systematic experimental study of the robustness of a variational quantum classifier under realistic multi-level noise conditions. Using the Titanic dataset as a benchmark, a range of dataset-level noise models-including speckle noise, impulse noise, quantization noise, and feature dropout are applied to classical features prior to quantum encoding using a ZZ feature map. In parallel, hardware-inspired quantum noise channels such as depolarizing noise, amplitude damping, phase damping, Pauli errors, and readout errors are incorporated at the circuit level using the Qiskit Aer simulator.
  The experimental results indicate that noise in classical input data can significantly intensify the effects of quantum decoherence, resulting in less stable training and noticeably lower classification accuracy. Together, these observations emphasize the importance of designing and evaluating quantum machine learning pipelines with noise in mind, and highlight the need to consider classical and quantum noise simultaneously when assessing QML performance in the NISQ era

</details>


### [156] [Minimizing classical resources in variational measurement-based quantum computation for generative modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11578)
*Arunava Majumder,Hendrik Poulsen Nautrup,Hans J. Briegel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种受限的变分测量基量子计算模型，仅用单个额外可训练参数将酉模型扩展为信道模型，能生成酉模型无法学习到的概率分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBQC通过经典处理校正测量随机性以确保确定性酉计算，而变分MBQC利用这种随机性在生成建模中获得优势。但现有VMBQC的信道模型参数数量是酉模型的两倍（N×D），导致优化困难且模型难以训练。

Method: 提出受限VMBQC模型，仅使用单个额外可训练参数将酉设置扩展到基于信道的模型。通过数值和代数方法证明这种最小扩展的充分性。

Result: 该最小扩展足以生成对应酉模型无法学习的概率分布。数值和代数分析均证实了该模型的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的受限VMBQC模型通过仅增加单个参数实现了从酉模型到信道模型的扩展，解决了传统VMBQC参数过多导致的训练困难问题，同时保持了生成酉模型无法学习分布的能力。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) is a framework for quantum information processing in which a computational task is carried out through one-qubit measurements on a highly entangled resource state. Due to the indeterminacy of the outcomes of a quantum measurement, the random outcomes of these operations, if not corrected, yield a variational quantum channel family. Traditionally, this randomness is corrected through classical processing in order to ensure deterministic unitary computations. Recently, variational measurement-based quantum computation (VMBQC) has been introduced to exploit this measurement-induced randomness to gain an advantage in generative modeling. A limitation of this approach is that the corresponding channel model has twice as many parameters compared to the unitary model, scaling as $N \times D$, where $N$ is the number of logical qubits (width) and $D$ is the depth of the VMBQC model. This can often make optimization more difficult and may lead to poorly trainable models. In this paper, we present a restricted VMBQC model that extends the unitary setting to a channel-based one using only a single additional trainable parameter. We show, both numerically and algebraically, that this minimal extension is sufficient to generate probability distributions that cannot be learned by the corresponding unitary model.

</details>


### [157] [Spectrum analysis with quantum dynamical systems. II. Finite-time analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11614)
*Xinyi Sui,Mankei Tsang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟验证了谱光子计数方法在有限观测时间内相对于零差探测的噪声谱学优势，证明了其Fisher信息量和估计误差均平滑趋近于渐近极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作提出了谱光子计数方法增强光学干涉仪噪声谱学性能，但其结果基于无限观测时间的渐近极限，有限时间下的有效性尚不明确。本研究旨在通过数值模拟验证该理论在有限时间内的有效性。

Method: 采用数值研究方法，假设信号为具有未知方差参数的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程。计算了零差探测的Fisher信息、谱光子计数Fisher信息的下限以及量子上界，均未采用无限时间极限。同时通过蒙特卡洛模拟计算最大似然估计器的误差，以验证Fisher信息作为精度度量的可靠性。

Result: 结果表明：1) Fisher信息量和估计误差均平滑地趋近于其渐近极限；2) 谱光子计数相对于零差探测的优势在有限时间内仍能保持显著；3) 最大似然估计器的误差与Fisher信息预测一致，验证了Fisher信息作为精度度量的有效性。

Conclusion: 谱光子计数方法在有限观测时间内确实能提供优于零差探测的噪声谱学性能，验证了先前理论预测的实际可行性，为实际实验应用提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The prequel to this work [Ng et al., Phys. Rev. A 93, 042121 (2016)] proposes the method of spectral photon counting to enhance noise spectroscopy with an optical interferometer. While the predicted enhancement over homodyne detection is promising, the results there are derived by taking an asymptotic limit of infinite observation time; their validity for a finite time remains unclear. To validate the theory, here we perform a numerical study of a finite-time model. Assuming that the signal is an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process with an unknown variance parameter, we evaluate the Fisher information for homodyne detection, a lower bound on the Fisher information for spectral photon counting, and a quantum upper bound, all without taking the infinite-time limit. To confirm that the Fisher-information quantities are satisfactory precision measures, we also compute the errors of the maximum-likelihood estimator by Monte-Carlo simulations.
