The results demonstrate that the Fisher-information quantities and the estimation errors all smoothly approach their asymptotic limits, and the advantage of spectral photon counting over homodyne detection can remain substantial for finite times.

</details>


### [158] [NV-ensemble enabled microwave/NV parametric amplifier with optimal driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11643)
*Roman Ovsiannikov,Kurt Jacobs,Andrii G. Sotnikov,Matthew E. Trusheim,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速、内存高效、保持幺正性的数值方法，用于超越旋转波近似的封闭Tavis-Cummings模型，该方法通过基变换将哈密顿量转化为三对角形式，实现线性计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tavis-Cummings模型描述多能级自旋系统与腔模的相互作用，但该模型不是三对角形式，传统数值方法计算复杂度和内存需求高，需要开发更高效的算法。

Method: 通过基变换（重新索引/置换基元素）将Tavis-Cummings模型转化为三对角形式，截断腔模的Fock基，使计算复杂度在时间和内存上均与耦合系统总维度呈线性关系。

Result: 该方法实现了快速、内存高效的数值模拟，保持幺正性，适用于任何哈密顿量项可转化为三对角形式的封闭量子系统。

Conclusion: 提出的数值方法通过基变换将Tavis-Cummings模型转化为三对角形式，显著降低了计算复杂度，为超越旋转波近似的量子系统模拟提供了高效工具。

Abstract: We present a fast, memory-efficient, unitarity-preserving numerical method beyond the rotating-wave approximation for the closed Tavis-Cummings model in which a multilevel spin system interacts with a cavity mode. This model can describe the interaction of an ensemble of spins with a cavity mode in which the spin frequency and other parameters are time-dependent. The method exploits the fact that, while the Tavis-Cummings model is not tri-diagonal, it can be brought into tri-diagonal form by a change of basis that can be implemented purely by re-indexing (permuting basis elements), which is a fast operation. By truncating the Fock basis of the cavity mode, the computational complexity of the method is linear in the total dimension of the coupled system in both time and memory. The method can be employed to simulate any closed quantum system whose Hamiltonian terms can be brought into tri-diagonal form.

</details>


### [159] [A Comparative Study of Hybrid Quantum and Classical Genetic Algorithms in Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11667)
*Romeu Rossi Junior,José Augusto Miranda Nacif,Leonardo Antônio Mendes Souza,Marcus Henrique Soares Mendes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 混合量子遗传算法在投资组合优化问题上比经典遗传算法收敛更快，同时保持更高的种群多样性，且比暴力搜索需要更少的评估次数达到全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合量子遗传算法（HQGA）与经典遗传算法（GA）在解决投资组合优化问题上的性能差异，探索量子计算技术在优化问题中的潜在优势。

Method: 使用混合量子遗传算法（HQGA）和经典遗传算法（GA）分别解决投资组合优化问题，并进行性能比较分析。

Result: HQGA比经典GA收敛到最优解的速度更快，在整个优化过程中保持更高的种群多样性水平，并且比暴力搜索方法需要显著更少的评估次数达到全局最优解。

Conclusion: 混合量子遗传算法在投资组合优化问题上展现出优于经典遗传算法的性能，特别是在收敛速度和种群多样性方面，表明量子计算技术在该类优化问题中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This work investigates the performance of a Hybrid Quantum Genetic Algorithm (HQGA) compared to a classical Genetic Algorithm (GA) for solving the portfolio optimization problem. Our results indicate that the HQGA converges faster to the optimal solution than its classical counterpart, while also maintaining a higher level of population diversity throughout the optimization process. In addition, the HQGA requires significantly fewer evaluations-to-solution than a brute-force approach to reach the global optimum.

</details>


### [160] [Frustration-Induced Expressibility Limitations in Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11688)
*Sandip Maiti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 几何阻挫导致变分量子算法在量子多体系统中表现受限，标准哈密顿量启发式ansatz难以捕捉非均匀关联，需要引入键分辨变分参数来改善


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究几何阻挫对变分量子算法在量子多体系统中的应用限制，几何阻挫源于竞争相互作用阻碍同时能量最小化，对变分量子算法构成基本挑战

Method: 研究具有阻挫对角耦合的方晶格横场伊辛模型，分析标准哈密顿量启发式ansatz的局限性，引入键分辨变分参数，研究低能激发和近简并谱

Result: 几何阻挫导致强非均匀关联，标准全局参数ansatz难以捕捉，所需电路深度在中场区域显著增加；引入键分辨参数可在减少电路深度下获得准确结果；阻挫区域的近简并谱进一步挑战变分方法

Conclusion: 几何阻挫限制了变分量子算法在阻挫系统中的表现，这种限制源于ansatz表达能力不足而非优化困难，为量子模拟提供了改进ansatz设计策略的物理解释

Abstract: Geometric frustration, arising from competing interactions that prevent simultaneous energy minimization, presents a fundamental challenge for variational quantum algorithms applied to quantum many-body systems. We investigate the transverse-field Ising model on a square lattice with frustrated diagonal coupling and show that geometric frustration leads to strongly inhomogeneous correlations that are difficult to capture using standard Hamiltonian-inspired ansätze with global parameters. As a result, the required circuit depth increases significantly in the intermediate-field regime. We demonstrate that this limitation is not caused by optimization difficulties such as barren plateaus, but instead arises from insufficient expressibility of the ansatz. By introducing bond-resolved variational parameters, we recover accurate results at reduced circuit depth. We also study low-energy excitations and find that near-degenerate spectra in the frustrated regime further challenge variational methods. Our results provide a clear physical explanation for the limitations of variational quantum algorithms in frustrated systems and suggest improved ansatz design strategies for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [161] [Sub-nanosecond control for spin-defect quantum memories with a low-cost, compact FPGA platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11743)
*Victor Marcenac,Tommy Nguyen,Julie Chen,Weitao He,Enrique Garcia,Yuyang Han,Bethany E. Matthews,Tiamike Dudley,Andrew Mounce,Kai-Mei C. Fu,Maxwell F. Parsons*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过在FPGA平台上扩展QICK框架，实现了200ps精度的时序控制，并将其应用于金刚石中氮空位中心的动态解耦光谱学，显著提升了核自旋超精细耦合的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子缺陷的动态解耦技术受限于控制硬件的时序分辨率，导致窄谱特征欠采样和参数提取失真。现有商业任意波形发生器昂贵且不够灵活，需要开发低成本、高分辨率的控制方案。

Method: 扩展开源QICK框架，采用波形偏移方法在RF片上系统设备上实现亚纳秒级时序控制，无需修改底层硬件。将这一能力应用于金刚石中氮空位中心的动态解耦光谱学。

Result: 实现了200ps的有效时序分辨率，精确提取了单个¹³C核自旋的超精细耦合参数，解析了原本会被欠采样的谱特征。相比商业设备，该方案成本更低、更灵活。

Conclusion: 基于FPGA的灵活低成本控制硬件能够实现高分辨率的设备级自旋量子存储器表征，为商业任意波形发生器提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Dynamical decoupling techniques are widely used to characterize and control the environments of solid-state quantum defects, enabling solid-state quantum memories and nanoscale quantum sensors. However, resolution is often limited by the timing granularity of control hardware, which can undersample narrow spectral features and distort extracted parameters. Here, we demonstrate sub-nanosecond timing control on an inexpensive FPGA-based platform by extending the open-source QICK (Quantum Instrumentation Control Kit) framework using a waveform-offset method. This approach achieves an effective timing resolution of 200~ps on an RF system-on-chip device without modification to the underlying hardware. We apply this capability to dynamical decoupling spectroscopy of nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond, enabling precise extraction of hyperfine couplings of individual $^{13}\mathrm{C}$ nuclear spins and resolving spectral features that are otherwise undersampled. These results demonstrate that high-resolution, device-level characterization of spin-based quantum memories can be achieved using flexible, inexpensive control hardware, providing a scalable alternative to commercial arbitrary waveform generators.

</details>


### [162] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Optimization Workflows for the Shipment Selection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11758)
*Miguel Angel Lopez-Ruiz,Daiwei Zhu,Jonas Hatzenbuhler,Shudian Zhao,Claudio Girotto,Willie Aboumrad,Jonas Alm,Julia Kompalla,Mena Issler,Ananth Kaushik,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子优化框架解决电动货运物流中的货物选择问题，使用Iterative-QAOA算法在混合工作流中实现高达12%的交付量提升和6%的行驶距离减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电动货运物流中因随机货物取消导致的空闲时间间隙问题，这些间隙降低了车队利用率和收入，需要解决具有二次间隙依赖关系的组合分配问题。

Method: 将货物选择问题建模为混合整数二次规划，映射到Ising成本哈密顿量，使用Iterative-QAOA（量子近似优化算法的非变分预热启动扩展）求解，采用固定线性斜坡参数调度，构建端到端混合工作流集成经典车辆路径规划求解器。

Result: 在真实匿名物流数据上评估（最多130量子比特），当量子分配作为预热启动传递给经典求解器时，混合工作流在特定实例中实现高达12%的交付量提升和高达6%的每货物总行驶距离减少，总运营成本基本保持不变。

Conclusion: Iterative-QAOA能够生成兼容性感知的分配方案，当嵌入混合物流优化工作流时具有操作价值，展示了量子优化在现实物流问题中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a quantum optimization framework for the Shipment Selection Problem (SSP) in electric freight logistics, developed jointly by IonQ and Einride. Idle gaps arising from stochastic shipment cancellations reduce fleet utilization and revenue; filling them optimally requires solving a combinatorial assignment problem with quadratic inter-gap dependencies. We formulate the SSP as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program, map it to an Ising cost Hamiltonian, and solve it using Iterative-QAOA, a non-variational warm-start extension of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) with a fixed linear-ramp parameter schedule. An end-to-end hybrid workflow integrates Einride's vehicle routing problem (VRP) solver with IonQ's quantum simulations, enabling evaluation on real, anonymized logistics data spanning up to 130 qubits. We assess solution quality through application-level performance metrics, including Shipments Delivered (SD), Schedule Compatibility Score (SCS), and Total Drive Distance (TDD). When the quantum assignment is passed to the classical solver as a warm start, the resulting hybrid workflow achieves improvements of up to 12\% in SD and a reduction of up to 6\% in total drive distance per shipment for specific instances, while total operational cost remains effectively unchanged. These results show that Iterative-QAOA can generate compatibility-aware assignments that become operationally valuable when embedded in a hybrid logistics optimization workflow.

</details>


### [163] [Measuring what matters: A scalable framework for application-level quantum benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11781)
*Willie Aboumrad,Claudio Girotto,Joshua Goings,Luning Zhao,Miguel Angel Lopez-Ruiz,Daiwei Zhu,Ananth Kaushik,Sayonee Ray,Samwel Sekwao,Jason Iaconis,Andrew Arrasmith,Andrii Maksymov,Yvette de Sereville,Felix Tripier,Far McKon,Coleman Collins,Evgeny Epifanovsky,Masako Yamada,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的应用级量子基准测试框架，支持系统内部评估和跨平台比较，包含13个基准测试族，评估解决方案质量、执行时间、能耗和求解时间等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算系统成熟，需要能够捕捉有意义应用级性能指标的基准测试方法，以弥合低级性能指标与实际应用价值之间的差距。

Method: 设计基于可测量性、简单性、可扩展性和可扩展性核心原则的框架，开发13个反映多领域实际工作负载的基准测试族，支持解决方案质量、总执行时间、总能耗和求解时间等系统评估。

Result: 创建了一个可复现、跨利益相关者可解释、适应系统能力演进的基准测试框架，为行业基准测试标准奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了评估量子系统的统一方法，支持开发和验证，有助于建立行业范围的基准测试标准。

Abstract: As quantum computing systems continue to mature, there is an increasing need for benchmarking methodologies that capture performance in terms of meaningful, application-level metrics. In this work, we present a scalable framework for application-level quantum benchmarking that is designed to support internal system evaluation and cross-platform comparison across technology providers. Our framework is guided by a set of core principles, including measurability, simplicity, scalability, and extensibility. We present 13 benchmark families that reflect realistic workloads across multiple domains. This enables the systematic evaluation of the quality of solutions, the total execution time, total used energy, as well as Time-to-Solution. The benchmarks are designed to be reproducible, interpretable across stakeholder groups, and adaptable to evolving system capabilities. The framework aims to bridge the gap between low-level performance metrics and real-world value, providing a unified approach to assessing quantum systems. The resulting benchmarks support development and validation and contribute to the foundation of industry-wide benchmarking standards.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Proof of entropic order in Generalized Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09768)
*Enrico Andriolo,Mendel Nguyen,Emily Richards,Tin Sulejmanpasic*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了广义伊辛模型在p≥1时存在任意高温下的有序现象（熵有序），并揭示这些模型可解决图包装问题，特别是最大独立集优化问题，导致某些晶格系统出现玻璃相（熵玻璃）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义伊辛模型在p≥1时是否存在任意高温下的有序现象（熵有序），并探索这些模型与图包装问题的联系，特别是最大独立集优化问题。

Method: 采用严格的数学证明方法，分析参数化广义伊辛模型（参数为实数p），证明当p≥1时存在任意高温下的有序现象。进一步研究这些模型在任意图上的行为，建立其与图包装问题的联系。

Result: 1. 严格证明了当p≥1时，广义伊辛模型存在任意高温下的有序现象（熵有序）；2. 证明了这些模型在任意图上可解决图包装问题，特别是最大独立集优化问题；3. 由于最大独立集问题在一般图上是NP难的，某些晶格系统将表现出玻璃相，作者称之为"熵玻璃"。

Conclusion: 广义伊辛模型在p≥1时确实存在任意高温下的有序现象（熵有序），这些模型与图包装问题有深刻联系，能够解决最大独立集优化问题。由于该问题的计算复杂性，某些系统将表现出玻璃相，即"熵玻璃"现象。

Abstract: Ordering at arbitrarily high temperature - entropic order - has been argued to take place in a class of generalized Ising models parameterised by a real interaction parameter $p$ when $p\ge 1$. We give a rigorous proof of this conjecture. We further show that on arbitrary graphs, these models solve graph packing problems - crucially, the Maximum Independent Set optimisation problem. Due to the NP-hardness of this packing problem on generic graphs, some lattice systems will exhibit glassy phases. We call this phenomenon $entropic$ $glass$.

</details>


### [165] [Universality and ambiguity in extremes of anomalous diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10004)
*Sean D Lawley*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究多随机搜索者中最快搜索者找到目标的极端首次通过时间(fFPT)，探讨其在有界和无界速度扩散模型中的普遍性特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究显示fFPT随搜索者数量对数衰减且亚扩散可能比正常扩散更快，但这些结论基于无界速度模型，与物理系统实际不符。本文旨在验证这些特征在有界速度模型中的普遍性

Method: 研究包括尺度布朗运动、Riemann-Liouville分数布朗运动和分数布朗运动在内的多种异常和正常扩散模型，分析有界和无界速度情况下的fFPT行为

Result: 在所有模型中，fFPT随搜索者数量对数衰减，亚扩散确实可以比正常扩散更快，超扩散可能比正常扩散更慢，表明这些特征具有普遍性

Conclusion: 虽然(i)对数衰减和(ii)亚扩散更快这两个特征具有普遍性，但其适用的参数范围取决于具体模型，因此在特定物理系统中的相关性仍存在模糊性

Abstract: Many biophysical processes begin when the fastest searcher finds a target out of many random searchers, which is called an extreme or fastest first passage time (fFPT). In some models, (i) the fFPT vanishes logarithmically as the number of searchers grows, and (ii) the fFPT can be faster for subdiffusive search compared to normal diffusion. Though mathematically rigorous, the relevance of (i) and (ii) to actual physical systems is suspect since their derivations involve searchers which move with unbounded speed. Indeed, we previously proved that the fFPT for searchers with bounded speed converges exponentially to a strictly positive minimal search time as the number of searchers grows. In this paper, we study fFPTs for a broad class of anomalous and normal diffusion models with bounded or unbounded speed. These models include scaled Brownian motion, Riemann-Liouville fractional Brownian motion, and fractional Brownian motion. For all of these models, we show that the fFPT decays logarithmically in the number of searchers and that subdiffusion can be faster than normal diffusion (we further show that superdiffusion can be slower than normal diffusion). In this sense, features (i) and (ii) are rather universal. On the other hand, we show that the parameter regimes in which (i) and (ii) are valid depend on the particulars of the individual model, and thus ambiguities remain in the relevance of these features to specific physical systems.

</details>


### [166] [Spectral Softening and the Structural Breakdown of Thermodynamic Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10216)
*Ilki Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在驱动二次哈密顿系统中，当软模频率趋于零时，即使驱动无限缓慢，热力学可逆性也会失效，因为平衡系综变得无定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学认为，在足够缓慢的驱动下，系统会经历一系列平衡态的可逆演化。本文旨在探究这一预期在二次哈密顿系统接近谱简并时的有效性。

Method: 通过分析驱动二次哈密顿系统，研究软模频率趋于零时的动力学行为。使用Wigner相空间表示及其经典对应，揭示奇异性结构，证明这一限制并非量子特有，而是源于哈密顿相空间几何。

Result: 当软模频率低于与驱动相关的有限阈值时，绝热跟随失效，即使驱动无限缓慢。正则配分函数发散，平衡系综无定义，导致准静态热力学过程的基础消失。这种失效源于谱软化，而非无界哈密顿量或临界慢化。

Conclusion: 热力学可逆性在谱软化移除固有频率尺度时会受到根本性限制，这是平衡态失效的直接后果。这一限制不仅存在于量子系统，也源于经典哈密顿相空间几何结构。

Abstract: Under sufficiently slow driving, thermodynamics predicts reversible evolution through a sequence of equilibrium states. We show that this expectation fails near spectral degeneracy in driven quadratic Hamiltonian systems. As the soft-mode frequency collapses, the intrinsic dynamical timescale diverges and quadratic confinement is lost, leading to a breakdown of timescale separation and the failure of adiabatic following even under arbitrarily slow driving. More precisely, adiabaticity is lost once the soft-mode frequency falls below a finite, drive-dependent threshold, implying that the breakdown extends over a finite regime rather than being confined to a singular limit. Crucially, this dynamical instability is accompanied by a divergence of the canonical partition function, rendering equilibrium ensembles ill-defined and eliminating the foundation of quasistatic thermodynamic processes. This breakdown does not arise from unbounded Hamiltonians or critical slowing down, but emerges structurally from spectral softening within a bounded quadratic system. Analysis of the Wigner phase-space representation, together with its classical counterpart, reveals the same singular structure, demonstrating that this limitation is not uniquely quantum but originates from the underlying Hamiltonian phase-space geometry. These results show that thermodynamic reversibility is fundamentally constrained, as a direct consequence of the breakdown of equilibrium, whenever spectral softening removes an intrinsic frequency scale.

</details>


### [167] [Algorithmic overlaps as thermodynamic variables: from local to cluster Monte Carlo dynamics in critical phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10254)
*Ian Pilé,Youjin Deng,Lev Shchur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究三种蒙特卡洛算法（Metropolis、Swendsen-Wang、Wolff）在伊辛模型和三态Potts模型中连续自旋构型空间重叠的动力学行为，发现簇算法的重叠行为可作为算法动力学的序参数，与Fortuin-Kastelein簇的临界行为相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同蒙特卡洛算法（局部Metropolis算法与Swendsen-Wang、Wolff簇算法）在临界区域中连续自旋构型空间重叠的动力学特性，理解算法动力学与相变热力学之间的关系。

Method: 使用三种蒙特卡洛算法（局部Metropolis算法、Swendsen-Wang簇算法、Wolff簇算法）对两个不同普适类模型（伊辛模型和三态Potts模型）进行模拟，分析连续自旋构型的空间重叠行为。特别关注Wolff算法中连续簇的重叠以及Swendsen-Wang算法中连续晶格构型重叠的变化。

Result: Wolff算法中连续簇的重叠表现出临界行为，可作为算法动力学的序参数。Swendsen-Wang算法中连续晶格构型重叠的变化也表现出类似序参数的行为。Metropolis算法未观察到类似现象，其临界区域动力学由自旋翻转频率（即接受率）决定。这些几何量（构型重叠或其变化）反映了相变的热力学特性。

Conclusion: 簇算法（Wolff和Swendsen-Wang）中连续构型的重叠行为与Fortuin-Kastelein簇的临界行为自然相关，可作为算法动力学的序参数，而Metropolis算法则没有这种关联。几何量（构型重叠或其变化）能够反映相变的热力学特性。

Abstract: We investigate the spatial overlap of successive spin configurations in Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations using the local Metropolis algorithm and the Swendsen-Wang and Wolff cluster algorithms. We examine the dynamics of these algorithms for two models in different universality classes: the Ising model and the Potts model with three components. The overlap of two successive Wolff clusters reflects critical behavior and can be used as an order parameter for the algorithm's dynamics. In the case of the Swendsen-Wang algorithm, similar behavior is demonstrated by the variation in the overlap of two consecutive lattice configurations, which behaves like order parameter. Nothing similar is observed for the Metropolis algorithm, and the dynamics in the critical region are determined by the spin flip frequency, which is equivalent to the acceptance rate. Thus, the critical behavior of Wolff cluster overlap and the variation of configuration overlap in the Swendsen-Wang algorithm are naturally related to the critical behavior of geometric objects - Fortuin-Kastelein clusters. Interestingly, in all cases, the geometric quantity - configuration overlap or its variation - reflects the thermodynamics of the phase transition.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Whittle: exact finite-time multispectral statistics from a single Brownian trajectory in a harmonic trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10323)
*Isaac Pérez Castillo,François Leyvraz,Miguel Eduardo Gómez Quintanar,Andrés Álvarez Ballesteros*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文开发了过阻尼布朗粒子在谐振势中的精确有限时间多谱理论，提供了谱估计器的联合分布表征，并建立了考虑频率间相关性的谱似然层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实验中只能获得单个有限记录，导致谱估计器分布广泛，且通常的频率独立性假设不成立。需要发展有限时间谱理论来超越渐近Whittle框架。

Method: 针对过阻尼布朗粒子在谐振势中的系统，推导了频率集合上有限时间估计器的精确联合分布，建立了具有显式协方差的傅里叶投影高斯表示，并构建了从因子化Whittle近似到块状协方差感知近似的谱似然层次结构。

Result: 获得了有限时间谱估计器的精确理论表征，揭示了观测窗口引起的频率间相关性如何随T→∞而消失。蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论，并量化了忽略交叉频率相关性对单轨迹参数估计的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为超越渐近区域的谱推断提供了受控的有限时间基准，展示了如何通过考虑频率间相关性改进单轨迹谱分析。

Abstract: Power spectral densities are often interpreted through ensemble averages and long-time asymptotics. In many experiments, however, only a single finite record is available, so spectral estimators remain broadly distributed and the usual independence assumptions across frequencies need not hold. Here we develop an exact finite-$T$ multispectral theory for an overdamped Brownian particle in a harmonic trap. For a collection of frequencies $\{ω_i\}$, we obtain an exact characterization of the joint law of the finite-time estimators $\{S(ω_i,T)\}$, together with a covariance-explicit Gaussian representation for the associated Fourier projections. This representation makes the observation-window-induced inter-frequency correlations explicit and shows how they vanish as $T\to\infty$, thereby recovering the asymptotic Whittle picture. We then use this structure to formulate a hierarchy of spectral likelihoods for inference from a single trajectory, ranging from the factorized Whittle approximation to blockwise covariance-aware approximations in frequency space. Monte Carlo simulations validate the finite-time theory and quantify the effect of neglected cross-frequency correlations on single-trajectory estimates of the trap parameters. Our results provide a controlled finite-time benchmark for spectral inference beyond the asymptotic regime.

</details>


### [169] [Heat Conduction in Momentum-Conserving Fluids: From quasi-2D to 3D systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10536)
*Rongxiang Luo,Jiaqi Wen,Juncheng Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过非平衡和平衡分子动力学模拟研究多粒子碰撞动力学模型中的热传导，识别出三种输运机制：弹道、动力学和流体动力学机制，揭示了从准二维到三维系统的维度交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准二维到三维系统中热传导的基本机制，特别是动量守恒介观流体中的输运行为，这对于理解微纳米尺度热器件设计中的热管理至关重要。

Method: 采用非平衡和平衡分子动力学模拟，使用多粒子碰撞动力学模型研究介观流体系统，分析热导率与系统尺寸的标度关系以及热流自相关函数的时间演化。

Result: 识别出三种输运机制：弹道机制（κ∼L，C(t)常数）、动力学机制（κ与尺寸无关，C(t)指数衰减）和流体动力学机制（准二维系统κ∼ln L，C(t)∼t⁻¹；三维系统κ有限，C(t)∼t⁻³/²），验证了流体动力学标度预测并揭示了从二维类反常输运到三维傅里叶行为的维度交叉。

Conclusion: 研究为准二维到三维系统的热输运提供了理论基础，揭示了热传导机制的维度依赖性，对微纳米尺度热器件的设计具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Using nonequilibrium and equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we investigate heat conduction in a momentum-conserving mesoscopic fluid modeled by multiparticle collision dynamics. Across quasi-two-dimensional (q-2D) to three-dimensional (3D) systems, we identify three distinct transport regimes: (i) a \emph{ballistic regime}, where thermal conductivity scales linearly with system size ($κ\sim L$) and the total heat current autocorrelation function $C(t)$ remains constant; (ii)~a \emph{kinetic regime}, characterized by size-independent $κ$ and exponentially decaying $C(t)$, demonstrating that normal heat conduction dominated by kinetic effects is far more ubiquitous than previously observed in 1D systems; and (iii)~a \emph{hydrodynamic regime}, where the q-2D system exhibits logarithmically divergent conductivity ($ κ\sim \ln L $ ) with $ C(t) \sim t^{-1} $ , while the 3D system displays finite $ κ$ and $ C(t) \sim t^{-3/2} $. Our results, observed in the hydrodynamic regime, quantitatively validate the scaling predictions for heat transport and reveal a clear dimensional crossover -- from 2D-like anomalous transport to 3D Fourier behavior. These results lay a foundation for understanding thermal transport in q-2D to 3D systems and have practical implications for the design of micro- and nanoscale thermal devices.

</details>


### [170] [Location of the liquid-vapor critical point in aluminum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10561)
*Xuyang Long,Kai Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合深度势能分子动力学和大规模模拟，首次精确确定了铝的液-汽临界点参数，解决了数十年来临界温度范围达4000K的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铝的液-汽临界点位置数十年来一直存在巨大不确定性，报告的临界温度范围近4000K，这种不确定性限制了在激光烧蚀、冲击压缩和行星建模等极端条件下的应用。

Method: 结合深度势能分子动力学和大规模模拟，基于高保真电子结构数据进行训练；通过比较多种交换关联泛函与实验液体密度，确定PBEsol提供最一致的描述；采用互补方法：状态方程的旋节线分析和具有高斯混合相识别的直接共存模拟。

Result: 收敛得到临界温度6531-6576K，临界密度0.637g/cm³，临界压力1.6kbar，温度不确定性约50K，精度相比之前估计有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究框架建立了预测金属临界现象的可转移策略，对激光烧蚀、冲击压缩和极端条件下的行星建模具有重要意义。

Abstract: The precise location of the liquid-vapor critical point (CP) in aluminum has remained elusive for decades, with reported critical temperatures spanning nearly 4000~K. Here we resolve this long-standing uncertainty by combining deep potential molecular dynamics with large-scale simulations trained on high-fidelity electronic-structure data. We benchmark multiple exchange-correlation functionals against experimental liquid densities and identify PBEsol as providing the most consistent description. Using complementary approaches -- spinodal analysis of the equation of state and direct coexistence simulations with Gaussian mixture phase identification -- we converge on a critical temperature of $6531$-$6576$~K, a critical density of $0.637$~g/cm$^{3}$, and a critical pressure of $1.6$~kbar. The precision of these values, with uncertainties of $\sim$50~K in temperature, represents a step change over previous estimates. Our framework establishes a transferable strategy for predicting critical phenomena in metals, with implications for laser ablation, shock compression, and planetary modeling under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [171] [Confined kinetics and heterogeneous diffusion driven by fractional Gaussian noise: A path integral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10663)
*David Santiago Quevedo,Felipe Segundo Abril-Bermúdez,Cristiane Morais Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文从路径积分角度研究由分数高斯噪声驱动的扩散问题，噪声具有长程相关性和状态依赖的乘性耦合特性，推导了基于Lamperti变换的高斯传播子，并分析了在Feynman-Kac框架下过程从属化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多复杂系统由Langevin型方程描述，其中噪声表现出长程相关性并以状态依赖的乘性方式与系统耦合，导致异质非马尔可夫扩散。需要从路径积分角度研究这种由分数高斯噪声驱动的扩散问题。

Method: 采用路径积分方法，使用平稳相位近似推导出基于过程Lamperti变换的高斯传播子。在Feynman-Kac框架下分析过程的从属化效应，并开发推导有效局部哈密顿量动力学方程的一般程序。

Result: 在加性极限下，结果恢复了基于Riemann-Liouville公式的分数布朗运动路径积分表示，并建立了其与Langevin构造的等价性。揭示了乘性扩散与约束之间的相互作用诱导出有效漂移项，导致概率在低噪声振幅区域积累。

Conclusion: 通过路径积分方法成功处理了具有长程相关性和乘性耦合的分数高斯噪声扩散问题，建立了不同表示之间的等价关系，并揭示了乘性扩散与约束相互作用的重要物理效应。

Abstract: Many complex systems are described by Langevin-type equations in which the noise exhibits long-range correlations and couples to the system in a state-dependent, multiplicative manner, leading to heterogeneous non-Markovian diffusion. Here, we investigate the problem of diffusion driven by fractional Gaussian noise with a general multiplicative coefficient from a path-integral perspective. Using a stationary-phase approximation, we derive a Gaussian propagator expressed in terms of the Lamperti transform of the process. In the additive limit, our results recover the path-integral representation of fractional Brownian motion based on its Riemann-Liouville formulation and establish its equivalence with the Langevin construction. We further analyze the effect of subordinating the process to a killing rate within the Feynman-Kac framework, and develop a general procedure to derive kinetic equations in terms of effective local Hamiltonians. We show that the interplay between multiplicative diffusion and confinement induces an effective drift term, leading to probability accumulation in regions of low noise amplitude.

</details>


### [172] [Forecasting Return Time of Extreme Precipitation by Large Deviation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10890)
*Haotian Xie,Haoxian Liu,Jingfang Fan,Ying Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 开发大偏差框架预测极端降水重现期，发现Landau分布比传统极值分布更准确，结合CMIP6预测显示21世纪出生人群面临急剧增加的极端降水暴露风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端降水预报至关重要但极具挑战性，因其罕见性和复杂性。传统方法在预测超越历史观测强度的极端事件时存在局限性，需要更准确的统计框架来估计重现期并评估未来气候变化下的风险。

Method: 1. 开发大偏差框架估计极端降水事件的重现期；2. 发现Landau分布（源自等离子体物理）能更准确地捕捉全球约93%位置的极端降水，优于传统极值分布（仅匹配76%位置）；3. 通过拟合的Landau分布丰富罕见事件样本，获得更准确的大偏差率函数和重现期估计；4. 将历史重现期映射到CMIP6未来预测，分析不同排放情景下的风险变化。

Result: 1. Landau分布在93%的全球位置准确捕捉极端降水，显著优于传统极值分布；2. 基于Landau分布的大偏差框架能更准确地估计超越历史观测强度的极端降水重现期；3. 不同排放情景下的重现期曲线收敛到统一关系；4. 在大多数未来排放情景下，21世纪出生人群的极端降水终身暴露风险急剧增加。

Conclusion: Landau分布为极端降水统计建模提供了更准确的框架，大偏差方法能有效预测超越历史观测的极端事件。结合CMIP6预测表明，气候变化将显著增加未来人群的极端降水暴露风险，不同排放情景下的统一关系揭示了气候系统的内在响应模式。

Abstract: Forecasting extreme precipitation is essential yet challenging due to its rarity and complexity. We develop a large deviation framework to estimate the return times of extreme precipitation events. We first find that the Landau distribution, originally introduced in plasma physics, accurately captures extreme precipitation at approximately 93% of global locations, outperforming conventional extreme value distributions with 76% matched locations under the same accuracy criterion. Enriching rare event samples by the fitted Landau distribution, we obtain more accurate estimates of large deviation rate functions and return times, enabling forecasts beyond historically observed precipitation intensities. Mapping historical return times to future projections from the Coupled Model Intercomparison Project Phase 6 (CMIP6), we show that return time curves under different emission scenarios collapse onto a unified relation, revealing a sharply increased lifetime exposure to extreme precipitation for 21st-century birth cohorts under most future emission scenarios.

</details>


### [173] [Dynamical Regimes of Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10961)
*Tomoei Takahashi,Takashi Takahashi,Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究将连续扩散模型中发现的物种形成和坍缩转变理论框架扩展到离散扩散模型，通过二类伊辛变量数据的统计力学分析验证了这些转变在离散数据中的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已在连续数据扩散模型中发现了物种形成和坍缩两种特征转变，但相同理论标准尚未应用于离散扩散模型。本研究旨在验证这些理论框架是否适用于离散数据，为离散生成扩散的统计力学分析提供基础。

Method: 提出一个简单的有效模型，用于分析在具有一般混合比例的二类伊辛变量数据上训练的离散扩散模型。使用统计力学方法（包括高温展开和随机能量模型）分析反向动力学，通过数值模拟和真实数据集上的训练离散扩散模型实验验证理论预测。

Result: 研究表明，与连续数据情况类似，物种形成转变可通过高温展开的二阶相变分析确定，而坍缩转变对应随机能量模型描述的凝聚转变。获得了物种形成时间的解析表达式，并证明当噪声随时间增加时，其标度行为与连续情况一致。数值模拟和真实数据集实验证实了这些理论预测。

Conclusion: 原始连续数据理论框架在离散数据中仍然有效，这为在更现实设置下进行离散生成扩散的统计力学分析提供了有用的起点。研究结果表明扩散模型中的特征转变具有普适性，跨越了连续和离散数据类型的界限。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data such as images by learning a process that gradually removes noise from corrupted data. Recent studies have shown that the backward dynamics of diffusion models exhibit two characteristic transitions: the speciation transition, at which generated samples begin to capture the global structure of the training data, and the collapse transition, at which the generation dynamics starts committing to individual training samples. While these transitions have been theoretically analyzed for continuous data, the same theoretical criteria have not been applied for discrete diffusion models, which are diffusion models for discrete data. In this work, we propose a simple effective model for discrete diffusion models trained on two-class Ising variable data with a general mixture ratio and analyze its backward dynamics using methods from statistical mechanics. We show that, as in the previous study on continuous data, the speciation transition can be determined through a second-order phase transition analysis using high-temperature expansion, while the collapse transition corresponds to a condensation transition described by the Random Energy Model. An analytical expression for the speciation time is obtained, and we show that its scaling becomes consistent with that of the continuous case when the noise increases with time as in practical diffusion models. These theoretical predictions are confirmed by numerical simulations and experiments with trained discrete diffusion models on real datasets. These results suggest that the original theoretical framework for continuous data remain valid for discrete data, and may provide a useful starting point for the statistical-mechanics analysis of discrete generative diffusion in more realistic settings.

</details>


### [174] [Scar subspaces stabilized by algebraic closure: Beyond equally-spaced spectra and exact solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11015)
*Chihiro Matsui*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一类具有𝔰𝔲(3)不变疤痕子空间的量子多体系统，扩展了传统量子多体疤痕范式，超越了等间距谱和单向塔结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子多体疤痕研究主要基于𝔰𝔲(2)代数结构，具有等间距能谱和单向塔结构。该工作旨在超越这一限制，探索更丰富的疤痕子空间结构和动力学行为。

Method: 基于局部约束构造量子多体系统，这些约束在疤痕子空间内实现代数闭包。代数闭包机制保证了子空间的不变性，即使系统整体不可精确求解。

Result: 构建了𝔰𝔲(3)不变疤痕子空间，其能谱不再等间距，而是形成由多个独立量子数参数化的多向晶格结构。动力学表现为多频振荡而非单频复苏。疤痕子空间的稳定性不依赖于本征态的可精确求解性。

Conclusion: 代数闭包是超越传统𝔰𝔲(2)范式的疤痕子空间的统一机制，为非可积量子系统中更丰富的非热动力学开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We construct a class of quantum many-body systems hosting an $\mathfrak{su}(3)$-invariant scar subspace, extending the conventional paradigm of quantum many-body scars beyond equally spaced spectra and single-directional tower structures. Our construction is based on local constraints that realize an algebraic closure within the scar subspace. As a result, the spectrum in the subspace is no longer equally spaced, but instead forms a multidirectional lattice structure parametrized by multiple independent quantum numbers. This leads to qualitatively new dynamical signatures: instead of single-frequency revivals, the system exhibits multifrequency oscillations governed by integer linear combinations of distinct energy scales. Importantly, the stability of the scar subspace does not rely on exact solvability of individual eigenstates. We show that algebraic closure preserves the invariant subspace even under perturbations that render the eigenstates analytically intractable, thereby realizing quantum many-body scars on an unsolvable reference state. Our results identify algebraic closure as a unifying mechanism underlying scar subspaces beyond the conventional $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ paradigm, and open a route toward richer nonthermal dynamics in nonintegrable quantum systems.

</details>


### [175] [Sluggish quantum mechanics of noninteracting fermions with spatially varying effective mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11173)
*Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar,Sanjib Sabhapandit*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维量子系统中位置依赖的有效质量（m_eff(x) ∝ |x|^α）导致的"迟缓量子力学"，精确求解无势场和谐振子势下的本征函数与传播子，分析N个无相互作用费米子在陷阱中的多体基态，发现关联核的新标度形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有位置依赖有效质量的量子系统，这种系统源于非均匀紧束缚模型中空间变化的跳跃振幅。这种"迟缓量子力学"框架中粒子运动在较大距离处逐渐被抑制，与工程光学晶格中位置依赖隧穿现象相关。

Method: 采用BenDaniel-Duke形式的薛定谔方程，有效质量按m_eff(x) = m_eff|x|^α标度。精确求解无外势和谐振子势V_ext(x) = ½m_effω²|x|^{α+2}下的本征函数和量子传播子。分析N个无相互作用自旋less费米子在陷阱中的多体基态波函数和位置联合概率密度函数。

Result: 多体基态量子概率密度形成行列式点过程，关联核可对任意N计算。大N极限下，标度平均密度分布在原点对称有限支撑上，但在原点处有非单调形状和消失最小值。关键发现：原点附近的标度核既不是贝塞尔核也不是艾里核，而是两个不同指标贝塞尔核的和，这是新的关联核形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有位置依赖隧穿的工程光学晶格提供了相关框架，揭示了迟缓量子力学系统中多体费米子统计的新特征，特别是原点附近关联核的新标度行为，与标准囚禁费米子情况不同。

Abstract: We analyze a class of one-dimensional quantum systems characterized by a position-dependent kinetic term arising as the continuum limit of an inhomogeneous tight-binding model with spatially varying hopping amplitudes. In this limit, the Schrodinger equation takes the so-called BenDaniel-Duke form with an effective mass, scaling as $m_{eff}(x) = m_{eff}|x|^α$ with $α> 0$, leading to a framework we term sluggish quantum mechanics, where particle motion is progressively suppressed at larger distances. Both without any external potential and with $V_{ext}(x)=\frac{1}{2}m_{eff}ω^2 |x|^{α+2}$, we obtain the eigenfunctions and the quantum propagators exactly. We then investigate the problem of $N$ noninteracting spinless fermions in the trap, determining the many-body ground-state wavefunction and the joint probability density function of the positions of the $N$ fermions. We show that the many-body quantum probability density in the ground state forms a determinantal point process whose correlation kernel can be computed for any $N$, giving access to the average density as well as higher order correlation functions for any finite $N$. Moreover, we analyze the scaling form of this kernel in the large $N$ limit in the bulk, near the edge, and close to the origin. Our results show that the scaled average density profile for large $N$ has a finite support symmetric with respect to the origin, but has a non-monotonic shape with a vanishing minimum at the origin for any $α>0$. One of the key findings of our work is that the scaled kernel near the origin $x=0$ for $α>0$ is neither the Bessel nor the Airy kernel (that are standard for trapped fermions), but is new, and is given by a sum of two Bessel kernels with different indices. Our results thus provide a framework relevant to engineered optical lattices with position-dependent tunneling.

</details>


### [176] [Absence of thermalization after a local quench and strong violation of the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11392)
*Peter Reimann,Christian Eidecker-Dunkel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究了局部量子淬火后系统的非热化现象，发现在XX自旋链模型中，即使只改变单个自旋杂质（末端或中心），系统也会违反本征态热化假设，导致无法达到热平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部量子淬火后系统的热化行为，探索在可积系统中，即使只进行局部扰动而非全局淬火，系统是否仍会违反本征态热化假设并表现出非热化现象。

Method: 采用解析分析和数值模拟相结合的方法。解析研究聚焦于开放边界条件的XX自旋链模型，通过突然开启或微调单个自旋杂质（位于链末端或中心）来产生局部淬火。数值研究扩展到更一般的XXZ模型，比较末端杂质和中心杂质的不同效应。

Result: 发现局部淬火后系统确实存在非热化现象，这对应于对本征态热化假设的强烈违反（甚至弱本征态热化假设也不满足）。在XX模型中，末端和中心杂质都导致非热化；在XXZ模型中，末端杂质产生类似现象，但中心杂质则不会。

Conclusion: 局部量子淬火足以导致可积系统的非热化，这表明非热化现象不仅限于全局淬火，局部扰动也能产生类似效应。不同杂质位置对热化行为的影响存在模型依赖性，末端杂质比中心杂质更容易破坏热化。

Abstract: Absence of thermalization after a global quantum quench is a well-established numerical observation in integrable many-body systems, and can be empirically related to a violation of the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) in such models. Still, in many of those examples a weaker version of the conventional ETH (wETH) has been numerically reported or even rigorously proven. In this paper we show analytically and illustrate numerically that the absence of thermalization is already possible after a local quench. A closely related finding is a strong violation of the ETH, meaning that not even the wETH is fulfilled anymore. In our analytical explorations we focus on XX-spin-chain models with open boundary conditions, where the local quench is generated by initiating the system in thermal equilibrium and then suddenly switching on (or slightly changing) a single-spin impurity either at the end or in the center of the chain. Numerically we observe qualitatively similar phenomena also for more general XXZ-models in the case of an end-impurity, but not in the case of a central impurity.

</details>


### [177] [Exact Criterion for Ground-State Overlap Dominance after Quantum Quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11420)
*Taisanul Haque*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文精确解决了自由费米子系统中量子淬火后基态重叠最大化的问题，证明了先前猜想在某些情况下成立，但在一般情况（如Kitaev链）下不成立，并揭示了相同相区内淬火也能产生动力学量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前在TIFM模型中提出并验证了一个猜想：在相同物理相区内进行突然淬火时，初始基态与最终本征态的重叠在最终基态处达到最大。本文旨在精确解决这一普遍性问题，验证猜想的适用范围并揭示其局限性。

Method: 作者对一类平移不变的自由费米子系统进行精确求解。对于可分解为独立2×2扇区的哈密顿量，推导了最终基态是唯一最大重叠态的充要条件：初始和最终扇区的布洛赫向量点积为正。通过分析Kitaev链等具体模型，检验该判据的普适性。

Result: 精确判据表明：对于大量系统类别，猜想成立；但在一般情况（如Kitaev链）下，存在相同相区内的淬火，其最终基态并非最大重叠态。这一机制还导致了一个直接的动力学后果：相同相区内的淬火也能产生动力学量子相变，而无需跨越物理相边界。

Conclusion: 论文精确解决了自由费米子系统中量子淬火后基态重叠最大化的问题，证明了先前猜想在某些情况下成立，但在一般情况（如Kitaev链）下不成立。这一发现不仅澄清了猜想的适用范围，还揭示了相同相区内淬火也能产生动力学量子相变的新物理机制。

Abstract: Recently, conjectured and verified in TIFM model that for a sudden quench within the same physical phase region, the overlap of the initial ground state with the final eigenstates is maximal for the final ground state. We solve this problem exactly for a broad class of translationally invariant free-fermion systems. For Hamiltonians that factorize into independent $2\times2$ sectors, the final ground state is uniquely maximal if and only if the initial and final sector Bloch vectors have positive dot product. This exact criterion proves the conjecture for large classes, but also shows that it is false in general: in Kitaev chains there are same-phase quenches for which the final ground state is not the maximal-overlap state. The same mechanism has a direct dynamical consequence, implying that same-phase quenches can generate DQPTs without crossing an physical phase boundary.

</details>


### [178] [Optimization of cooling power of a thermoelectric refrigerator: A unified approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11441)
*Rajeshree Chakraborty,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文分析了热电制冷器冷却功率优化的稳态形式，将内可逆和外可逆近似统一在一个框架内，推导出依赖于热电优值和内外热导比值的性能系数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内可逆模型基于牛顿传热定律时冷却功率无法优化，需要解决这一问题，特别是在近可逆区域，同时考虑内部和外部不可逆性对热电制冷器性能的影响。

Method: 建立统一的稳态形式框架，分析近可逆区域（外部热导大但有限）的冷却功率优化，推导包含内部和外部不可逆性的性能系数表达式，将内可逆和外可逆模型作为特例。

Result: 在近可逆区域可以规避内可逆模型的优化问题，推导出依赖于热电优值和内外热导比值的性能系数闭式表达式，对于小温差，组合不可逆性将性能系数降低到1/2以下，与单级热电制冷器观测性能一致。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够提供热电制冷器性能系数的现实估计，内可逆和外可逆模型是其特例，对于小温差情况，组合不可逆性显著降低性能系数，这与实验观测相符。

Abstract: We analyze the steady-state formalism for optimizing the cooling power of a thermoelectric refrigerator (TER), unifying the endoreversible and exoreversible approximations within one framework. Although the cooling power is non-optimizable within the endoreversible model based on Newtonian heat-transfer law, we show that the issue can be circumvented in the near-reversible regime where the external thermal conductances are large, but finite. We extend this analysis to optimize the cooling power in the presence of both internal and external irreversibilities and derive a closed-form expression for the coefficient of performance (COP) that depends on the thermoelectric figure of merit and the ratio of internal to external thermal conductances. The model reproduces the endoreversible and the exoreversible limits as special cases. We conclude that for small temperature differences, the combined irreversibilities reduce the COP to values below 1/2, which aligns with the observed performance of the single-stage TER, and can provide realistic estimates for the COP.

</details>


### [179] [Inverse engineering of cooling protocols: from normal behavior to Mpemba effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11486)
*Hartmut Löwen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究如何通过逆向工程外部温度协议T_ext(t)来实现预设的内部冷却曲线T_int(t)，而非传统的外部温度变化如何影响内部温度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究关注给定外部温度协议T_ext(t)如何影响系统冷却行为T_int(t)，本文考虑逆问题：对于给定的系统冷却曲线T_int(t)，如何设计合适的外部温度协议T_ext(t)来实现预设的冷却行为。

Method: 使用现象学牛顿冷却方程和微观模型（离散二能级系统和布朗谐振子含时噪声模型）解析计算实现预设T_int(t)所需的T_ext(t)协议；同时研究包含异常姆潘巴效应、过冷、冷却加热不对称性及延迟现象的现象学广义牛顿定律。

Result: 逆向工程协议并不总是存在且可能非唯一；建立了通过时变外部热源系统性地调控冷却行为的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为通过时间变化的外部热源系统性地调控冷却行为提供了重要理论基础，揭示了逆向工程协议的存在性和唯一性问题。

Abstract: When a cup of hot coffee is suddenly put into a cold environment, it cools down as a function of time $t$ until the internal temperature $T_\text{int}$ of the coffee equals the external ambient temperature $T_\text{ext}$. This instantaneous shock-freezing corresponds to an imposed cooling protocol of the external temperature $T_\text{ext}(t)$, ideally described as a step-function in time, causing the time-dependent change of the internal temperature $T_\text{int}(t)$. While the effect of different given protocols $T_\text{ext}(t)$ on the resulting system cooling behaviour, embodied in $T_\text{int}(t)$, has been studied extensively, we consider here the inverse question: for a given system cooling $T_\text{int}(t)$ how can an appropriate protocol $T_\text{ext}(t)$ be engineered to produce the desired prescribed $T_\text{int}(t)$. We use both the phenomenological Newtonian equation for cooling and microscopic models, such as a discrete two-level system and a Brownian harmonic oscillator with time-dependent noise, to compute analytically the protocol $T_\text{ext}(t)$ needed to achieve a prescribed $T_\text{int}(t)$. We then discuss the same question for phenomenological generalizations of the Newtonian law which include anomalous Mpemba effects, overcooling, asymmetries in cooling and heating as well as delay phenomena. It is shown that backward-engineered protocols do not always exist and can be non-unique. The results are important for steering the cooling behavior by time-varying external heat sources in a systematic way.

</details>


### [180] [The Widom line in the Ising model on a decorated bilayer lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11606)
*Joseph Chapman,Justas Gidziunas,Bruno Tomasello,Sam Carr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将一维受挫晶格模型扩展到二维装饰双层晶格，发现一维模型的伪相变在二维中变为真实的一阶相变，且在双临界点之上仍存在伪相变特征线（Widom线）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受挫一维统计力学晶格模型在二维扩展中的行为，特别是探索一维模型中观察到的伪相变在二维系统中的演化

Method: 将一维受挫模型扩展到二维装饰双层晶格，研究Ising模型在该二维结构上的热力学行为

Result: 一维模型的伪相变在二维装饰双层晶格中转变为真实的一阶相变；在双临界点之上仍存在伪相变特征，可表征为Widom线

Conclusion: 二维扩展使伪相变变为真实相变，Widom线的存在为重新解释先前研究的一维模型物理提供了新视角

Abstract: There has been much recent interest devoted to a class of frustrated one-dimensional statistical mechanics lattice models which exhibit sharp thermodynamics. In this work, we study an extension of one of these models to two dimensions; the Ising model on a decorated bilayer lattice. We show that the pseudo-transitions of the one-dimensional models become a real first order phase transition in this two-dimensional analogue. Moreover, the pseudo-transition is found to still exist above a bi-critical point. This can be characterised as a Widom line, which allows a re-interpretation of the physics in the previously studied one-dimensional models.

</details>


### [181] [Diffusing diffusivity model with dichotomous noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11800)
*Dongho Lee,Jae-Hyung Jeon,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究具有随机扩散系数的朗之万动力学，扩散系数由对称二分噪声驱动的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程建模，分析粒子位移的概率密度函数及其渐近行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由周围介质波动引起的随机扩散系数对朗之万动力学的影响，为有界或切换波动的环境中随机输运提供最小化可解析处理的框架

Method: 使用由对称二分噪声驱动的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程对随机扩散系数建模，推导粒子位移的短时概率密度函数解析表达式，分析其渐近行为，并研究时间平均随机扩散系数的方差和协方差

Result: 概率密度函数在原点保持特征性对数发散，但尾部与高斯白噪声情况不同：指数尾部被高斯尾部取代，并受切换速率相关幂律调制；长时间下动力学收敛于普通高斯扩散；时间平均随机扩散系数是自平均的

Conclusion: 该模型为有界或切换波动环境中的随机输运提供了一个最小化且可解析处理的框架，揭示了随机扩散系数对粒子位移分布的显著影响，特别是在短时尺度上尾部行为的改变

Abstract: We study Langevin dynamics with stochastic diffusivity arising from fluctuations of the surrounding medium. The diffusivity is modeled as Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by symmetric dichotomous noise, which confines it to a finite interval. We derive analytical expressions for the short-time probability density function (PDF) of the particle displacement and analyse its asymptotic behaviour. While the PDF retains the characteristic logarithmic divergence at the origin, its tails differ from the Gaussian white-noise case: exponential tails are replaced by Gaussian ones modulated by a power-law with a switching-rate-dependent exponent. At long times, the dynamics converges to ordinary Gaussian diffusion. We determine the variance and covariance of the time-averaged stochastic diffusivity and show that it is self-averaging. The model provides a minimal analytically tractable framework for stochastic transport in environments with bounded or switching fluctuations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Decoding Superconductivity in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_{7-δ}$ Thin Films via Ozone-Driven Structure and Oxidation Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09807)
*Mathieu Flavenot,Hoshang Sahib,Jérôme Robert,Marc Lenertz,Gilles Versini,Laurent Schlur,Alexandre Gloter,Nathalie Viart,Daniele Preziosi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过STEM和EELS技术系统分析了La₃Ni₂O₇-δ薄膜的结构与超导性能关系，揭示了氧化学计量均匀性、外延应变和结构基序对稳定镍酸盐超导相的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₃Ni₂O₇在高压下的超导发现挑战了镍酸盐超导仅限于3d⁹电子构型和平面配位的传统认知，但该相具有亚稳态特性，其稳定超导的结构和电子先决条件尚不明确，特别是在薄膜中实现常压超导的机制需要深入探究。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积在SrLaAlO₄衬底上生长La₃Ni₂O₇-δ薄膜，通过不同后退火条件调控性能，结合扫描透射电子显微镜(STEM)和电子能量损失谱(EELS)进行详细结构表征，将丰富的堆垛多型体与输运行为相关联。

Result: 薄膜表现出随后退火条件变化的超导特性，建立了堆垛多型体与输运行为的关联框架，明确了氧化学计量均匀性、外延应变和结构基序是稳定超导相的关键因素，为设计常压超导镍酸盐提供了清晰路径。

Conclusion: 该工作系统阐明了双层镍酸盐薄膜中亚稳态超导相的稳定机制，强调了结构均匀性和外延调控的重要性，为实现常压镍酸盐超导材料的设计提供了理论基础和实验指导。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in bulk Ruddlesden-Popper La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$(LNO327) under high hydrostatic pressure has redefined the recent experimental consensus that nickelate superconductivity is restricted to systems with a $3d^9$ electronic configuration and square-planar coordination. However, the structural and electronic prerequisites for stabilizing superconductivity, whether under pressure or at ambient conditions in the case of thin films, remain poorly understood, largely due to the metastable nature of the LNO327 phase. Here, we present a detailed structural study of epitaxial La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_{7-δ}$ thin films by using scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) combined with electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS). Grown via pulsed laser deposition onto SrLaAlO$_4$ substrates, those films exhibit distinct superconducting properties as a function of the different post-annealing conditions used. By correlating the rich landscape of stacking polymorphs with transport behavior, this work establishes a framework for understanding the metastable superconducting phase in bilayer nickelate thin films. Our findings underscore the critical role of homogeneity in oxygen stoichiometry, epitaxial strain and structural motif in stabilizing superconductivity, offering a clear pathway for designing ambient-pressure superconducting nickelates.

</details>


### [183] [Strong Electron-Phonon Coupling and Multiband Superconductivity in Hexagonal BP3 Monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10026)
*Jakkapat Seeyangnok,Udomsilp Pinsook*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算和各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论研究六方BP3单层膜的结构、电子和超导性质，发现其为强耦合多带二维超导体，Tc=9.7K


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低维系统中轨道杂化在电子-声子介导超导性中的作用，探索新型二维超导材料

Method: 采用第一性原理计算结合各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，包括结构优化、声子色散分析、从头算分子动力学模拟、电子能带结构计算和电子-声子耦合计算

Result: BP3单层膜具有稳定的微弯曲结构，呈现多带金属态，电子-声子耦合常数λ=1.59，超导转变温度Tc=9.7K，具有无节点但各向异性的能隙结构（2.25meV和1.74meV）

Conclusion: BP3单层膜被确认为强耦合多带二维超导体，研究结果为理解低维系统中轨道杂化在电子-声子介导超导性的作用提供了见解

Abstract: We investigate the structural, electronic, and superconducting properties of a hexagonal BP3 monolayer using first-principles calculations combined with anisotropic Migdal-Eliashberg theory. The optimized structure exhibits a stable, slightly buckled configuration, as confirmed by phonon dispersion analysis and ab initio molecular dynamics simulations. The phonon spectrum indicates high-frequency vibrational modes associated with B-P bonding. Electronic band structure calculations reveal a multiband metallic state, with states near the Fermi level predominantly derived from pz orbitals of both boron and phosphorus atoms, forming two distinct Fermi surface sheets. The electron-phonon coupling is relatively strong, with a total coupling constant of lambda = 1.59, dominated by low- and intermediate-frequency phonon modes. Solving the anisotropic Migdal-Eliashberg equations yields a superconducting transition temperature of Tc = 9.7 K. The superconducting state is characterized by a nodeless but anisotropic gap structure, exhibiting two distinct gap values of approximately 2.25 and 1.74 meV associated with different Fermi surface sheets. These results identify the BP3 monolayer as a strongly coupled, multiband two-dimensional superconductor and provide insight into the role of orbital hybridization in electron-phonon-mediated superconductivity in low-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [184] [Hidden Universal Metal in Cuprate Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10133)
*Abigail Lee,Juergen Haase*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文基于铜氧化物超导体中平面Cu和O核弛豫数据，提出了一种简单的唯象描述，揭示了普遍金属态与奇异金属态之间的交叉行为，以及弛豫各向异性与最大临界温度的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体中核弛豫作为温度依赖电子激发的探针，其行为在临界温度Tc附近尚未被充分理解。作者旨在通过分析已有的平面Cu和O弛豫数据，建立一个新的唯象框架来更好地理解铜氧化物的相图。

Method: 基于文献中已有的平面Cu和O核弛豫数据，开发了一个简单的唯象模型。该模型的核心是识别出一个普遍金属态，其特征是1/(63T1⊥)Tc ≈ 25/Ks，存在于所有材料中。分析了该普遍金属态在Tc以上向奇异金属态的交叉行为，以及弛豫各向异性与掺杂水平的关系。

Result: 发现所有材料中都存在一个普遍金属态，其Cu核弛豫率与Tc直接相关。在Tc以上，该普遍金属态随温度升高逐渐过渡到弛豫滞后的奇异金属态。平面O弛豫表现出预期的固定各向异性，而Cu弛豫的各向异性则随掺杂变化（从约3.6到1）。这种与掺杂相关的各向异性决定了该材料家族的最大临界温度，并可能与两种弛豫组分有关。

Conclusion: 提出的新唯象描述为理解铜氧化物相图提供了更好的基础，揭示了普遍金属态、弛豫各向异性与最大临界温度之间的内在联系，表明可能需要两种弛豫组分来解释观察到的行为。

Abstract: Nuclear relaxation is a powerful probe of temperature dependent electronic excitations in superconducting metals. Their emergence from a condensed state near the critical temperature, $T_\mathrm{c}$, is of particular interest. In cuprate superconductors, the behavior is still not understood. Here, based on planar Cu and O relaxation data available in the literature, a simple phenomenology is developed. At its core is a universal metal, characterized by $1/{^{63}T}_{1\perp} T_\mathrm{c} \approx 25$/Ks, found in all materials, i.e. $T_\mathrm{c}$ is directly related to the Cu nuclear relaxation rate. Above $T_\mathrm{c}$, as a function of temperature, this universal metal crosses over into a strange metal regime where relaxation increasingly lags behind that of the universal metal. The latter is also tied to the metallic planar O relaxation, which only deviates from it at lower energies in underdoped materials with a doping dependent, but temperature independent pseudogap described earlier. Planar O relaxation has the expected fixed anisotropy, but for Cu it is doping dependent and varies between about 3.6 for lower and 1 for high doping levels. It is this doping-related anisotropy that sets the maximum critical temperature of the family and in that sense is tied to the universal metal. This likely requires two components of relaxation. The new phenomenology will be discussed and should give a better foundation for the understanding of the phase diagram of the cuprates.

</details>


### [185] [Electrodynamics of Quantum-Critical Conductors and Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10621)
*Uwe S. Pracht*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过光学低温实验研究无序NbN、颗粒Al薄膜和重费米子化合物CeCoIn5，为量子临界超导性提供了统一图像


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子临界超导性的统一物理图像，通过实验和理论结合理解不同材料体系中的量子临界超导行为

Method: 采用光学低温实验技术，研究三种不同材料体系：无序NbN薄膜、颗粒Al薄膜和重费米子化合物CeCoIn5；结合理论模型解释实验结果，通过详细计算和图示建立实验与前沿理论之间的联系

Result: 提供了量子临界超导性的统一物理图像，通过实验数据和理论分析揭示了不同材料体系中量子临界超导行为的共性和特性

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了实验观测与前沿理论之间的桥梁，为理解量子临界超导性提供了统一框架，对超导物理和量子临界现象研究具有重要意义

Abstract: This thesis presents and discusses optical low-temperature experiments on disordered NbN, granular Al thin-films, and the heavy-fermion compound CeCoIn5, offering a unified picture of quantum-critical superconductivity. It provides a concise introduction to the respective theoretical models employed to interpret the experimental results, and guides readers through in-depth calculations supplemented with supportive figures in order to both retrace the interpretations and span the bridge between experiment and state-of-the art theory.

</details>


### [186] [Microscopic mechanism for resonant light-enhanced pair correlations in K$_3$C$_{60}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10987)
*Juan I. Aranzadi,Joseph Tindall,Paul Fadler,Michael A. Sentef*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: K₃C₆₀中光诱导超导响应的共振增强机制：通过对称性约束的双光子路径，实现配对关联的显著增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现K₃C₆₀在泵浦频率接近10 THz时，光诱导超导响应出现巨大增强（比非共振激发高约两个数量级），需要从电子机制上解释这一共振增强现象

Method: 1. 基于第一性原理参数构建K₃C₆₀的驱动电子模型；2. 小团簇上的精确对角化识别对称性约束的双光子路径；3. 开发DMRG+Krylov方法处理更大团簇；4. 建立简化的单轨道模型扩展到14位点fcc团簇

Result: 1. 识别出对称性约束的双光子路径：第一光子驱动系统从偶宇称基态到中间奇宇称态，第二光子驱动到具有增强配对关联的偶宇称激发态；2. 共振能量随系统尺寸向下移动（离域双激子的动能增益）；3. 在14位点fcc团簇中，共振峰被推至~30 THz

Conclusion: 建立了K₃C₆₀中光增强配对关联的纯电子机制，支持实验观察到的10 THz共振确实源于超导相干配对形成而非金属性改善，表明类似共振路径可能出现在其他U和W尺度相当的中间耦合Hubbard材料中

Abstract: Recent experiments on K$_3$C$_{60}$ revealed a giant enhancement of the light-induced superconducting-like optical response for pump frequencies near 10 THz, with an efficiency roughly two orders of magnitude larger than for off-resonant excitation. Here we show that a resonant enhancement of pair correlations arises naturally in a driven electronic model of K$_3$C$_{60}$ derived from \emph{ab initio} parameters. Exact diagonalization on small clusters identifies a symmetry-constrained two-photon pathway: the first photon drives the system from the even-parity ground state to an intermediate odd-parity manifold, and the second photon drives it to an even-parity excited state with enhanced pair correlations. Guided by this structure, we develop a DMRG+Krylov approach for larger clusters and find that the resonance energy shifts downwards with system size due to the kinetic-energy gain of the delocalized doublon excitation. A simplified single-orbital model reproduces the same scaling trend and allows us to reach a 14-site fcc cluster, where the resonant peak is pushed to $\sim$ 30 THz. Our results establish a purely electronic mechanism for resonant light-enhanced pair correlations in K$_3$C$_{60}$ and independently support the view that the experimentally observed 10 THz resonance is indeed due to superconducting-like coherent pair formation rather than improved metallicity. More broadly, they suggest that related resonant pathways may arise in other intermediate-coupling Hubbard materials with on-site repulsion $U$ and electronic bandwidth $W$ on comparable scales.

</details>


### [187] [Type-II superconductivity in the Dirac semimetal PdTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11047)
*Ritu Gupta,Catherine Witteveen,Debarchan Das,Fabian O. von Rohr,Rustem Khasanov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: PdTe2狄拉克半金属的微观超导特性研究：通过μSR、交流磁化率和电阻测量发现，镶嵌晶体中存在两个超导转变温度（1.8K和1.6K），且为II型而非先前报道的I型超导体，具有通量线晶格特征，超导态为s波对称能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究PdTe2狄拉克半金属的超导特性，特别是澄清其超导类型（I型或II型），并探索非平凡拓扑、表面超导性和体超导性在范德瓦尔斯材料中的相互作用。

Method: 使用镶嵌晶体样品，结合零场和横向场μ子自旋弛豫/旋转（μSR）测量、交流磁化率测量和电阻测量，分析超导转变温度、磁穿透深度λ(T)和通量线晶格特征。

Result: 发现两个超导转变温度（1.8K和1.6K），确认PdTe2镶嵌晶体为II型超导体，观察到通量线晶格导致的抗磁位移和高斯展宽，超导能隙为s波对称，完全能隙态，无序可将超导性从I型转变为II型。

Conclusion: PdTe2是研究范德瓦尔斯材料中非平凡拓扑、表面超导性和II型体超导性相互作用的理想模型系统，系统无序可轻易将超导类型从I型转变为II型。

Abstract: We report on the microscopic superconducting properties of the Dirac semimetal PdTe2. In this study, we have focused on mosaic crystals of PdTe2, and used detailed zero field and transverse field muon spin relaxation/rotation ($μ$SR), ac-magnetic susceptibility, and resistivity measurements to investigate their superconducting properties. The magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal two superconducting transition temperatures at 1.8 and 1.6~K, respectively, in agreement with earlier reports. In contrary to these reports, we find that these mosaic PdTe2 crystals, are not type-I, but rather type-II superconductors. In fact, we observe the clear manifestation of a flux line lattice through a clear diamagnetic shift and Gaussian broadening of the Fourier spectra in the superconducting state. This behavior is likely caused by the disorder in the mosaic crystals of PdTe2 studied here. Our analysis of the superconducting order parameter by the means of temperature dependent magnetic penetration depth $λ(T)$ reveals a fully gapped superconducting state that can be well-fitted using an s-wave symmetric gap. We find that PdTe2 is a promising model system for the investigation and interplay of non-trivial topology, surface superconductivity, and type-II bulk superconductivity in a van-der-Waals material. Moreover, our results indicate that the superconductivity in this material can be easily modified from type-I to type-II by disorder in the system.

</details>


### [188] [Statistical Signatures of Majorana Zero Modes in Disordered Topological Superconductor Antidot Vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11692)
*Zhibo Ren,Jukka I. Väyrynen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三维拓扑绝缘体-超导体平台中反点钉扎涡旋内的Majorana零模与CdGM态在无序影响下的统计分布差异，发现Majorana零模概率密度方差是CdGM态的两倍，这一差异可用于通过扫描隧道显微镜识别Majorana零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维拓扑绝缘体-超导体平台的反点钉扎涡旋中，Majorana零模与大量CdGM态共存，传统零偏压电导峰方法难以区分两者。需要发展理论来研究无序对系统的影响，特别是区分Majorana零模与CdGM态的统计特性差异。

Method: 采用解析的随机矩阵理论方法和数值模拟相结合，推导Majorana零模和CdGM态的统计分布。通过分析无序对系统的影响，比较两种态的概率密度方差特性。

Result: Majorana零模的概率密度方差是CdGM态的两倍，这一差异源于Majorana零模具有实波函数而CdGM态具有复波函数。这种统计特性差异可以通过扫描隧道显微镜在无序反点涡旋中测量。

Conclusion: Majorana零模与CdGM态在无序影响下表现出不同的统计分布特性，特别是概率密度方差的差异为识别Majorana零模提供了超越零偏压电导峰的新特征，可通过扫描隧道显微镜实验验证。

Abstract: An antidot-pinned vortex in a three-dimensional topological insulator-superconductor platform hosts a Majorana zero mode (MZM). However, numerous Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon (CdGM) states coexist with it. We develop a general theory to study the effects of disorder on the system, emphasizing the difference between Majorana zero mode and CdGM states. Using both an analytical random matrix theory approach and numerical simulations, we derive the statistical distributions of these states. Our results demonstrate that the variance of the MZM probability density is twice that of the CdGM states, a difference due to the former having a real wave function as opposed to a complex one. This distinction can be measured by using scanning tunneling microscopy in a disordered antidot vortex, providing a signature of MZM beyond the zero-bias conductance peak.

</details>


### [189] [Reduced pair breaking from extended disorder in unconventional superconductors: implications to 4Hb-TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11738)
*Yuval Tsur,Mark H. Fischer,Jonathan Ruhman*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文研究了过渡金属二硫化物中非传统超导性在强无序条件下依然存在的原因，发现扩展杂质势能显著降低对破坏率，使非传统配对态比标准AG理论预测的更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AG理论认为非传统超导性会被非磁性无序强烈抑制，但实验观察到某些材料（如过渡金属二硫化物）在高电阻率下仍表现出非传统配对特征，这需要解释无序条件下动量弛豫与对破坏之间的反常关系。

Method: 采用多带模型包含自旋轨道耦合，引入模拟常见晶格缺陷（如硫空位或位点吸附原子）的扩展杂质势，计算无序修饰的配对磁化率，并同时评估动量弛豫率和对破坏率。

Result: 扩展杂质势导致对破坏率相对于输运散射率参数化减小，Γτ_D ∼ 1/3，这种减小源于杂质势的动量结构与超导能隙内部结构的匹配，抑制了对破坏过程。

Conclusion: 扩展杂质势能显著增强非传统配对态在无序条件下的稳定性，为高电阻率材料中非传统超导性的存在提供了自然解释，缓解了高电阻率与非传统配对之间的明显矛盾。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity is generally expected to be strongly suppressed by nonmagnetic disorder, as captured by Abrikosov--Gor'kov (AG) theory. However, several materials, including transition metal dichalcogenides, exhibit signatures of unconventional pairing despite relatively high resistivities, suggesting a breakdown of the conventional relation between momentum relaxation and pair breaking. Here, we study this problem in H-phase transition metal dichalcogenides by computing the disorder-dressed pairing susceptibility. We employ a multiband model with spin-orbit coupling and include an impurity potential that mimics a common lattice defect, namely a chalcogen vacancy or site ad-atom. This yields to an extended impurity potential, which we compare with the commonly considered on-site (point defect) potential. We evaluate the momentum-relaxation rate and the pair-breaking rate on equal footing. We find that extended impurity potentials lead to a parametrically reduced pair-breaking rate compared to the transport scattering rate, with $Γτ_D \sim 1/3$ over a wide parameter range. This reduction originates from the momentum structure of the disorder potential, which partially matches the internal structure of the superconducting gap and suppresses pair-breaking processes. As a result, unconventional pairing states are significantly more robust than predicted by standard AG theory. Our results provide a natural explanation for the persistence of unconventional superconductivity in systems with strong disorder and substantially alleviate the apparent conflict between high resistivity and unconventional pairing in materials such as 4Hb-TaS$_2$.

</details>
